### [1] [Practice Less, Explain More: LLM-Supported Self-Explanation Improves Explanation Quality on Transfer Problems in Calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00142)
*Eason Chen,Xinyi Tang,Yvonne Zhao,Meiyi Chen,Meryam Elmir,Elizabeth McLaughlin,Mingyu Yuan,Yumo Wang,Shyam Agarwal,Jared Cochrane,Jionghao Lin,Tongshuang Wu,Ken Koedinger*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: LLM支持的开放式自我解释能提高NEI迁移问题解释质量，但练习量减少，整体迁移效果证据较弱


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究旨在探索不同自我解释方式（无解释、菜单式、开放式+LLM反馈）在微积分学习环境中的效果，特别是LLM支持的开放式自我解释是否能改善学习效果和迁移能力

Method: 采用被试间实验设计（N=92），比较三种条件：无自我解释（控制组）、菜单式自我解释、开放式自我解释+LLM生成反馈。在60分钟固定练习时间内，测量后测表现和迁移问题表现，特别关注"信息不足"（NEI）问题

Result: 所有条件均显示积极学习收益，组间后测表现无显著差异。在NEI迁移问题上，开放式条件解释质量显著优于控制组（β=+11.9%，p=.030），但NEI选择题准确率优势不显著（p=.183）。所有后测开放式解释中，开放式条件有边际显著优势（β=+7.3%，p=.057）。开放式条件练习问题数量显著减少

Conclusion: LLM支持的开放式自我解释能改善NEI迁移问题的解释质量，但整体迁移效果证据较弱。值得注意的是，这种效果是在学习者完成更少练习的情况下实现的，表明LLM反馈可能提高了练习质量而非数量

Abstract: We conducted a between-subjects experiment (N=92) comparing three conditions in a calculus learning environment: no self-explanation (control), menu-based self-explanation, and open-ended self-explanation with LLM-generated feedback. All conditions showed positive learning gains within a fixed 60-minute practice session, with no significant between-condition differences in post-test performance. On transfer questions, the open-ended condition produced significantly higher-quality explanations than control on "Not Enough Information" (NEI) problems ($β$=+11.9 percentage points, $p$=.030), though the corresponding NEI multiple-choice accuracy advantage was not significant ($p$=.183). Moreover, across all post-test open-ended explanations, the open-ended condition showed a marginally significant advantage ($β$=+7.3%, $p$=.057). These findings suggest that LLM-supported open-ended self-explanation can improve explanation quality on NEI transfer problems, with weaker evidence across broader transfer explanation measures. Notably, these effects emerged even though learners in the open-ended condition completed substantially fewer practice problems within the same practice time.

</details>


### [2] [Not Just Duolingo: Supporting Immigrant Language Preservation Through Family-Based Play](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00282)
*Alejandro Ciuba,Zheng YY Li,Aakash Gautam*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究尼泊尔移民在美国面临的语言传承障碍，并开发了一款基于可理解输入理论的音频优先点击式语言学习游戏，支持亲子共同游戏以促进语言传承。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移民群体在保持母语和身份认同方面面临挑战，特别是尼泊尔移民在美国面临语言传承障碍，需要了解这些障碍并设计技术支持语言传承。

Method: 1) 访谈8名尼泊尔移民了解语言传承障碍；2) 基于可理解输入理论开发音频优先、点击式语言学习游戏，支持亲子共同游戏；3) 与4名设计专家进行早期评估。

Result: 参与者表现出强烈动机但面临制度支持不足、时间资源限制、英语主导环境导致亲子语言差距扩大等问题。设计专家评估显示游戏玩法有前景，但需要简化符号密集的UI。

Conclusion: 设计语言技术应支持通过关系实现语言传承，同时承认设计的局限性。亲子共同游戏技术有助于弥合语言差距，但需考虑简化界面设计。

Abstract: For immigrants, language preservation is crucial to maintain their identity, but the process of immigration can put a strain on a community's ability to do so. We interviewed eight Nepali immigrants to understand barriers to language preservation across sociopolitical contexts in Nepal and immigrant life in the United States. Participants described strong motivation but limited institutional support, time and resource constraints, and English-dominant environments that widen parent-child language gaps. They envisioned technology that supports interactive, family centered learning. In response, we are developing an audio-first, point-and-click language learning game based on the theory of comprehensible input, designed for parent-child co-playing. An early evaluation with four design experts reveals promising gameplay, and the need to simplify symbol-heavy UI. We conclude with implications for designing language technologies that support preservation through relations while acknowledging the limits of design.

</details>


### [3] [NeuroVase: A Tangible Mobile Augmented Reality Learning System for Neurovascular Anatomy and Stroke Education](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00296)
*Bahar Jahani,Matsanga Leyila Kaseka,Marta Kersten-Oertel,Yiming Xiao*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: NeuroVase：基于平板电脑的移动增强现实平台，通过交互式3D解剖模型和结构化教学框架，改善卒中相关神经血管解剖学习


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卒中作为全球主要死亡和致残原因，需要快速、明智的临床决策。对脑血管解剖和血管区域与卒中症状和严重程度关系的空间理解至关重要。然而，传统教学通常使用静态2D图表和印刷材料，难以掌握复杂的神经血管系统及其临床意义。移动增强现实为提供直观的3D解剖教育提供了可访问的媒介，但针对神经血管系统和卒中的应用仍然有限。

Method: 提出NeuroVase，一个基于平板电脑的移动增强现实平台，采用结构化教学框架。系统采用双模式设置：使用有形提示卡作为独立学习辅助工具，同时作为AR内容交付的交互式标记。定制学习课程专注于脑血管解剖和卒中，支持探索血管区域、卒中综合征和动脉闭塞，结合NeuroVase中注释的3D解剖模型。通过40名参与者的对照用户研究评估效果。

Result: 对照用户研究显示，与传统学习方法相比，NeuroVase是一个有效且用户友好的增强现实平台，能够促进复杂的解剖学和生理学教育。

Conclusion: NeuroVase提供了一个交互式、引人入胜且易于访问的替代传统方法的学习平台，通过移动增强现实技术改善了卒中相关神经解剖学的学习效果，有助于临床决策和患者护理。

Abstract: Stroke remains a leading cause of mortality and disability worldwide, requiring rapid and informed clinical decision-making. A solid spatial understanding of cerebrovascular anatomy and vascular territories in relation to stroke symptoms and severity is critical for timely clinical decision and patient care. However, this knowledge is typically conveyed through static 2D diagrams and printed materials, which can hinder mastery of the complex neurovascular system and their clinical implications. Mobile augmented reality (AR) offers an accessible medium for delivering intuitive 3D anatomical education, yet applications focused on the neurovascular system and stroke remain limited despite the demand. To address this, we propose NeuroVase, a tablet-based mobile AR platform within a structured pedagogical framework that enhances stroke-related neuroanatomy learning by providing an interactive, engaging, and accessible alternative to traditional methods. NeuroVase features a dual-mode setup, using tangible cue cards as standalone study aids while also serving as interactive markers for AR content delivery. A custom learning curriculum focused on cerebrovascular anatomy and stroke supports exploration of vascular territories, stroke syndromes, and arterial occlusions, in the context of annotated 3D anatomical models in NeuroVase. A controlled user study with 40 participants revealed that NeuroVase is an effective and user-friendly AR platform to facilitate complex anatomical and physiological education, compared with traditional learning.

</details>


### [4] [Physically-intuitive Privacy and Security: A Design Paradigm for Building User Trust in Smart Sensing Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00312)
*Youngwook Do,Yuxi Wu,Gregory D. Abowd,Sauvik Das*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出PIPS设计范式，通过物理直观的隐私安全控制提升用户对传感器交互系统的信任


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传感器交互系统（如智能音箱、摄像头、RFID标签）虽然将计算功能嵌入物理环境，但也带来真实和感知的隐私风险。用户知道设备制造商、应用开发者和恶意第三方希望收集并货币化其个人数据，这导致用户即使存在隐私安全控制措施，仍对这些系统缺乏信任。

Method: 提出物理直观隐私安全（PIPS）设计范式，包含三个原则：1）直接物理操作传感器状态；2）传感器状态的可感知保证；3）意图对齐的传感器（去）激活。通过三个案例研究说明这些原则：智能摄像头盖、隐私供电和按需RFID。

Result: 每个案例研究都显示相对于现有传感器系统，PIPS设计能够改善用户信任。

Conclusion: PIPS设计范式通过提供简单、基于物理的概念模型，能够有效提升用户对传感器交互系统的信任。

Abstract: Sensor-based interactive systems -- e.g., "smart" speakers, webcams, and RFID tags -- allow us to embed computational functionality into physical environments. They also expose users to real and perceived privacy risks: users know that device manufacturers, app developers, and malicious third parties want to collect and monetize their personal data, which fuels their mistrust of these systems even in the presence of privacy and security controls. We propose a new design paradigm, physically-intuitive privacy and security (PIPS), which aims to improve user trust by designing privacy and security controls that provide users with simple, physics-based conceptual models of their operation. PIPS consists of three principles: (1) direct physical manipulation of sensor state; (2) perceptible assurance of sensor state; and, (3) intent-aligned sensor (de)activation. We illustrate these principles through three case studies -- Smart Webcam Cover, Powering for Privacy, and On-demand RFID -- each of which has been shown to improve trust relative to existing sensor-based systems.

</details>


### [5] [Not My Truce: Personality Differences in AI-Mediated Workplace Negotiation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00464)
*Veda Duddu,Jash Rajesh Parekh,Andy Mao,Hanyi Min,Ziang Xiao,Vedant Das Swain,Koustuv Saha*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究挑战了AI对话辅导在职场谈判中普遍有效的假设，通过实验发现人格特质显著调节辅导效果，不同人格类型对AI辅导的响应存在差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假定AI驱动的对话辅导在职场谈判中对所有用户都同样有效，但本研究挑战这一假设，探讨个体差异（特别是人格特质）如何调节辅导效果，以更精确地理解AI干预的有效性边界。

Method: 采用被试间实验设计（N=267），比较三种干预方式：理论驱动AI（Trucey）、通用AI（Control-AI）和传统谈判手册（Control-NoAI）。基于大五人格特质和ARC类型学将参与者聚类为三种人格剖面：坚韧型、过度控制型和欠控制型。

Result: 坚韧型工作者主要通过手册获得广泛心理收益；过度控制型工作者在理论驱动AI下表现出结果特异性改善；欠控制型工作者尽管接触了框架，但效果最小。人格可作为超越阶段式定制的准备度预测指标。

Conclusion: 人格特质是AI辅导效果的重要调节变量，脆弱用户需要针对性而非全面性干预。研究推进了对人格决定干预前提的理解，强调自适应AI辅导系统应根据个体准备度而非假定普遍有效性来调整支持强度。

Abstract: AI-driven conversational coaching is increasingly used to support workplace negotiation, yet prior work assumes uniform effectiveness across users. We challenge this assumption by examining how individual differences, particularly personality traits, moderate coaching outcomes. We conducted a between-subjects experiment (N=267) comparing theory-driven AI (Trucey), general-purpose AI (Control-AI), and a traditional negotiation handbook (Control-NoAI). Participants were clustered into three profiles -- resilient, overcontrolled, and undercontrolled -- based on the Big-Five personality traits and ARC typology. Resilient workers achieved broad psychological gains primarily from the handbook, overcontrolled workers showed outcome-specific improvements with theory-driven AI, and undercontrolled workers exhibited minimal effects despite engaging with the frameworks. These patterns suggest personality as a predictor of readiness beyond stage-based tailoring: vulnerable users benefit from targeted rather than comprehensive interventions. The study advances understanding of personality-determined intervention prerequisites and highlights design implications for adaptive AI coaching systems that align support intensity with individual readiness, rather than assuming universal effectiveness.

</details>


### [6] [Trust and Reliance on AI in Education: AI Literacy and Need for Cognition as Moderators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01114)
*Griffin Pitts,Neha Rani,Weedguet Mildort*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究发现学生信任AI与适当依赖之间存在非线性关系：高信任度反而导致较低适当依赖，表明学生难以区分AI建议的正确与错误，且AI素养和认知需求调节此关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着生成式AI系统融入教育环境，学生在学习任务中经常接触AI生成内容。信任AI会影响学生如何解释和使用这些内容，包括是否批判性评估或过度依赖。研究旨在探讨学生信任如何影响编程问题解决中对AI助手的适当依赖，以及学习者特征是否调节这种关系。

Method: 432名本科生参与实验，完成Python输出预测问题，同时接收AI聊天机器人的建议和解释（包括准确和故意误导的建议）。将依赖行为操作化为学生响应反映适当使用AI建议的程度：正确时接受，错误时拒绝。通过前后测调查评估对助手的信任、AI素养、认知需求、编程自我效能感和编程素养。

Result: 结果显示信任与适当依赖之间存在非线性关系：更高的信任与更低的适当依赖相关，表明学生更难以区分正确和错误的建议。这种关系显著受到学生AI素养和认知需求的调节。

Conclusion: 研究强调需要在教学和系统支持方面开展未来工作，以鼓励在问题解决过程中更反思性地评估AI辅助。高信任度可能导致对AI建议的批判性评估不足，而AI素养和认知需求可以帮助缓解这一问题。

Abstract: As generative AI systems are integrated into educational settings, students often encounter AI-generated output while working through learning tasks, either by requesting help or through integrated tools. Trust in AI can influence how students interpret and use that output, including whether they evaluate it critically or exhibit overreliance. We investigate how students' trust relates to their appropriate reliance on an AI assistant during programming problem-solving tasks, and whether this relationship differs by learner characteristics. With 432 undergraduate participants, students' completed Python output-prediction problems while receiving recommendations and explanations from an AI chatbot, including accurate and intentionally misleading suggestions. We operationalize reliance behaviorally as the extent to which students' responses reflected appropriate use of the AI assistant's suggestions, accepting them when they were correct and rejecting them when they were incorrect. Pre- and post-task surveys assessed trust in the assistant, AI literacy, need for cognition, programming self-efficacy, and programming literacy. Results showed a non-linear relationship in which higher trust was associated with lower appropriate reliance, suggesting weaker discrimination between correct and incorrect recommendations. This relationship was significantly moderated by students' AI literacy and need for cognition. These findings highlight the need for future work on instructional and system supports that encourage more reflective evaluation of AI assistance during problem-solving.

</details>


### [7] [In the Middle, Not on Top: AI-Mediated Communication for Patient-Provider Care Relationships](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00643)
*Ut Gong,Yibo Meng,Qihan Zhang,Xin Chen,Yan Guan*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 论文提出AI在临床环境中应作为"中间而非顶端"的沟通调解者，通过CLEAR异步消息系统研究，展示这种配置如何支持关系为中心的护理价值观


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI进入临床环境，需要探讨如何定位AI以支持关系为中心的护理价值观（信任和有意义的连接），而不仅仅是关注AI能做什么

Method: 研究CLEAR异步消息系统，采用"中间而非顶端"的方法，让AI调解沟通而不取代人类判断，分析调解功能如何应对时间压力和健康素养不均等现实约束

Result: 调解功能（如可用性、中立性）重新分配了解释性工作并减少了关系摩擦，AI调解作为关系基础设施，揭示了框架权力和隐私方面的关键设计张力

Conclusion: AI调解应作为关系基础设施来构建，需要平衡调解功能与设计张力，特别是框架权力和隐私问题，以支持关系为中心的护理价值观

Abstract: Relationship-centered care relies on trust and meaningful connection. As AI enters clinical settings, we must ask not just what it can do, but how it should be positioned to support these values. We examine a "middle, not top" approach where AI mediates communication without usurping human judgment. Through studies of CLEAR, an asynchronous messaging system, we show how this configuration addresses real-world constraints like time pressure and uneven health literacy. We find that mediator affordances (e.g., availability, neutrality) redistribute interpretive work and reduce relational friction. Ultimately, we frame AI mediation as relational infrastructure, highlighting critical design tensions around framing power and privacy.

</details>


### [8] [Steering through Time: Blending Longitudinal Data with Simulation to Rethink Human-Autonomous Vehicle Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00832)
*Yasaman Hakiminejad,Shiva Azimi,Luis Gomero,Elizabeth Pantesco,Irene P. Kan,Meltem Izzetoglu,Arash Tavakoli*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种结合纵向移动传感与高保真驾驶模拟的混合框架，用于评估半自动驾驶中驾驶员接管准备状态，通过多模态数据收集验证了框架可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着半自动驾驶车辆普及，确保控制权交接时有效的人车交互成为关键安全挑战。现有研究多依赖单次模拟器实验或自然驾驶数据集，缺乏驾驶员在接管事件前的认知和生理状态的时序背景信息。

Method: 采用混合框架：收集38名参与者7天的可穿戴生理数据和每日压力、唤醒度、效价及睡眠质量调查，随后进行实验室模拟，在多种次级任务条件下设置脚本化接管事件，使用眼动追踪、fNIRS和生理测量等多模态传感技术捕获实时响应。

Result: 初步分析显示框架可行性及个体在基线和任务测量中的变异性：注视持续时间和接管控制时间随任务类型不同而差异，RMSSD表现出较高的个体间稳定性。

Conclusion: 该概念验证支持通过将时序分层数据与实时性能关联，开发个性化、情境感知的驾驶员监控系统。

Abstract: As semi-automated vehicles (SAVs) become more common, ensuring effective human-vehicle interaction during control handovers remains a critical safety challenge. Existing studies often rely on single-session simulator experiments or naturalistic driving datasets, which often lack temporal context on drivers' cognitive and physiological states before takeover events. This study introduces a hybrid framework combining longitudinal mobile sensing with high-fidelity driving simulation to examine driver readiness in semi-automated contexts. In a pilot study with 38 participants, we collected 7 days of wearable physiological data and daily surveys on stress, arousal, valence, and sleep quality, followed by an in-lab simulation with scripted takeover events under varying secondary task conditions. Multimodal sensing, including eye tracking, fNIRS, and physiological measures, captured real-time responses. Preliminary analysis shows the framework's feasibility and individual variability in baseline and in-task measures; for example, fixation duration and takeover control time differed by task type, and RMSSD showed high inter-individual stability. This proof-of-concept supports the development of personalized, context-aware driver monitoring by linking temporally layered data with real-time performance.

</details>


### [9] [Evaluating the Feasibility of Augmented Reality to Support Communication Access for Deaf Students in Experiential Higher Education Contexts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00856)
*Roshan Mathew,Roshan L. Peiris*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AR智能眼镜系统ARRAE用于改善听障学生在实验室等体验式学习环境中的沟通障碍，通过将手语翻译或字幕叠加到视野中，减少注意力分散和认知负荷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 听障学生在高等教育中面临沟通障碍，尤其在实验室等体验式学习环境中更为严重。传统的无障碍服务（如手语翻译和字幕）要求学生将注意力分散在关键任务、安全隐患、教学材料和访问提供者之间，造成安全与公平沟通之间的权衡，增加了认知负荷并干扰任务参与。

Method: 本研究开发了基于AR智能眼镜的ARRAE生态系统，通过将手语翻译或字幕直接叠加到学生视野中，实现无障碍服务与动手学习的整合。研究采用实证方法，对12名听障参与者进行评估，分析AR介导的访问如何影响视觉注意模式和在动手任务中的感知认知负荷。

Result: 研究发现AR介导的沟通在改善注意力管理和体验式学习环境的沟通无障碍方面显示出强大潜力。参与者强调无障碍偏好高度依赖于具体情境。研究还识别了多个设计和人机工程学挑战，包括显示位置、视觉疲劳以及与助听设备的兼容性。

Conclusion: AR技术在支持视觉要求高的环境中的无障碍参与方面具有前景，但需要解决关键的设计考虑因素。研究强调了AR系统在改善听障学生体验式学习环境中的沟通障碍方面的潜力，同时指出了未来系统设计需要关注的具体挑战。

Abstract: Deaf and hard of hearing (DHH) students often experience communication barriers in higher education, which are particularly acute in experiential learning environments such as laboratories. Traditional accessibility services, such as interpreting and captioning, often require DHH students to divide their attention between critical tasks, potential safety hazards, instructional materials, and access providers, creating trade-offs between safety and equitable communication. These demands can disrupt task engagement and increase cognitive load in settings that require sustained visual focus, highlighting the limitations of current approaches. To address these challenges, this study investigates Augmented Reality Real-Time Access for Education (ARRAE), an ecosystem based on augmented reality (AR) smart glasses, as a potential intervention for laboratory-based environments. By overlaying interpreters or captions directly into a student's field of view, AR enables the integration of accessibility into hands-on learning without compromising safety or comprehension. Through an empirical study with 12 DHH participants, we evaluate how AR-mediated access influences visual attention patterns and perceived cognitive load during hands-on tasks. The findings suggest that AR-mediated communication shows strong potential to improve attention management and communication accessibility in experiential learning environments, though participants emphasized that accessibility preferences are highly context-dependent. Participants also identified several design and ergonomic challenges, including display positioning, visual fatigue, and compatibility with hearing devices. Together, these results highlight both the promise of AR for supporting accessible participation in visually demanding environments and key design considerations for future systems.

</details>


### [10] [AuraDesk: Data Physicalization through Olfaction Metaphors for Representing and Mitigating Workplace Stress](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00869)
*Siying Hu,Zhenhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: AuraDesk：一种嗅觉数据物化系统，通过气味表达工作场所压力相关的生理信号，支持瞬时意识、微休息和环境协调，而非作为明确警报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统工作场所压力干预通常使用视觉或听觉模态，但这些方式会竞争注意力并导致感官过载。本研究探索嗅觉作为替代的周边媒介，用于在办公环境中表示压力相关的生理信号。

Method: 开发AuraDesk嗅觉数据物化系统，将可穿戴设备获取的生理线索转化为工作站的定位气味表达。系统结合局部生理状态推断和受限驱动策略，产生适合日常工作环境的时间调节和空间定位的气味输出。通过25名知识工作者在实际工作站的单日现场部署研究，评估该方法的可行性和体验质量。

Result: 参与者通常将气味输出解释为微妙的环境线索而非明确警报，支持瞬时意识、微休息采取和感知环境协调。同时，参与者对共享办公室中的气味偏好、习惯化和情境适当性提出了重要关切。

Conclusion: 本研究贡献包括：(1)工作场所生理驱动周边反馈的嗅觉接口；(2)连续生物信号解释与受限气味驱动耦合的混合映射方法；(3)关于工作者在真实办公环境中如何感知、协商和适应周边嗅觉反馈的经验见解。AuraDesk定位为探索工作场所福祉和注意力敏感交互的嗅觉数据物化设计空间的探针。

Abstract: Workplace stress is often addressed through visual or auditory interventions, yet these modalities can compete with attention and contribute to sensory overload. We explore olfaction as an alternative ambient medium for representing stress-related physiological signals in office settings. We present AuraDesk, an olfactory data physicalization system that translates wearable-derived physiological cues into situated scent expressions at the workstation. The system combines local physiological state inference with a constrained actuation strategy to produce temporally regulated and spatially localized scent output suitable for everyday work environments. To examine the feasibility and experiential qualities of this approach, we conducted a one-day in-situ field deployment with 25 knowledge workers at their actual workstations. Our findings show that participants often interpreted the scent output not as an explicit alert, but as a subtle atmospheric cue that supported momentary awareness, micro-break taking, and perceived environmental attunement. At the same time, participants raised important concerns regarding scent preference, habituation, and contextual appropriateness in shared offices. This work contributes (1) an olfactory interface for physiologically driven ambient feedback in the workplace, (2) a hybrid mapping approach for coupling continuous biosignal interpretation with constrained scent actuation, and (3) empirical insights into how workers perceive, negotiate, and appropriate ambient olfactory feedback in real office contexts. Rather than claiming therapeutic efficacy, we position AuraDesk as a probe into the design space of olfactory data physicalization for workplace wellbeing and attention-sensitive interaction.

</details>


### [11] [FlexAI: A Multi-modal Solution for Delivering Personalized and Adaptive Fitness Interventions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00968)
*Shivangi Agarwal,Zoya Ghoshal,Bharat Jain,Siddharth Siddharth*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: FlexAI是一个多模态健身系统，结合计算机视觉、生理传感器和LLM推理，提供实时个性化锻炼指导，相比静态系统显著提升用户体验和效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有健身解决方案大多依赖静态计划，无法根据用户的实时生理状态（如疼痛阈值、疲劳水平、动作姿势）进行自适应调整，缺乏个性化实时反馈。

Method: 开发FlexAI多模态系统，集成计算机视觉（监测动作姿势）、生理传感器（心率和语音）以及LLM推理能力，提供动态干预，包括运动强度、休息时间和激励指导。

Result: 技术评估确认模型准确性和管道延迟；专家评审验证LLM干预的正确性；25名参与者的对照研究显示，相比静态非自适应系统，FlexAI显著提升用户享受感、成就感，降低无聊感和挫败感。

Conclusion: 通过整合多模态感知与LLM驱动推理，自适应系统如FlexAI能够创造更吸引人、更有效的锻炼体验，为构建可靠的自适应教练系统提供了蓝图。

Abstract: Personalization of exercise routines is a crucial factor in helping people achieve their fitness goals. Despite this, many contemporary solutions fail to offer real-time, adaptive feedback tailored to an individual's physiological states. Contemporary fitness solutions often rely only on static plans and do not adjust to factors such as a user's pain thresholds, fatigue levels, or form during a workout routine. This work introduces FlexAI, a multi-modal system that integrates computer vision, physiological sensors (heart rate and voice), and the reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) to deliver real-time, personalized workout guidance. FlexAI continuously monitors a user's physical form and level of exertion, among other parameters, to provide dynamic interventions focused on exercise intensity, rest periods, and motivation. To validate our system, we performed a technical evaluation confirming our models' accuracy and quantifying pipeline latency, alongside an expert review where certified trainers validated the correctness of the LLM's interventions. Furthermore, in a controlled study with 25 participants, FlexAI demonstrated significant improvements over a static, non-adaptive control system. With FlexAI, users reported significantly greater enjoyment, a stronger sense of achievement, and significantly lower levels of boredom and frustration. These results indicate that by integrating multi-modal sensing with LLM-driven reasoning, adaptive systems like FlexAI can create a more engaging and effective workout experience. Our work provides a blueprint for integrating multi-modal sensing with LLM-driven reasoning, demonstrating that it is possible to create adaptive coaching systems that are not only more engaging but also demonstrably reliable.

</details>


### [12] [True (VIS) Lies: Analyzing How Generative AI Recognizes Intentionality, Rhetoric, and Misleadingness in Visualization Lies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01181)
*Graziano Blasilli,Marco Angelini*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 研究评估了16种多模态大语言模型在识别和解释误导性可视化方面的能力，使用COVID-19推文数据集和VisLies真实案例，并与可视化专家的人类判断进行比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着多模态LLMs在视觉内容理解方面的应用日益广泛，需要评估这些模型在识别误导性可视化方面的能力。当前缺乏对LLMs在理解可视化修辞技巧、作者意图和误导性方面的系统评估，特别是与人类专家判断的比较研究。

Method: 1. 使用包含2,336条COVID-19相关推文的数据集（一半包含误导性可视化）；2. 补充VisLies社区的真实误导性可视化案例；3. 评估16种最先进的LLMs（15个开源模型和1个专有模型），涵盖不同规模、架构和推理能力；4. 进行可视化专家用户研究，建立人类判断基准；5. 基于可视化修辞理论和作者意图分类法设计评估框架。

Result: 研究评估了16种模型在识别误导性可视化、解释其观察结果、分析根本原因和潜在意图方面的表现。通过比较模型输出与专家判断，揭示了LLMs与人类在理解可视化修辞和作者意图方面的相似性和差异性。

Conclusion: 多模态LLMs在识别和解释误导性可视化方面展现出一定能力，但与人类专家判断存在差异。研究为理解LLMs在视觉修辞分析方面的优势和局限性提供了重要见解，有助于指导未来模型改进和实际应用。

Abstract: This study investigates the ability of multimodal Large Language Models (LLMs) to identify and interpret misleading visualizations, and recognize these observations along with their underlying causes and potential intentionality. Our analysis leverages concepts from visualization rhetoric and a newly developed taxonomy of authorial intents as explanatory lenses. We formulated three research questions and addressed them experimentally using a dataset of 2,336 COVID-19-related tweets, half of which contain misleading visualizations, and supplemented it with real-world examples of perceptual, cognitive, and conceptual errors drawn from VisLies, the IEEE VIS community event dedicated to showcasing deceptive and misleading visualizations. To ensure broad coverage of the current LLM landscape, we evaluated 16 state-of-the-art models. Among them, 15 are open-weight models, spanning a wide range of model sizes, architectural families, and reasoning capabilities. The selection comprises small models, namely Nemotron-Nano-V2-VL (12B parameters), Mistral-Small-3.2 (24B), DeepSeek-VL2 (27B), Gemma3 (27B), and GTA1 (32B); medium-sized models, namely Qianfan-VL (70B), Molmo (72B), GLM-4.5V (108B), LLaVA-NeXT (110B), and Pixtral-Large (124B); and large models, namely Qwen3-VL (235B), InternVL3.5 (241B), Step3 (321B), Llama-4-Maverick (400B), and Kimi-K2.5 (1000B). In addition, we employed OpenAI GPT-5.4, a frontier proprietary model. To establish a human perspective on these tasks, we also conducted a user study with visualization experts to assess how people perceive rhetorical techniques and the authorial intentions behind the same misleading visualizations. This allows comparison between model and expert behavior, revealing similarities and differences that provide insights into where LLMs align with human judgment and where they diverge.

</details>


### [13] [Assessing Affective Objectives for Communicative Visualizations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01183)
*Elsie Lee-Robbins,Eytan Adar*

Main category: cs.HC

TL;DR: 提出基于学习目标定义可视化设计意图的框架，特别针对情感目标评估的挑战，建立评估标准并应用于结合个人叙事与可视化的复杂情感设计任务


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 使用学习目标定义可视化设计意图是强大的设计工具，但情感目标评估面临独特挑战：认知目标已有成熟评估方法，而情感目标（如态度改变）难以观察和测量

Method: 定义一套从教育、倡导、经济、健康、心理学等领域选择与情感目标对齐的评估标准，在结合个人叙事与可视化的复杂情感设计任务中应用该框架

Result: 所选评估方法能够在目标框架内评估不同设计方案，并检验相互竞争的心理理论，为情感目标的可视化设计提供系统评估工具

Conclusion: 提出的评估框架解决了情感目标可视化设计的评估难题，使设计师能够更系统地创建、评估和比较具有情感影响的可视化方案

Abstract: Using learning objectives to define designer intents for communicative visualizations can be a powerful design tool. Cognitive and affective objectives are concrete and specific, which can be translated to assessments when creating, evaluating, or comparing visualization ideas. However, while there are many well-validated assessments for cognitive objectives, affective objectives are uniquely challenging. It is easy to see if a visualization helps someone remember the number of patients in a clinic, but harder to observe the change in their attitudes around donations to a crisis. In this work, we define a set of criteria for selecting assessments--from education, advocacy, economics, health, and psychology--that align with affective objectives. We illustrate the use of the framework in a complex affective design task that combines personal narratives and visualizations. Our chosen assessments allow us to evaluate different designs in the context of our objectives and competing psychological theories.

</details>

### [14] [Generalized Heavy-tailed Mutation for Evolutionary Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00502)
*Anton V. Eremeev,Dmitri V. Silaev,Valentin A. Topchii*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 本文推广了重尾变异算子的幂律假设，使用正则变化约束，扩展了(1+(λ,λ))遗传算法在OneMax函数上的期望优化时间上界分析，提出了满足广义条件的新变异算子版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Doerr等人(2017)提出的重尾变异算子基于变异率的幂律分布假设。本文旨在推广这一假设，使用更一般的正则变化约束来扩展对(1+(λ,λ))遗传算法性能的理论分析。

Method: 1. 使用正则变化约束推广幂律分布假设；2. 在OneMax函数类上分析(1+(λ,λ))遗传算法的期望优化时间；3. 提出满足广义条件的新重尾变异算子版本；4. 进行计算实验验证。

Result: 1. 成功推广了Antipov等人(2022)的上界分析；2. 证明了在OneMax函数类上，确保O(n)期望优化时间的充分条件可以推广；3. 该优化时间渐近小于任何静态变异率下的(1+(λ,λ))遗传算法；4. 新变异算子版本在计算实验中表现出良好性能。

Conclusion: 正则变化约束为分析重尾变异算子提供了更一般的理论框架，成功扩展了(1+(λ,λ))遗传算法的性能分析，提出的新变异算子版本在理论和实验上都表现出优势。

Abstract: The heavy-tailed mutation operator, proposed by Doerr, Le, Makhmara, and Nguyen (2017) for evolutionary algorithms, is based on the power-law assumption of mutation rate distribution. Here we generalize the power-law assumption using a regularly varying constraint on the distribution function of mutation rate. In this setting, we generalize the upper bounds on the expected optimization time of the $(1+(λ,λ))$ genetic algorithm obtained by Antipov, Buzdalov and Doerr (2022) for the OneMax function class parametrized by the problem dimension $n$. In particular, it is shown that, on this function class, the sufficient conditions of Antipov, Buzdalov and Doerr (2022) on the heavy-tailed mutation, ensuring the $O(n)$ optimization time in expectation, may be generalized as well. This optimization time is known to be asymptotically smaller than what can be achieved by the $(1+(λ,λ))$ genetic algorithm with any static mutation rate.
  A new version of the heavy-tailed mutation operator is proposed, satisfying the generalized conditions, and promising results of computational experiments are presented.

</details>


### [15] [Finding Low Star Discrepancy 3D Kronecker Point Sets Using Algorithm Configuration Techniques](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00786)
*Imène Ait Abderrahim,Carola Doerr,Martin Durand*

Main category: cs.NE

TL;DR: 优化Kronecker构造的参数可显著降低三维点集的L∞星偏差，在500点以上超越现有最优值


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有经典构造（如Sobol'、Halton、Hammersley序列）在固定尺寸点集上表现不佳，需要研究通过尺寸特定优化能多大程度改进现有构造

Method: 针对Kronecker构造，优化其两个可配置参数，在三维设置下使用算法配置技术irace推导不同尺寸范围的参数

Result: 优化参数后的点集在至少500个点时超越现有最优星偏差值，为多个尺寸范围创造了新的最优偏差记录

Conclusion: 通过尺寸特定优化可显著改进Kronecker构造的星偏差性能，为实验设计、贝叶斯优化和拟蒙特卡洛积分等应用提供更优的点集

Abstract: The L infinity star discrepancy is a measure for how uniformly a point set is distributed in a given space. Point sets of low star discrepancy are used as designs of experiments, as initial designs for Bayesian optimization algorithms, for quasi-Monte Carlo integration methods, and many other applications. Recent work has shown that classical constructions such as Sobol', Halton, or Hammersley sequences can be outperformed by large margins when considering point sets of fixed sizes rather than their convergence behavior. These results, highly relevant to the aforementioned applications, raise the question of how much existing constructions can be improved through size-specific optimization. In this work, we study this question for the so-called Kronecker construction. Focusing on the 3-dimensional setting, we show that optimizing the two configurable parameters of its construction yields point sets outperforming the state-of-the-art value for sets of at least 500 points. Using the algorithm configuration technique irace, we then derive parameters that yield new state-of-the-art discrepancy values for whole ranges of set sizes.

</details>

### [16] [Secure Forgetting: A Framework for Privacy-Driven Unlearning in Large Language Model (LLM)-Based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00430)
*Dayong Ye,Tainqing Zhu,Congcong Zhu,Feng He,Qi He,Shang Wang,Bo Liu,Wanlei Zhou*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文首次提出LLM智能体遗忘学习概念，针对智能体可能积累的敏感或过时知识问题，建立了一个包含状态遗忘、轨迹遗忘和环境遗忘的三类遗忘框架，并开发了基于自然语言的遗忘方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LLM智能体在现实应用中的广泛集成，其积累的敏感或过时知识引发严重担忧。现有研究主要关注提升智能体任务性能，但缺乏有效的知识遗忘机制。为解决这一问题，需要开发能够让智能体选择性遗忘先前学习知识的机制，从而提出了LLM智能体遗忘学习这一新研究方向。

Method: 提出了一个全面的遗忘框架，将遗忘场景分为三类：状态遗忘（遗忘特定状态或项目）、轨迹遗忘（遗忘动作序列）和环境遗忘（遗忘整个环境或任务类别）。开发了基于自然语言的遗忘方法，训练转换模型将高级遗忘请求转化为可操作的遗忘提示，引导智能体进行受控遗忘过程。同时引入了遗忘推理对抗者来评估框架鲁棒性。

Result: 实验结果表明，该方法能有效使智能体遗忘目标知识，同时保持非目标任务的性能，并能防止对抗者推断出被遗忘的知识。

Conclusion: 本文开创了LLM智能体遗忘学习的研究领域，提出的框架和方法为解决智能体知识管理中的隐私和安全问题提供了有效途径，为未来智能体系统的安全部署奠定了基础。

Abstract: Large language model (LLM)-based agents have recently gained considerable attention due to the powerful reasoning capabilities of LLMs. Existing research predominantly focuses on enhancing the task performance of these agents in diverse scenarios. However, as LLM-based agents become increasingly integrated into real-world applications, significant concerns emerge regarding their accumulation of sensitive or outdated knowledge. Addressing these concerns requires the development of mechanisms that allow agents to selectively forget previously learned knowledge, giving rise to a new term LLM-based agent unlearning. This paper initiates research on unlearning in LLM-based agents. Specifically, we propose a novel and comprehensive framework that categorizes unlearning scenarios into three contexts: state unlearning (forgetting specific states or items), trajectory unlearning (forgetting sequences of actions) and environment unlearning (forgetting entire environments or categories of tasks). Within this framework, we introduce a natural language-based unlearning method that trains a conversion model to transform high-level unlearning requests into actionable unlearning prompts, guiding agents through a controlled forgetting process. Moreover, to evaluate the robustness of the proposed framework, we introduce an unlearning inference adversary capable of crafting prompts, querying agents, and observing their behaviors in an attempt to infer the forgotten knowledge. Experimental results show that our approach effectively enables agents to forget targeted knowledge while preserving performance on untargeted tasks, and prevents the adversary from inferring the forgotten knowledge.

</details>


### [17] [Internal State-Based Policy Gradient Methods for Partially Observable Markov Potential Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00433)
*Wonseok Yang,Thinh T. Doan*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 该论文研究了部分可观测马尔可夫势博弈中的多智能体强化学习，提出基于内部状态的自然策略梯度方法，建立了非渐进收敛界，并通过仿真验证了有限状态控制器相比仅使用当前观测的性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决部分可观测马尔可夫势博弈中的多智能体强化学习问题面临三大挑战：部分可观测性、分散信息和维度灾难。现有方法在处理这些挑战时存在局限性，需要开发既能应对部分可观测性又能保证计算可行性的算法。

Method: 采用共同信息框架处理部分可观测性和分散信息问题，引入内部状态压缩累积信息防止无限增长，实现基于内部状态的自然策略梯度方法来寻找马尔可夫势博弈的纳什均衡。

Result: 建立了该方法的非渐进收敛界，该界可分解为两个可解释部分：标准马尔可夫势博弈中出现的统计误差项，以及捕捉有限状态控制器使用的近似误差。在多个部分可观测环境中的仿真表明，使用有限状态控制器的方法相比仅使用当前观测的设置实现了持续的性能改进。

Conclusion: 提出的基于内部状态的自然策略梯度方法有效解决了部分可观测马尔可夫势博弈中的多智能体强化学习问题，理论收敛界提供了性能保证，仿真结果验证了有限状态控制器在部分可观测环境中的优越性。

Abstract: This letter studies multi-agent reinforcement learning in partially observable Markov potential games. Solving this problem is challenging due to partial observability, decentralized information, and the curse of dimensionality. First, to address the first two challenges, we leverage the common information framework, which allows agents to act based on both shared and local information. Second, to ensure tractability, we study an internal state that compresses accumulated information, preventing it from growing unboundedly over time. We then implement an internal state-based natural policy gradient method to find Nash equilibria of the Markov potential game. Our main contribution is to establish a non-asymptotic convergence bound for this method. Our theoretical bound decomposes into two interpretable components: a statistical error term that also arises in standard Markov potential games, and an approximation error capturing the use of finite-state controllers. Finally, simulations across multiple partially observable environments demonstrate that the proposed method using finite-state controllers achieves consistent improvements in performance compared to the setting where only the current observation is used.

</details>


### [18] [CASCADE: Cascaded Scoped Communication for Multi-Agent Re-planning in Disrupted Industrial Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00451)
*Mingjie Bi*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: CASCADE：一种预算化重规划机制，通过显式可审计的通信范围控制，在多智能体协调中实现质量-延迟-通信的权衡优化


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业中断重规划需要在严格延迟和通信预算下进行多智能体协调，现有协调方案要么假设通信免费（广播式升级），要么预先固定通信范围（手动调优邻域），这两种方案在中断影响超出局部区域时都很脆弱

Method: CASCADE采用预算化重规划机制，使通信范围显式化且可审计。每个智能体维护显式知识库，解决角色条件化的本地决策问题来修订承诺，通过轻量级契约原语协调，仅当本地验证表明当前范围不足时才扩展通信足迹

Result: 在中断的制造和供应链场景中评估，使用统一诊断测试机制设计主张——显式范围控制是否能在不确定性下产生有用的质量-延迟-通信权衡并提高鲁棒性

Conclusion: CASCADE通过分离统一智能体基板（知识库/决策管理器/通信管理器）与范围化交互层，实现了显式范围控制，提供了优于传统固定或隐式通信方案的鲁棒性和权衡能力

Abstract: Industrial disruption replanning demands multi-agent coordination under strict latency and communication budgets, where disruptions propagate through tightly coupled physical dependencies and rapidly invalidate baseline schedules and commitments. Existing coordination schemes often treat communication as either effectively free (broadcast-style escalation) or fixed in advance (hand-tuned neighborhoods), both of which are brittle once the disruption footprint extends beyond a local region. We present \CASCADE, a budgeted replanning mechanism that makes communication scope explicit and auditable rather than fixed or implicit. Each agent maintains an explicit knowledge base, solves role-conditioned local decision problems to revise commitments, and coordinates through lightweight contract primitives whose footprint expands only when local validation indicates that the current scope is insufficient. This design separates a unified agent substrate (Knowledge Base / Decision Manager / Communication Manager) from a scoped interaction layer that controls who is contacted, how far coordination propagates, and when escalation is triggered under explicit budgets. We evaluate \CASCADE on disrupted manufacturing and supply-chain settings using unified diagnostics intended to test a mechanism-design claim -- whether explicit scope control yields useful quality-latency-communication trade-offs and improved robustness under uncertainty -- rather than to provide a complete algorithmic ranking.

</details>


### [19] [Role Differentiation in a Coupled Resource Ecology under Multi-Level Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00810)
*Siddharth Chaturvedi,Ahmed El-Gazzar,Marcel van Gerven*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 多层级选择模型使群体通过角色分化避免公地悲剧，在耦合资源通道中实现持续更新下的稳定共存。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在持续个体层面选择下，非合作群体如何避免公地悲剧并自发形成角色分化，以利用不同资源通道。

Method: 引入多层级选择计算模型，群体选择塑造所有成员共享的共同基质和突变算子；在具身生态中设置正和摄入通道与零和再分配通道的耦合资源系统；通过出生死亡驱动的更新机制研究角色分化。

Result: 在习得生态中，群体层面两个通道均被占据，避免了单一获取模式的崩溃；零和通道使用随时间增加；通道占用在个体生命周期中波动；消融研究表明大部分基线性能由遗传行为基础承载，学习变异过程提供较小但系统的改进。

Conclusion: 多层级选择能使公共池环境中的群体通过耦合通道的分化使用，在持续更新下规避公地悲剧。

Abstract: A group of non-cooperating agents can succumb to the \emph{tragedy-of-the-commons} if all of them seek to maximize the same resource channel to improve their viability. In nature, however, groups often avoid such collapses by differentiating into distinct roles that exploit different resource channels. It remains unclear how such coordination can emerge under continual individual-level selection alone. To address this, we introduce a computational model of multi-level selection, in which group-level selection shapes a common substrate and mutation operator shared by all group members undergoing individual-level selection. We also place this process in an embodied ecology where distinct resource channels are not segregated, but coupled through the same behavioral primitives. These channels are classified as a positive-sum intake channel and a zero-sum redistribution channel. We investigate whether such a setting can give rise to role differentiation under turnover driven by birth and death. We find that in a learned ecology, both channels remain occupied at the colony level, and the collapse into a single acquisition mode is avoided. Zero-sum channel usage increases over generations despite not being directly optimized by group-level selection. Channel occupancy also fluctuates over the lifetime of a boid. Ablation studies suggest that most baseline performance is carried by the inherited behavioral basis, while the learned variation process provides a smaller but systematic improvement prior to saturation. Together, the results suggest that multi-level selection can enable groups in a common-pool setting to circumvent tragedy-of-the-commons through differentiated use of coupled channels under continual turnover.

</details>


### [20] [OrgAgent: Organize Your Multi-Agent System like a Company](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01020)
*Yiru Wang,Xinyue Shen,Yaohui Han,Michael Backes,Pin-Yu Chen,Tsung-Yi Ho*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: OrgAgent：一种公司式分层多智能体框架，通过治理、执行和合规三层结构组织多智能体协作，在推理任务中优于扁平结构，同时显著降低计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基于大语言模型的多智能体系统在复杂推理方面展现出强大潜力，但如何有效组织多个智能体仍是一个开放性问题。现有方法缺乏系统化的组织结构设计，导致协作效率不高。

Method: 提出OrgAgent框架，采用公司式分层结构将多智能体推理分解为三层：治理层负责规划和资源分配，执行层负责任务解决和审查，合规层负责最终答案控制。评估了不同推理任务、LLMs、执行模式和执行策略下的性能。

Result: 公司式分层结构在多智能体系统中普遍优于其他组织结构。分层协调在大多数设置下相比扁平协作减少了令牌消耗。例如，GPT-OSS-120B在SQuAD 2.0上，分层设置相比扁平多智能体系统性能提升102.73%，同时令牌使用减少74.52%。分析表明，当任务受益于稳定技能分配、受控信息流和分层验证时，分层结构帮助最大。

Conclusion: 组织结构是多智能体推理中的重要因素，不仅影响有效性和成本，还塑造协调行为。分层组织能够提供更好的性能-成本平衡，特别适合需要结构化协作的复杂推理任务。

Abstract: While large language model-based multi-agent systems have shown strong potential for complex reasoning, how to effectively organize multiple agents remains an open question. In this paper, we introduce OrgAgent, a company-style hierarchical multi-agent framework that separates collaboration into governance, execution, and compliance layers. OrgAgent decomposes multi-agent reasoning into three layers: a governance layer for planning and resource allocation, an execution layer for task solving and review, and a compliance layer for final answer control. By evaluating the framework across reasoning tasks, LLMs, execution modes, and execution policies, we find that multi-agent systems organized in a company-style hierarchy generally outperform other organizational structures. Besides, hierarchical coordination also reduces token consumption relative to flat collaboration in most settings. For example, for GPT-OSS-120B, the hierarchical setting improves performance over flat multi-agent system by 102.73% while reducing token usage by 74.52% on SQuAD 2.0. Further analysis shows that hierarchy helps most when tasks benefit from stable skill assignment, controlled information flow, and layered verification. Overall, our findings highlight organizational structure as an important factor in multi-agent reasoning, shaping not only effectiveness and cost, but also coordination behavior.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [21] [Quantifying Confidence in Assurance 2.0 Arguments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00034)
*Robin Bloomfield,John Rushby*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一种评估保证案例论证中概率置信度的新方法，该方法简单、系统且可靠，利用结构化论证中的分解方式，根据子主张的（不）依赖性和多样性程度提供不同方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 置信度对安全和保证案例至关重要，需要评估决策所需的置信度与实际论证提供的置信度。现有方法存在缺陷，需要一种专门针对保证论证的简单、系统且可靠的概率置信度评估方法。

Method: 利用结构化论证中主张的分解方式，根据子主张的依赖性和多样性程度提供不同评估方法。使用在其他领域已知的基本概率构造（如Frechet边界），但以专门针对保证论证的方式进行解释和应用，无需概率分析背景。

Result: 该方法能够抵御Graydon和Holloway对其他置信度方法提出的反例，可作为Assurance 2.0论证评估的补充工具。虽然Assurance 2.0的主要评估标准仍然是逻辑不可辩驳性和辩证检验，但概率评估可用于评估不同风险水平的成本/置信度权衡以及结构化论证中的整体置信度平衡。

Conclusion: 提出了一种简单、系统且可靠的概率置信度评估方法，专门针对保证案例论证设计，能够补充Assurance 2.0的现有评估框架，帮助评估成本/置信度权衡和整体置信度平衡。

Abstract: Confidence is central to safety and assurance cases: how much confidence a decision requires and how much the argument actually provides are both important questions. We present a new method for assessing probabilistic confidence in assurance case arguments that is simple, systematic and sound.
  It exploits the ways claims are decomposed in a structured argument and provides different approaches according to the different degrees of (in)dependence and diversity among subclaims and the way they eliminate concerns that undermine confidence in their parent claims. The method uses only elementary probabilistic constructions that are well-known in other contexts (e.g., Frechet bounds) but we interpret and apply them in a manner that is specifically focused on assurance arguments and requires no background in probabilistic analysis.
  We show that the method is not susceptible to the counterexamples that Graydon and Holloway exhibit for other approaches to confidence and we recommend it as an additional tool in evaluation of Assurance 2.0 arguments. The primary evaluation criteria for Assurance 2.0 remain logical indefeasibility and dialectical examination, but probabilistic assessment can be useful in evaluating cost/confidence tradeoffs for different risk levels, and the overall balance of confidence across a structured argument.

</details>


### [22] [Large Language Models for Analyzing Enterprise Architecture Debt in Unstructured Documentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00046)
*Christin Pagels,Simon Hacks,Rob Henk Bemthuis*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 使用大型语言模型自动检测企业架构债务中的"架构异味"，通过微调模型分析非结构化文档，并与GPT基准模型比较性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业架构债务源于次优设计决策和组件错位，会随时间恶化IT环境。现有方法主要依赖手动检测或仅分析结构化工件，大量非结构化文档未被充分利用，需要自动化解决方案

Method: 采用设计科学研究方法，开发基于LLM的原型系统：1) 输入非结构化文档（流程描述、战略文件等）；2) 应用微调检测模型；3) 输出识别的架构异味。通过案例研究评估，使用合成但真实的业务文档，并与自定义GPT模型进行基准测试

Result: LLM能够有效检测非结构化文本中的多种预定义EA异味。基准GPT模型在精度和处理速度上表现更优，而微调的本地模型在数据保护方面具有优势。两种方法都能识别架构债务的早期迹象

Conclusion: LLM为基础的企业架构异味检测具有实际应用价值，可集成到EA治理实践中。需要在性能（GPT模型）和数据安全（本地模型）之间权衡选择。该方法为自动化架构债务管理提供了新途径

Abstract: Enterprise Architecture Debt (EA Debt) arises from suboptimal design decisions and misaligned components that can degrade an organization's IT landscape over time. Early indicators, Enterprise Architecture Smells (EA Smells), are currently mainly detected manually or only from structured artifacts, leaving much unstructured documentation under-analyzed. This study proposes an approach using a large language model (LLM) to identify and quantify EA Debt in unstructured architectural documentation. Following a design science research approach, we design and evaluate an LLM-based prototype for automated EA Smell detection. The artifact ingests unstructured documents (e.g., process descriptions, strategy papers), applies fine-tuned detection models, and outputs identified smells. We evaluate the prototype through a case study using synthetic yet realistic business documents, benchmarking against a custom GPT-based model. Results show that LLMs can detect multiple predefined EA Smells in unstructured text, with the benchmark model achieving higher precision and processing speed, and the fine-tuned on-premise model offering data protection advantages. The findings highlight opportunities for integrating LLM-based smell detection into EA governance practice.

</details>


### [23] [The Energy Footprint of LLM-Based Environmental Analysis: LLMs and Domain Products](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00053)
*Alicia Bao,Jiamian He,Angel Hsu,Diego Manya,Ji,Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究评估了特定领域RAG系统与通用LLM在能源消耗方面的对比，发现RAG系统的能源消耗高度依赖于其设计，特别是更自主的流程会显著增加推理时能耗。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着大语言模型在气候变化和环境研究等特定领域应用的增加，理解其能源足迹变得重要。RAG系统在气候领域分析中的采用引发了一个关键问题：特定领域RAG工作流的能源消耗与直接使用通用LLM相比如何？先前研究主要关注独立模型调用或粗略的基于token的估计，而对实际应用工作流的能源影响理解不足。

Method: 评估了两个基于LLM的气候分析聊天机器人（ChatNetZero和ChatNDC）与通用GPT-4o-mini模型在推理时的能源消耗。通过将每个工作流分解为检索、生成和幻觉检查组件来估计实际用户查询下的能源使用。还在不同时间段和地理访问位置进行了测试。

Result: 特定领域RAG系统的能源消耗高度依赖于其设计。更自主的流程会显著增加推理时能源使用，特别是当用于额外的准确性或验证检查时，尽管它们可能不会带来相应比例的回答质量提升。

Conclusion: 虽然需要更多研究来在不同模型、环境和提示结构下更稳健地测试这些初步发现，但本研究提供了关于特定领域LLM产品设计如何影响能源足迹和输出质量的新理解。

Abstract: As large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in domain-specific applications, including climate change and environmental research, understanding their energy footprint has become an important concern. The growing adoption of retrieval-augmented (RAG) systems for climate-domain specific analysis raises a key question: how does the energy consumption of domain-specific RAG workflows compare with that of direct generic LLM usage? Prior research has focused on standalone model calls or coarse token-based estimates, while leaving the energy implications of deployed application workflows insufficiently understood. In this paper, we assess the inference-time energy consumption of two LLM-based climate analysis chatbots (ChatNetZero and ChatNDC) compared to the generic GPT-4o-mini model. We estimate energy use under actual user queries by decomposing each workflow into retrieval, generation, and hallucination-checking components. We also test across different times of day and geographic access locations. Our results show that the energy consumption of domain-specific RAG systems depends strongly on their design. More agentic pipelines substantially increase inference-time energy use, particularly when used for additional accuracy or verification checks, although they may not yield proportional gains in response quality. While more research is needed to further test these initial findings more robustly across models, environments and prompting structures, this study provides a new understanding on how the design of domain-specific LLM products affects both the energy footprint and quality of output.

</details>


### [24] [Making Sense of AI Agents Hype: Adoption, Architectures, and Takeaways from Practitioners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00189)
*Ruoyu Su,Matteo Esposito,Roberta Capuano,Rafiullah Omar,June Sallou,Henry Muccini,Davide Taibi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 对138个AI智能体实践者会议演讲的综述，分析企业如何采用基于智能体的架构、识别常见架构策略模式，以及分析应用领域和技术实现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 帮助从业者理解智能体系统在真实工业实践中的设计方式，填补理论与实际应用之间的知识鸿沟

Method: 对138个录制的实践者会议演讲进行内容分析，采用系统性综述方法，聚焦三个研究目标

Result: 识别了企业采用智能体架构的具体方式、发现了重复出现的架构策略和模式，并分析了LLM驱动的智能体系统的应用领域和技术实现

Conclusion: 该研究为工业界智能体系统设计提供了实证基础，揭示了实际应用中的架构模式和最佳实践，有助于指导从业者设计和实施智能体系统

Abstract: To support practitioners in understanding how agentic systems are designed in real-world industrial practice, we present a review of practitioner conference talks on AI agents. We analyzed 138 recorded talks to examine how companies adopt agent-based architectures (Objective 1), identify recurring architectural strategies and patterns (Objective 2), and analyze application domains and technologies used to implement and operate LLM-driven agentic systems (Objective 3).

</details>


### [25] [An Empirical Study on How Architectural Topology Affects Microservice Performance and Energy Usage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00080)
*Irena Ristova,Vincenzo Stoico*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究量化了六种典型微服务拓扑结构（顺序扇出、并行扇出、链式、分层、概率、网状）在不同规模下对能耗效率和性能的影响，发现拓扑结构显著影响微服务的能耗效率，网状拓扑表现最差，概率和并行扇出拓扑在CPU密集型负载下能效最高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务架构在现代软件系统中广泛应用，但其在云原生环境中的能耗效率问题日益凸显。虽然已有研究关注微服务分解和部署，但拓扑结构（服务间的结构安排和交互模式）对能耗效率的影响尚未得到充分探索。本研究旨在量化不同拓扑结构对微服务能耗效率和性能的影响。

Method: 使用μBench框架实例化六种典型拓扑结构（顺序扇出、并行扇出、链式、分层、概率、网状），每种拓扑分别在5、10、20个服务规模下进行测试。在相同工作负载下测量吞吐量、响应时间、能耗、CPU利用率和故障率等指标。

Result: 拓扑结构显著影响微服务的能耗效率。随着系统规模增大，能耗增加，其中网状和链式拓扑的能耗增长最为显著。网状拓扑整体表现最差，吞吐量低、响应时间长、故障率高。分层、链式和扇出设计在性能和能耗之间取得较好平衡。随着系统规模扩大，各项指标趋于收敛，概率和并行扇出拓扑在CPU密集型负载下表现出最高的能耗效率。

Conclusion: 拓扑结构是影响微服务能耗效率的关键因素。研究结果为设计更环保的微服务架构提供了指导，并为未来研究不同工作负载和部署方式对能耗效率的影响建立了基准。概率和并行扇出拓扑在CPU密集型场景下具有最佳能效表现。

Abstract: Microservice architectures form the backbone of modern software systems for their scalability, resilience, and maintainability, but their rise in cloud-native environments raises energy efficiency concerns. While prior research addresses microservice decomposition and placement, the impact of topology, the structural arrangement and interaction pattern among services, on energy efficiency remains largely underexplored. This study quantifies the impact of topologies on energy efficiency and performance across six canonical ones (Sequential Fan-Out, Parallel Fan-Out, Chain, Hierarchical, Probabilistic, Mesh), each instantiated at 5-, 10-, and 20-service scales using the $μ\text{Bench}$ framework. We measure throughput, response time, energy usage, CPU utilization, and failure rates under an identical workload. The results indicate that topology influences the energy efficiency of microservices under the studied conditions. As system size increases, energy consumption grows, with the steepest rise observed in dense Mesh and Chain topologies. Mesh topologies perform worst overall, with low throughput, long response times, and high failure rates. Hierarchical, Chain, and Fan-Out designs balance performance and energy use better. As systems scale, metrics converge, with Probabilistic and Parallel Fan-Out emerging as the most energy-efficient under CPU-bound loads. These results guide greener microservice architecture design and serve as a baseline for future research on workload and deployment impacts.

</details>


### [26] [From Domain Understanding to Design Readiness: a playbook for GenAI-supported learning in Software Engineering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00120)
*Rafal Wlodarski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究评估了在软件工程课程中使用定制化ChatGPT导师辅助学生学习加密货币金融基础和领域驱动设计的效果，发现AI响应准确度高但支持性较低，学生自我效能感显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软件工程课程需要快速提升学生在领域理解和建模方法等支持性知识领域的技能，研究旨在探索生成式AI如何辅助教学，特别是在领域理解和建模任务中。

Method: 在硕士课程的两周里程碑中，29名学生使用基于课程知识库定制的ChatGPT（GPT-3.5）导师学习加密货币金融基础和领域驱动设计。记录了所有交互，随机抽样34.5%的提示-答案对（60/174）使用五维度评估标准（准确性、相关性、教学价值、认知负荷、支持性）进行评估，并收集了前后自我效能感数据。

Result: AI响应在准确性（平均98.9%，无事实错误，仅2/60轻微不准确）和相关性（平均92.2%）方面表现优异。教学价值高（89.4%），认知负荷通常适当（82.78%），但支持性较低（37.78%）。学生在生成式AI辅助的领域学习和DDD应用方面报告了显著的自我效能感提升。

Conclusion: 研究提炼了17个具体的教学实践，涵盖提示/配置和课程/工作流设计。在单一课程背景下，结果表明生成式AI支持的学习可以补充领域理解和建模任务的教学，但在语气和后续结构方面仍有改进空间。

Abstract: Software engineering courses often require rapid upskilling in supporting knowledge areas such as domain understanding and modeling methods. We report an experience from a two-week milestone in a master's course where 29 students used a customized ChatGPT (GPT-3.5) tutor grounded in a curated course knowledge base to learn cryptocurrency-finance basics and Domain-Driven Design (DDD). We logged all interactions and evaluated a 34.5% random sample of prompt-answer pairs (60/~174) with a five-dimension rubric (accuracy, relevance, pedagogical value, cognitive load, supportiveness), and we collected pre/post self-efficacy. Responses were consistently accurate and relevant in this setting: accuracy averaged 98.9% with no factual errors and only 2/60 minor inaccuracies, and relevance averaged 92.2%. Pedagogical value was high (89.4%) with generally appropriate cognitive load (82.78%), but supportiveness was low (37.78%). Students reported large pre-post self-efficacy gains for genAI-assisted domain learning and DDD application. From these observations we distill seventeen concrete teaching practices spanning prompt/configuration and course/workflow design (e.g., setting expected granularity, constraining verbosity, curating guardrail examples, adding small credit with a simple quality rubric). Within this single-course context, results suggest that genAI-supported learning can complement instruction in domain understanding and modeling tasks, while leaving room to improve tone and follow-up structure.

</details>


### [27] [A Study on the Impact of Fault localization Granularity for Repository-Scale Code Repair Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00167)
*Joseph Townsend,Chandresh Pravin,Kwun Ho Ngan,Matthieu Parizy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究探讨了在仓库级自动程序修复中，故障定位粒度对代码修复效果的影响，发现函数级粒度通常表现最佳，但理想粒度可能因任务而异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前仓库级程序修复方法通常将故障定位和代码修复分为两个独立阶段，但缺乏在完美故障定位假设下对定位粒度影响的系统性研究。现有研究未将粒度问题与定位准确性问题分离，也没有在仓库规模下专门研究粒度对修复效果的影响。

Method: 研究者修改了Agentless框架的定位阶段，使其能够检索真实故障定位数据并将其作为上下文信息包含在修复阶段的提示中。使用SWE-Bench-Mini数据集进行实验，比较了行级、函数级和文件级三种不同粒度级别的修复效果。

Result: 在完美故障定位假设下，函数级粒度在SWE-Bench-Mini数据集上获得了最高的修复率，优于行级和文件级粒度。然而，深入分析表明理想的定位粒度可能依赖于具体任务类型。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一个概念验证，表明故障定位粒度确实会影响仓库级自动代码修复的效果。虽然函数级粒度通常表现最佳，但最佳粒度可能是任务相关的。研究鼓励进一步探索故障定位与代码修复两个阶段之间的关系。

Abstract: Automatic program repair can be a challenging task, especially when resolving complex issues at a repository-level, which often involves issue reproduction, fault localization, code repair, testing and validation. Issues of this scale can be commonly found in popular GitHub repositories or datasets that are derived from them.
  Some repository-level approaches separate localization and repair into distinct phases. Where this is the case, the fault localization approaches vary in terms of the granularity of localization. Where the impact of granularity is explored to some degree for smaller datasets, not all isolate this issue from the separate question of localization accuracy by testing code repair under the assumption of perfect fault localization. To the best of the authors' knowledge, no repository-scale studies have explicitly investigated granularity under this assumption, nor conducted a systematic empirical comparison of granularity levels in isolation.
  We propose a framework for performing such tests by modifying the localization phase of the Agentless framework to retrieve ground-truth localization data and include this as context in the prompt fed to the repair phase. We show that under this configuration and as a generalization over the SWE-Bench-Mini dataset, function-level granularity yields the highest repair rate against line-level and file-level. However, a deeper dive suggests that the ideal granularity may in fact be task dependent.
  This study is not intended to improve on the state-of-the-art, nor do we intend for results to be compared against any complete agentic frameworks. Rather, we present a proof of concept for investigating how fault localization may impact automatic code repair in repository-scale scenarios. We present preliminary findings to this end and encourage further research into this relationship between the two phases.

</details>


### [28] [Risk-Aware Batch Testing for Performance Regression Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00222)
*Ali Sayedsalehi,Peter C. Rigby,Gregory Mierzwinski*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出一个风险感知框架，将机器学习预测的提交风险与自适应批处理结合，用于性能回归测试，在Firefox案例中显著减少测试执行次数和成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模持续集成系统中，为每个提交执行完整性能测试成本过高。现有性能回归预测和批处理方法各有局限：预测模型很少集成到CI决策中，传统批处理策略忽略提交级别的异质性。

Method: 引入风险感知框架，集成机器学习预测的提交风险与自适应批处理。使用Mozilla Firefox作为案例研究，构建生产数据衍生的已确认回归数据集，微调ModernBERT、CodeBERT和LLaMA-3.1变体来估计提交级性能回归风险。风险分数驱动一系列风险感知批处理策略，包括风险老化优先级批处理和风险自适应流批处理。

Result: CodeBERT达到0.694 ROC-AUC的最佳风险预测性能。最佳配置RAPB-la相比Mozilla生产基线：减少32.4%总测试执行次数，降低3.8%平均反馈时间，保持平均定位时间接近基线水平，减少26.2%最大定位时间，估计每年节省约49.1万美元基础设施成本。

Conclusion: 风险感知批处理测试能够减少CI资源消耗，同时提高诊断及时性。该框架在Firefox案例中展示了显著的性能改进和成本节约，为性能回归测试提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: Performance regression testing is essential in large-scale continuous-integration (CI) systems, yet executing full performance suites for every commit is prohibitively expensive. Prior work on performance regression prediction and batch testing has shown independent benefits, but each faces practical limitations: predictive models are rarely integrated into CI decision-making, and conventional batching strategies ignore commit-level heterogeneity.
  We unify these strands by introducing a risk-aware framework that integrates machine-learned commit risk with adaptive batching. Using Mozilla Firefox as a case study, we construct a production-derived dataset of human-confirmed regressions aligned chronologically with Autoland, and fine-tune ModernBERT, CodeBERT, and LLaMA-3.1 variants to estimate commit-level performance regression risk, achieving up to 0.694 ROC-AUC with CodeBERT. The risk scores drive a family of risk-aware batching strategies, including Risk-Aged Priority Batching and Risk-Adaptive Stream Batching, evaluated through realistic CI simulations.
  Across thousands of historical Firefox commits, our best overall configuration, Risk-Aged Priority Batching with linear aggregation (RAPB-la), yields a Pareto improvement over Mozilla's production-inspired baseline. RAPB-la reduces total test executions by 32.4%, decreases mean feedback time by 3.8%, maintains mean time-to-culprit at approximately the baseline level, reduces maximum time-to-culprit by 26.2%, and corresponds to an estimated annual infrastructure cost savings of approximately $491K under our cost model. These results demonstrate that risk-aware batch testing can reduce CI resource consumption while improving diagnostic timeliness. To support reproducibility and future research, we release a complete replication package containing all datasets, fine-tuning pipelines, and implementations of our batching algorithms.

</details>


### [29] [VeriAct: Beyond Verifiability -- Agentic Synthesis of Correct and Complete Formal Specifications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00280)
*Md Rakib Hossain Misu,Iris Ma,Cristina V. Lopes*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文揭示了当前基于LLM的JML规范生成方法存在验证通过率与规范实际正确性之间的差距，提出了Spec-Harness评估框架和VeriAct验证引导的智能框架来生成既可通过验证又正确完整的规范。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于大语言模型自动生成形式化规范（特别是JML）的研究虽然报告了高验证通过率，但验证通过并不等同于规范的正确性和完整性。需要更深入地评估生成的规范质量，并开发能够生成真正正确和完整规范的方法。

Method: 1. 首先对经典方法和基于提示的方法进行JML规范综合的全面评估；2. 研究提示优化是否能通过结构化验证反馈提升综合质量；3. 提出Spec-Harness评估框架，通过符号验证测量规范的正确性和完整性；4. 提出VeriAct验证引导的智能框架，通过LLM驱动的规划、代码执行、验证和Spec-Harness反馈的闭环迭代合成和修复规范。

Result: 实验发现：1. 提示优化虽然提高了验证通过率，但存在明显的性能上限；2. Spec-Harness揭示大量验证器接受的规范（包括优化后的）实际上不正确或不完整，存在验证器无法检测的输入输出过度约束或约束不足问题；3. VeriAct在两个基准数据集上优于基于提示和提示优化的基线方法，生成的规范不仅可验证，而且正确完整。

Conclusion: 验证通过率不能准确反映形式化规范的实际质量，需要更严格的评估方法。VeriAct框架通过验证引导的迭代修复过程，能够生成真正正确和完整的规范，超越了现有方法的性能上限。

Abstract: Formal specifications play a central role in ensuring software reliability and correctness. However, automatically synthesizing high-quality formal specifications remains a challenging task, often requiring domain expertise. Recent work has applied large language models to generate specifications in Java Modeling Language (JML), reporting high verification pass rates. But does passing a verifier mean that the specification is actually correct and complete? In this work, we first conduct a comprehensive evaluation comparing classical and prompt-based approaches for automated JML specification synthesis. We then investigate whether prompt optimization can push synthesis quality further by evolving prompts through structured verification feedback. While optimization improves verifier pass rates, we find a clear performance ceiling. More critically, we propose Spec-Harness, an evaluation framework that measures specification correctness and completeness through symbolic verification, revealing that a large fraction of verifier-accepted specifications, including optimized ones, are in fact incorrect or incomplete, over- or under-constraining both inputs and outputs in ways invisible to the verifier. To push beyond this ceiling, we propose VeriAct, a verification-guided agentic framework that iteratively synthesizes and repairs specifications through a closed loop of LLM-driven planning, code execution, verification, and Spec-Harness feedback. Our experiments on two benchmark datasets show that VeriAct outperforms both prompt-based and prompt-optimized baselines, producing specifications that are not only verifiable but also correct and complete.

</details>


### [30] [Shapley-Guided Neural Repair Approach via Derivative-Free Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00422)
*Xinyu Sun,Wanwei Liu,Haoang Chi,Tingyu Chen,Xiaoguang Mao,Shangwen Wang,Lei Bu,Jingyi Wang,Yang Tan,Zhenyi Qi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: SHARPEN：一种结合可解释故障定位与无导数优化的DNN修复框架，用于处理后门、对抗攻击和不公平性等缺陷，在保持准确性的同时显著提升修复效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有DNN修复方法主要依赖梯度计算（限制特定激活函数）或使用搜索算法（定位和修复不可解释），且缺乏跨多个属性的通用性。需要一种可解释、无导数、通用的修复方法。

Method: 1. 基于Deep SHAP的可解释故障定位：采用分层粗到细方法，先按聚合影响重新排序层，然后通过分析属性违反状态与良性状态之间的激活差异定位故障神经元/过滤器。2. 使用CMA-ES（协方差矩阵自适应进化策略）进行修复：利用协方差矩阵捕获变量依赖关系，实现无梯度搜索和耦合神经元的协调调整。

Result: 在三个修复任务中表现优异：后门移除（+10.56%）、对抗缓解（+5.78%）、不公平性修复（+11.82%）。在平衡属性修复和准确性保持方面优于基线方法，且能处理多样化任务。

Conclusion: SHARPEN通过结合可解释定位与进化优化，实现了跨架构的无导数修复，对梯度异常和超参数不敏感。其模块化设计可与不同无导数优化器即插即用，展现了灵活性和通用性。

Abstract: DNNs are susceptible to defects like backdoors, adversarial attacks, and unfairness, undermining their reliability. Existing approaches mainly involve retraining, optimization, constraint-solving, or search algorithms. However, most methods rely on gradient calculations, restricting applicability to specific activation functions (e.g., ReLU), or use search algorithms with uninterpretable localization and repair. Furthermore, they often lack generalizability across multiple properties. We propose SHARPEN, integrating interpretable fault localization with a derivative-free optimization strategy. First, SHARPEN introduces a Deep SHAP-based localization strategy quantifying each layer's and neuron's marginal contribution to erroneous outputs. Specifically, a hierarchical coarse-to-fine approach reranks layers by aggregated impact, then locates faulty neurons/filters by analyzing activation divergences between property-violating and benign states. Subsequently, SHARPEN incorporates CMA-ES to repair identified neurons. CMA-ES leverages a covariance matrix to capture variable dependencies, enabling gradient-free search and coordinated adjustments across coupled neurons. By combining interpretable localization with evolutionary optimization, SHARPEN enables derivative-free repair across architectures, being less sensitive to gradient anomalies and hyperparameters. We demonstrate SHARPEN's effectiveness on three repair tasks. Balancing property repair and accuracy preservation, it outperforms baselines in backdoor removal (+10.56%), adversarial mitigation (+5.78%), and unfairness repair (+11.82%). Notably, SHARPEN handles diverse tasks, and its modular design is plug-and-play with different derivative-free optimizers, highlighting its flexibility.

</details>


### [31] [Programming by Chat: A Large-Scale Behavioral Analysis of 11,579 Real-World AI-Assisted IDE Sessions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00436)
*Ningzhi Tang,Chaoran Chen,Zihan Fang,Gelei Xu,Maria Dhakal,Yiyu Shi,Collin McMillan,Yu Huang,Toby Jia-Jun Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首次大规模研究IDE集成AI编程助手在真实开发环境中的使用情况，分析了近7.5万条开发者消息，揭示了对话式编程如何改变软件开发工作组织方式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究主要基于小规模受控环境或通用聊天机器人，缺乏对代码库感知的IDE工作流程在真实开发环境中的实证研究。IDE集成AI编程助手正在重塑软件开发，但这一转变的实证调查仍然有限。

Method: 分析了74,998条开发者消息，来自11,579个聊天会话，涵盖1,300个代码库和899名开发者，使用Cursor和GitHub Copilot。这些聊天记录作为日常开发的一部分被提交到公共代码库，捕获了真实环境中的行为。

Result: 发现对话式编程在三个方面改变了编程工作组织方式：1) 渐进式规范 - 开发者迭代细化输出而非预先指定完整任务；2) 认知工作重新分配 - 开发者将诊断、理解和验证工作委托给AI；3) 主动管理协作 - 开发者将计划外化为持久工件，并通过上下文注入和行为约束来协商AI自主性。

Conclusion: 研究提供了AI辅助开发的基础性实证见解，为未来编程环境设计提供了启示，揭示了对话式编程如何从根本上改变软件开发的工作组织和认知过程。

Abstract: IDE-integrated AI coding assistants, which operate conversationally within developers' working codebases with access to project context and multi-file editing, are rapidly reshaping software development. However, empirical investigation of this shift remains limited: existing studies largely rely on small-scale, controlled settings or analyze general-purpose chatbots rather than codebase-aware IDE workflows. We present, to the best of our knowledge, the first large-scale study of real-world conversational programming in IDE-native settings, analyzing 74,998 developer messages from 11,579 chat sessions across 1,300 repositories and 899 developers using Cursor and GitHub Copilot. These chats were committed to public repositories as part of routine development, capturing in-the-wild behavior. Our findings reveal three shifts in how programming work is organized: conversational programming operates as progressive specification, with developers iteratively refining outputs rather than specifying complete tasks upfront; developers redistribute cognitive work to AI, delegating diagnosis, comprehension, and validation rather than engaging with code and outputs directly; and developers actively manage the collaboration, externalizing plans into persistent artifacts, and negotiating AI autonomy through context injection and behavioral constraints. These results provide foundational empirical insights into AI-assisted development and offer implications for the design of future programming environments.

</details>


### [32] [LDMDroid: Leveraging LLMs for Detecting Data Manipulation Errors in Android Apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00458)
*Xiangyang Xiao,Huaxun Huang,Rongxin Wu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LDMDroid：基于大语言模型的Android应用数据操作错误自动检测框架，通过状态感知的UI事件序列生成和视觉特征识别数据状态变化，提高了数据操作功能触发成功率和错误检测准确性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Android应用的数据操作功能对应用可靠性至关重要，但数据操作错误检测面临两大挑战：现有UI测试工具难以充分覆盖触发数据操作功能的UI路径，且依赖手动编写测试脚本。

Method: 提出LDMDroid框架，利用大语言模型进行状态感知的UI事件序列生成以增强数据操作功能触发成功率，同时使用视觉特征识别数据状态变化来提高错误验证准确性。

Result: 在24个真实Android应用上评估，相比基线方法提高了数据操作功能触发成功率，发现了17个独特bug，其中14个被开发者确认，11个已修复。

Conclusion: LDMDroid有效解决了Android应用数据操作错误检测的挑战，通过结合大语言模型和视觉特征分析，实现了自动化的高效错误检测。

Abstract: Android apps rely heavily on Data Manipulation Functionalities (DMFs) for handling app-specific data through CRUDS operations, making their correctness vital for reliability. However, detecting Data Manipulation Errors (DMEs) is challenging due to their dependence on specific UI interaction sequences and manifestation as logic bugs. Existing automated UI testing tools face two primary challenges: insufficient UI path coverage for adequate DMF triggering and reliance on manually written test scripts. To address these issues, we propose an automated approach using Large Language Models (LLMs) for DME detection. We developed LDMDroid, an automated UI testing framework for Android apps. LDMDroid enhances DMF triggering success by guiding LLMs through a state-aware process for generating UI event sequences. It also uses visual features to identify changes in data states, improving DME verification accuracy. We evaluated LDMDroid on 24 real-world Android apps, demonstrating improved DMF triggering success rates compared to baselines. LDMDroid discovered 17 unique bugs, with 14 confirmed by developers and 11 fixed. The tool is publicly available at https://github.com/runnnnnner200/LDMDroid.

</details>


### [33] [Enhancing REST API Fuzzing with Access Policy Violation Checks and Injection Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00702)
*Omur Sahin,Man Zhang,Andrea Arcuri*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出了一系列用于检测REST API安全漏洞的新型自动化预言机，包括访问策略违规、SQL注入和XSS攻击检测，并将其集成到现有模糊测试工具EvoMaster中


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有REST API模糊测试工具主要关注崩溃检测（如500服务器错误），但安全漏洞对云基础设施有重大影响，需要更全面的安全测试方法

Method: 开发了检测访问策略违规、SQL注入和XSS攻击的新型自动化预言机，将其集成到EvoMaster模糊测试工具中，在模糊测试会话后执行"安全测试"阶段验证这些预言机

Result: 在52个不同API上进行实验（9个人工示例、8个黑盒测试的易受攻击API、36个白盒测试的WFD语料库API），结果显示新技术能够在多个API中检测到安全问题

Conclusion: 提出的新型预言机及其在模糊测试过程中的自动化集成能够有效检测REST API中的安全问题，并能生成可执行的测试用例（Java、Kotlin、Python、JavaScript格式）

Abstract: Due to their widespread use in industry, several techniques have been proposed in the literature to fuzz REST APIs. Existing fuzzers for REST APIs have been focusing on detecting crashes (e.g., 500 HTTP server error status code). However, security vulnerabilities can have major drastic consequences on existing cloud infrastructures.
  In this paper, we propose a series of novel automated oracles aimed at detecting violations of access policies in REST APIs, as well as executing traditional attacks such as SQL Injection and XSS. These novel automated oracles can be integrated into existing fuzzers, in which, once the fuzzing session is completed, a ``security testing'' phase is executed to verify these oracles. When a security fault is detected, as output our technique is able to general executable test cases in different formats, like Java, Kotlin, Python and JavaScript test suites.
  Our novel techniques are integrated as an extension of EvoMaster, a state-of-the-art open-source fuzzer for REST APIs. Experiments are carried out on 9 artificial examples, 8 vulnerable-by-design REST APIs with black-box testing, and 36 REST APIs from the WFD corpus with white-box testing, for a total of 52 distinct APIs. Results show that our novel oracles and their automated integration in a fuzzing process can lead to detect security issues in several of these APIs.

</details>


### [34] [Yet Even Less Is Even Better For Agentic, Reasoning, and Coding LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00824)
*Yang Ye,Jingyuan Tan,Tianyue Jiang,Ruizhe Ye,Qiankun He,Jiarui Yang,Jian Dong,Sicong Liang,Chongjian Yue,Peibai Xu,Lufan Lu,Taotao Qian,Junbao Hu,Yuechan Hao,Ensheng Shi,Qi Zhang,Yi Hao,Na Fan,Xin Tan,Shuai Yao,Zhiwei Shen,Zongchen Li,Yanlin Wang,Chong Chen,Yuchi Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: STITCH框架通过筛选高质量训练轨迹，实现"少即是多"的软件工程智能体训练，在多种编程语言和模型规模上显著提升性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 训练有效的软件工程智能体需要大量任务特定轨迹，数据构建成本高昂。受数学推理中"少即是多"假设启发，研究者探索将其扩展到智能体场景，旨在用更少但更高质量的训练轨迹获得更优的智能体能力

Method: 提出STITCH（滑动记忆轨迹推理和任务分块启发式）框架，采用从粗到精的机制过滤低价值噪声，保留决策关键token以最大化训练信号质量。该端到端训练框架在多种智能体框架（mini-SWE-agent、MSWE-agent）、模型规模（30B到355B）和多语言设置（Python、Java、ArkTS）中进行实验

Result: 在SWE-bench Verified上，使用STITCH训练的模型相比基础模型获得高达63.16%的相对改进。在Multi-SWE-bench（Java）上，MiniMax-M2.5-STITCH达到43.75%（使用CodeArts Agent框架增加16.67%）。在HarmonyOS（ArkTS）上，GLM-4.7-STITCH将编译通过率提升至61.31%（+43.34%），仅使用不到1K训练轨迹

Conclusion: 实验结果证实"少即是多"范式能有效推广到跨多种语言和模型规模的复杂智能体任务中，STITCH框架通过优化训练轨迹质量显著提升软件工程智能体性能

Abstract: Training effective software engineering agents requires large volumes of task-specific trajectories, incurring substantial data construction costs. Inspired by the "Less-Is-More" hypothesis in mathematical reasoning, we investigate its extension to agentic scenarios and propose an end-to-end training framework that achieves superior agentic capabilities with fewer but higher-quality training trajectories. This is achieved via STITCH (Sliding-memory Trajectory Inference and Task Chunking Heuristic), a coarse-to-fine mechanism that filters low-value noise and retains decision-critical tokens to maximize training signal quality. We conduct experiments across multiple agent frameworks (e.g., mini-SWE-agent, MSWE-agent), model scales (30B to 355B), and multilingual settings (Python, Java, and ArkTS). On SWE-bench Verified, models trained with STITCH achieve up to 63.16% relative improvement over base models. On Multi-SWE-bench (Java), MiniMax-M2.5-STITCH achieves 43.75% with our CodeArts Agent scaffold (+16.67%). On HarmonyOS (ArkTS), GLM-4.7-STITCH improves the compilation pass rate to 61.31% (+43.34%) with less than 1K training trajectories. Our results confirm that the "Less-Is-More" paradigm generalizes effectively to complex agentic tasks across diverse languages and model scales.

</details>


### [35] [Portable and Secure CI/CD for COBOL: Lessons from an Industrial Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00936)
*Andreas Askholm,Kenneth Johnsen,Jacopo Mauro*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 银行数据系统将脆弱的Jenkins COBOL CI/CD流水线迁移到容器化架构，实现82%运行时间减少


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在受监管行业中使用遗留技术时，CI/CD流水线本身可能成为技术债务的来源。Bankdata的Jenkins-based COBOL CI/CD流水线变得脆弱、缓慢且与平台特定逻辑紧密耦合，导致执行时间长、维护成本高和供应商锁定问题。

Method: 采用容器化架构迁移，包括：1) 为平台逻辑创建抽象层；2) 简化仓库结构；3) 使用预构建的OCI兼容镜像包含COBOL工具和依赖。从原本依赖Groovy脚本分布在四个仓库并需要运行时依赖安装的架构迁移。

Result: 新设计实现了82%的运行时间减少。通过容器化架构解决了原始架构的脆弱性、缓慢执行和平台耦合问题，显著降低了维护成本。

Conclusion: 该工业案例研究为在遗留、高安全环境中现代化流水线提供了指导，强调了抽象化、容器化和组织采用方面的经验教训，展示了如何通过架构改进显著提升CI/CD流水线的效率和可维护性。

Abstract: Continuous integration and delivery (CI/CD) pipelines are critical for sustaining the evolution of large software systems. In regulated industries with legacy technologies, however, pipelines themselves can become a source of technical debt. This paper presents an industrial case study of Bankdata, a cooperative IT provider for Danish banks, where a Jenkins-based COBOL CI/CD pipeline had grown fragile, slow, and tightly coupled to platform-specific logic. The original architecture relied on Groovy scripts spread across four repositories with runtime dependency installation, leading to long execution times, high maintenance costs, and vendor lock-in.
  We report on the migration to a containerized architecture featuring an abstraction layer for platform logic, simplified repository structure, and a pre-built OCI-compliant image containing COBOL tools and dependencies. The new design achieved an 82% runtime reduction. Our experience highlights lessons on abstraction, containerization, and organizational adoption, offering guidance for modernizing pipelines in legacy, high-security environments.

</details>


### [36] [Revision or Re-Solving? Decomposing Second-Pass Gains in Multi-LLM Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01029)
*Jingjie Ning,Xueqi Li,Chengyu Yu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 多LLM修订流程的增益主要来自任务结构、草稿质量和信息类型，而非单纯的错误修正。在选择题任务中，直接使用强模型更有效；在代码生成中，即使语义空洞的草稿也能提供结构支架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑多LLM修订流程增益源于真正错误修正的普遍假设，需要分解修订过程中的不同贡献因素，以更精确地理解多模型协作机制。

Method: 采用对照分解实验设计，使用四个匹配条件将第二遍增益分解为三个可加性成分：重新求解、支架和内容。在两个模型对上评估三个基准测试（知识密集型选择题和竞争性编程）。

Result: 多LLM修订增益不是单一的，取决于任务结构、草稿质量和草稿信息类型。选择题任务中，大部分增益与强模型重新求解一致，直接路由到强模型比修订弱草稿更有效。代码生成任务中，两阶段提示仍然有用，因为即使语义空洞的草稿也能提供结构支架，而弱草稿内容可能有害。角色反转实验显示强草稿明显有益于弱审阅者。

Conclusion: 多LLM修订的效用受任务结构和草稿质量的动态瓶颈限制，需要更有针对性的流程设计而非通用的修订策略。

Abstract: Multi-LLM revision pipelines, in which a second model reviews and improves a draft produced by a first, are widely assumed to derive their gains from genuine error correction. We question this assumption with a controlled decomposition experiment that uses four matched conditions to separate second-pass gains into three additive components: re-solving, scaffold, and content. We evaluate this design across two model pairs on three benchmarks spanning knowledge-intensive MCQ and competitive programming. Our results show that the gains of multi-LLM revision are not monolithic, but depend on task structure, draft quality, and the type of draft information. On MCQ tasks, where the answer space is constrained and drafts provide little structural guidance, most gains are consistent with stronger-model re-solving, and directly routing queries to the stronger model can be more effective than revising a weak draft. On code generation tasks, however, two-stage prompting remains useful because even semantically null drafts can provide substantial structural scaffolding, while weak draft content can be harmful. Finally, role-reversed experiments show that strong drafts clearly benefit weak reviewers. Ultimately, our findings demonstrate that the utility of multi-LLM revision is dynamically bottlenecked by task structure and draft quality, necessitating more targeted pipeline designs rather than blanket revision strategies.

</details>
