### [37] [Birdcast: Interest-aware BEV Multicasting for Infrastructure-assisted Collaborative Perception](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00701)
*Yanan Ma,Zhengru Fang,Yihang Tao,Yu Guo,Yiqin Deng,Xianhao Chen,Yuguang Fang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Birdcast是一个用于车路协同感知的组播框架，通过联合特征选择和组播分组优化，解决异构感知需求和通信瓶颈问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 车路协同感知中，下行链路传输存在通信瓶颈，且车辆需要根据自身遮挡和位置获取异构但重叠的信息，传统单播/广播协议效率低下。

Method: 提出Birdcast组播框架，通过考虑个体兴趣区域，将问题建模为联合特征选择和组播分组的优化问题，并开发具有理论保证的加速贪婪算法。

Result: 在V2X-Sim数据集上的仿真表明，Birdcast在系统效用和感知质量上显著优于现有基线，总效用提升达27%，平均精度均值(mAP)提高3.2%。

Conclusion: Birdcast不仅解决了车路协同感知中的通信效率问题，还提供了一个适用于用户具有异构兴趣和不同信道条件的通用组播框架。

Abstract: Vehicle-to-infrastructure collaborative perception (V2I-CP) leverages a high-vantage node to transmit supplementary information, i.e., bird's-eye-view (BEV) feature maps, to vehicles, effectively overcoming line-of-sight limitations. However, the downlink V2I transmission introduces a significant communication bottleneck. Moreover, vehicles in V2I-CP require \textit{heterogeneous yet overlapping} information tailored to their unique occlusions and locations, rendering standard unicast/broadcast protocols inefficient. To address this limitation, we propose \textit{Birdcast}, a novel multicasting framework for V2I-CP. By accounting for individual maps of interest, we formulate a joint feature selection and multicast grouping problem to maximize network-wide utility under communication constraints. Since this formulation is a mixed-integer nonlinear program and is NP-hard, we develop an accelerated greedy algorithm with a theoretical $(1 - 1/\sqrt{e})$ approximation guarantee. While motivated by CP, Birdcast provides a general framework applicable to a wide range of multicasting systems where users possess heterogeneous interests and varying channel conditions. Extensive simulations on the V2X-Sim dataset demonstrate that Birdcast significantly outperforms state-of-the-art baselines in both system utility and perception quality, achieving up to 27\% improvement in total utility and a 3.2\% increase in mean average precision (mAP).

</details>


<div id='cs.LO'></div>

# cs.LO [[Back]](#toc)

### [38] [The Varieties of Ought-Implies-Can and Deontic STIT Logic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00967)
*Kees van Berkel,Tim S. Lyon*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 该论文为STIT逻辑提供了一个模块化框架，用于处理多种"应当蕴含能够"原则的解读，形式化了十种不同解读，并提供了相应的完备演算系统。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在现有的STIT逻辑道义扩展中，"应当蕴含能够"原则扮演核心角色，但哲学文献中提出了多种不同的OiC解读。需要建立一个能够容纳多种OiC解读的模块化框架。

Method: 为道义STIT逻辑构建模块化框架，讨论、比较并形式化十种不同的OiC解读。为容纳这些OiC原则的各种STIT逻辑提供可靠且完备的序列式演算系统。

Result: 建立了能够处理多种OiC解读的STIT逻辑系统，形式化了十种不同解读，提供了相应的演算系统，并分析了不同OiC原则之间的逻辑关系。

Conclusion: 该论文成功构建了一个模块化框架，能够容纳多种"应当蕴含能够"原则的解读，为STIT逻辑的道义扩展提供了更丰富的理论基础，并提出了描述不同OiC解读之间逻辑承诺的认可原则。

Abstract: STIT logic is a prominent framework for the analysis of multi-agent choice-making. In the available deontic extensions of STIT, the principle of Ought-implies-Can (OiC) fulfills a central role. However, in the philosophical literature a variety of alternative OiC interpretations have been proposed and discussed. This paper provides a modular framework for deontic STIT that accounts for a multitude of OiC readings. In particular, we discuss, compare, and formalize ten such readings. We provide sound and complete sequent-style calculi for all of the various STIT logics accommodating these OiC principles. We formally analyze the resulting logics and discuss how the different OiC principles are logically related. In particular, we propose an endorsement principle describing which OiC readings logically commit one to other OiC readings.

</details>


### [39] [A Framework for Coalgebraic Reward-Sensitive Bisimulation (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01103)
*Pedro H. Azevedo de Amorim,Mayuko Kori,Koko Muroya*

Main category: cs.LO

TL;DR: 提出一个用于建模奖励敏感双模拟的框架，该框架统一处理定性和定量差异，通过分级和非分级双模拟概念以及范畴化方法实现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有双模拟概念主要关注定性行为等价，缺乏对定量差异（如累积奖励）的系统性处理。需要建立一个统一框架来同时捕捉系统的定性和定量方面

Method: 采用纤维化和余代数方法，引入两种交互的双模拟概念：分级双模拟（跟踪有界奖励差异）和非分级双模拟（抽象化奖励差异）。使用范畴粘合技术形式化关联这两个概念，并与标准余代数概念（如最终余代数）良好交互

Result: 该框架能够统一表征各种系统的双模拟概念，包括带奖励自动机的关系型双模拟和带标签马尔可夫过程的度量型双模拟。在温和假设下，通过组合双模拟概念获得统一表征

Conclusion: 提出的奖励敏感双模拟框架具有广泛适用性，能够统一处理定性和定量系统行为差异，为不同系统类型的双模拟分析提供了通用的范畴化理论基础

Abstract: In this paper we present a framework for modelling \emph{reward-sensitive bisimulations}, that is, bisimulations that account for quantitative differences such as accumulated rewards. To capture both qualitative and quantitative aspects uniformly, we consider two interacting notions of bisimulation: a graded variant that tracks bounded reward differences, and an ungraded one that abstracts from them.
  Our characterization of these notions is done in the fibrational and coalgebraic approach to (bi)simulation initiated by Hermida and Jacobs. To formally relate the graded and ungraded notions, we deploy categorical gluing, a standard technique in categorical logic. Furthermore, we show that this construction interacts well with standard coalgebra concepts, such as final coalgebras, and that it yields a unified characterization in terms of combined notions of bisimulations under mild assumptions.
  In order to demonstrate the versatility of our approach, we show how it encompasses various bisimulation notions for different kinds of systems, including relation-based bisimulations for automata with rewards and metric-based notions of bisimulations for labelled Markov processes.

</details>


<div id='cs.RO'></div>

# cs.RO [[Back]](#toc)

### [40] [Generalizable Dense Reward for Long-Horizon Robotic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00055)
*Silong Yong,Stephen Sheng,Carl Qi,Xiaojie Wang,Evan Sheehan,Anurag Shivaprasad,Yaqi Xie,Katia Sycara,Yesh Dattatreya*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: VLLR是一个结合LLM/VLM外部奖励和策略自确定性内部奖励的密集奖励框架，用于微调机器人基础策略，无需人工奖励工程，在CHORES基准上显著提升任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人基础策略主要通过大规模模仿学习训练，但在长时程任务中因分布偏移和误差累积而表现不佳。虽然强化学习可以微调这些模型，但需要人工设计奖励函数，难以适应多样化任务。

Method: VLLR框架结合：(1) 使用LLM分解任务为可验证子任务，VLM估计进度作为外部奖励；(2) 基于策略自确定性的内部奖励。VLM初始化价值函数进行短暂预热，避免完整训练中的高推理成本；自确定性在整个PPO微调过程中提供每步内在指导。

Result: 在CHORES基准（移动操作和导航任务）上，VLLR相比预训练策略获得高达56%的绝对成功率提升，相比最先进的RL微调方法在分布内任务上提升5%，在分布外任务上提升10%，且无需人工奖励工程。消融研究表明VLM价值初始化主要提升任务完成效率，自确定性主要提升成功率（尤其在分布外任务）。

Conclusion: VLLR通过结合LLM/VLM的外部奖励和策略自确定性的内部奖励，有效解决了机器人基础策略微调中的奖励工程问题，在分布内和分布外任务上都取得了显著性能提升，为机器人策略的强化学习微调提供了实用框架。

Abstract: Existing robotic foundation policies are trained primarily via large-scale imitation learning. While such models demonstrate strong capabilities, they often struggle with long-horizon tasks due to distribution shift and error accumulation. While reinforcement learning (RL) can finetune these models, it cannot work well across diverse tasks without manual reward engineering. We propose VLLR, a dense reward framework combining (1) an extrinsic reward from Large Language Models (LLMs) and Vision-Language Models (VLMs) for task progress recognition, and (2) an intrinsic reward based on policy self-certainty. VLLR uses LLMs to decompose tasks into verifiable subtasks and then VLMs to estimate progress to initialize the value function for a brief warm-up phase, avoiding prohibitive inference cost during full training; and self-certainty provides per-step intrinsic guidance throughout PPO finetuning. Ablation studies reveal complementary benefits: VLM-based value initialization primarily improves task completion efficiency, while self-certainty primarily enhances success rates, particularly on out-of-distribution tasks. On the CHORES benchmark covering mobile manipulation and navigation, VLLR achieves up to 56% absolute success rate gains over the pretrained policy, up to 5% gains over state-of-the-art RL finetuning methods on in-distribution tasks, and up to $10\%$ gains on out-of-distribution tasks, all without manual reward engineering. Additional visualizations can be found in https://silongyong.github.io/vllr_project_page/

</details>


### [41] [MRReP: Mixed Reality-based Hand-drawn Reference Path Editing Interface for Mobile Robot Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00059)
*Takumi Taki,Masato Kobayashi,Yuki Uranishi*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: MRReP：基于混合现实的界面，允许用户通过手势在物理地面上绘制手绘参考路径，以指导移动机器人导航，相比传统2D界面提高了路径指定精度和可用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统路径规划器主要优化几何成本，对操作员显式指定路径的支持有限，而移动机器人在人机共享环境中需要反映人类空间意图的路径。

Method: 提出MRReP混合现实界面，用户通过手势在物理地面上绘制手绘参考路径，通过自定义的手绘参考路径规划器将用户指定的点序列转换为全局路径，集成到机器人导航栈中。

Result: 在与传统2D基线界面的受试者内实验中，MRReP提高了路径指定精度、可用性和感知工作量，同时在物理环境中实现了更稳定的路径指定。

Conclusion: 在混合现实中直接指定路径是将人类空间意图融入移动机器人导航的有效方法。

Abstract: Autonomous mobile robots operating in human-shared indoor environments often require paths that reflect human spatial intentions, such as avoiding interference with pedestrian flow or maintaining comfortable clearance. However, conventional path planners primarily optimize geometric costs and provide limited support for explicit route specification by human operators. This paper presents MRReP, a Mixed Reality-based interface that enables users to draw a Hand-drawn Reference Path (HRP) directly on the physical floor using hand gestures. The drawn HRP is integrated into the robot navigation stack through a custom Hand-drawn Reference Path Planner, which converts the user-specified point sequence into a global path for autonomous navigation. We evaluated MRReP in a within-subject experiment against a conventional 2D baseline interface. The results demonstrated that MRReP enhanced path specification accuracy, usability, and perceived workload, while enabling more stable path specification in the physical environment. These findings suggest that direct path specification in MR is an effective approach for incorporating human spatial intention into mobile robot navigation. Additional material is available at https://mertcookimg.github.io/mrrep

</details>


### [42] [Hierarchical Motion Planning and Control under Unknown Nonlinear Dynamics via Predicted Reachability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00320)
*Zhiquan Zhang,Melkior Ornik*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层规划-控制框架，在未知非线性动力学下实现在线运动规划，通过自适应状态空间分区学习分段仿射模型，结合图抽象和可达性验证进行探索与利用平衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在未知非线性动力学下进行自主运动规划需要同时学习系统特性并导航至目标。现有方法通常需要先验系统知识或难以保证可达性，特别是在欠驱动系统中。

Method: 1) 将状态空间划分为多面体，使用分段仿射模型近似未知非线性系统；2) 引入非均匀自适应分区策略，仅在任务相关区域细化分区；3) 将PWA系统抽象为有向加权图，边存在性通过可达控制理论和预测可达性条件增量验证；4) 已认证边使用可证明的到达时间界限加权，不确定边使用信息论权重引导探索；5) 引入松弛可达性条件扩展至欠驱动系统；6) 高层规划通过图搜索实现，低层使用仿射反馈控制器执行计划。

Result: 仿真结果表明该方法能有效平衡探索与利用，同时提供形式化的可达性保证。框架在欠驱动系统中也能有效工作，通过松弛可达性条件扩展了经典可达控制理论的应用范围。

Conclusion: 提出的分层规划-控制框架能够在有限先验知识下实现未知非线性系统的在线运动合成，通过自适应分区、图抽象和可达性验证的组合，提供了具有形式化保证的探索-利用平衡策略，特别适用于欠驱动系统。

Abstract: Autonomous motion planning under unknown nonlinear dynamics requires learning system properties while navigating toward a target. In this work, we develop a hierarchical planning-control framework that enables online motion synthesis with limited prior system knowledge. The state space is partitioned into polytopes and approximates the unknown nonlinear system using a piecewise-affine (PWA) model. The local affine models are identified once the agent enters the corresponding polytopes. To reduce computational complexity, we introduce a non-uniform adaptive state space partition strategy that refines the partition only in task-relevant regions. The resulting PWA system is abstracted into a directed weighted graph, whose edge existence is incrementally verified using reach control theory and predictive reachability conditions. Certified edges are weighted using provable time-to-reach bounds, while uncertain edges are assigned information-theoretic weights to guide exploration. The graph is updated online as new data becomes available, and high-level planning is performed by graph search, while low-level affine feedback controllers are synthesized to execute the plan. Furthermore, the conditions of classical reach control theory are often difficult to satisfy in underactuated settings. We therefore introduce relaxed reachability conditions to extend the framework to such systems. Simulations demonstrate effective exploration-exploitation trade-offs with formal reachability guarantees.

</details>


### [43] [Long-Horizon Geometry-Aware Navigation among Polytopes via MILP-MPC and Minkowski-Based CBFs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00162)
*Yi-Hsuan Chen,Salman Ghori,Ania Adil,Eric Feron,Calin Belta*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出分层规划控制框架，结合MILP-MPC长时域引导与基于精确符号距离的CBF安全滤波器，实现多面体机器人在多面体障碍环境中的几何感知安全导航。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 复杂非凸环境中考虑机器人动力学、控制限制和精确几何形状的自主导航仍然具有挑战性，需要同时满足长时域引导和几何感知安全保证。

Method: 分层框架：高层使用MILP-MPC将机器人建模为质点生成名义轨迹；低层使用基于Minkowski差空间精确符号距离的CBF作为安全滤波器，并扩展为高阶CBF，显式强制执行机器人几何约束。

Result: 在U形和迷宫环境中测试单/双积分器动力学，结果表明该架构缓解了纯反应式CBF导航的拓扑诱导局部最小值问题，实现了安全、实时、几何感知的导航。

Conclusion: 提出的分层规划控制框架成功结合了长时域轨迹规划与几何感知安全保证，为多面体机器人在复杂障碍环境中的导航提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in complex, non-convex environments remains challenging when robot dynamics, control limits, and exact robot geometry must all be taken into account. In this paper, we propose a hierarchical planning and control framework that bridges long-horizon guidance and geometry-aware safety guarantees for a polytopic robot navigating among polytopic obstacles. At the high level, Mixed-Integer Linear Programming (MILP) is embedded within a Model Predictive Control (MPC) framework to generate a nominal trajectory around polytopic obstacles while modeling the robot as a point mass for computational tractability. At the low level, we employ a control barrier function (CBF) based on the exact signed distance in the Minkowski-difference space as a safety filter to explicitly enforce the geometric constraints of the robot shape, and further extend its formulation to a high-order CBF (HOCBF). We demonstrate the proposed framework in U-shaped and maze-like environments under single- and double-integrator dynamics. The results show that the proposed architecture mitigates the topology-induced local-minimum behavior of purely reactive CBF-based navigation while enabling safe, real-time, geometry-aware navigation.

</details>

*Elaheh Sanoubari,Neil Fernandes,Keith Rebello,Alicia Pan,Andrew Houston,Kerstin Dautenhahn*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: REMind是一个创新的教育机器人介导角色扮演游戏，旨在支持儿童的反欺凌旁观者干预。通过让玩家观察社交机器人表演的欺凌场景、反思角色视角，并通过操控机器人化身来演练防御策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发一种创新的教育工具来支持儿童的反欺凌旁观者干预，利用机器人技术增强社会情感学习效果。

Method: 采用机器人介导应用戏剧（RMAD）作为教学框架，设计了一个包含观察欺凌场景、反思角色视角和通过操控机器人化身演练防御策略的角色扮演游戏。通过混合方法游戏测试研究，对18名9-10岁儿童进行了评估。

Result: 研究结果表明，REMind体验支持了与自我效能感、观点采择、理解防御结果和干预策略相关的关键学习目标。儿童在反欺凌旁观者干预方面获得了积极的学习效果。

Conclusion: 机器人介导应用戏剧（RMAD）作为一种新颖的教学框架，在支持社会情感学习方面具有潜力，特别是在反欺凌教育领域。

Abstract: This paper presents REMind, an innovative educational robot-mediated role-play game designed to support anti-bullying bystander intervention among children. REMind invites players to observe a bullying scenario enacted by social robots, reflect on the perspectives of the characters, and rehearse defending strategies by puppeteering a robotic avatar. We evaluated REMind through a mixed-methods play-testing study with 18 children aged 9--10. The findings suggest that the experience supported key learning goals related to self-efficacy, perspective-taking, understanding outcomes of defending, and intervention strategies. These results highlight the promise of Robot-Mediated Applied Drama (RMAD) as a novel pedagogical framework to support Social-Emotional Learning.

</details>


### [45] [DreamControl-v2: Simpler and Scalable Autonomous Humanoid Skills via Trainable Guided Diffusion Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00202)
*Sudarshan Harithas,Sangkyung Kwak,Pushkal Katara,Srujan Deolasee,Dvij Kalaria,Srinath Sridhar,Sai Vemprala,Ashish Kapoor,Jonathan Chung-Kuan Huang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种改进的人形机器人运动控制框架，通过直接在机器人运动空间训练引导扩散模型，整合人类和机器人数据集，实现更鲁棒的自主操作技能学习。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开发鲁棒的人形机器人自主操作技能仍然是一个开放问题。虽然强化学习在腿部运动控制中取得了成功，但将其应用于复杂的交互式操作任务面临长期规划挑战。现有的DreamControl方法利用人类运动扩散模型作为生成先验来指导强化学习策略，但存在局限性。

Method: 提出改进框架：1) 直接在机器人运动空间训练引导扩散模型；2) 将多样化的人类和机器人数据集聚合到统一的体现空间；3) 扩展参考轨迹生成以提高下游强化学习策略的鲁棒性；4) 建立更自动化的流程，减少人工干预需求。

Result: 方法在仿真和真实Unitree-G1机器人上进行了广泛验证。结果表明：1) 由于更大的训练数据混合，能够捕捉更广泛的技能范围；2) 建立了更自动化的流程，无需手动过滤干预；3) 扩展参考轨迹生成对实现鲁棒的下游强化学习策略至关重要。

Conclusion: 提出的改进框架通过直接在机器人运动空间训练扩散模型，整合多源数据，实现了更有效的人形机器人自主操作技能学习，为复杂交互任务提供了更鲁棒的解决方案。

Abstract: Developing robust autonomous loco-manipulation skills for humanoids remains an open problem in robotics. While RL has been applied successfully to legged locomotion, applying it to complex, interaction-rich manipulation tasks is harder given long-horizon planning challenges for manipulation. A recent approach along these lines is DreamControl, which addresses these issues by leveraging off-the-shelf human motion diffusion models as a generative prior to guide RL policies during training. In this paper, we investigate the impact of DreamControl's motion prior and propose an improved framework that trains a guided diffusion model directly in the humanoid robot's motion space, aggregating diverse human and robot datasets into a unified embodiment space. We demonstrate that our approach captures a wider range of skills due to the larger training data mixture and establishes a more automated pipeline by removing the need for manual filtering interventions. Furthermore, we show that scaling the generation of reference trajectories is important for achieving robust downstream RL policies. We validate our approach through extensive experiments in simulation and on a real Unitree-G1.

</details>


### [46] [Reachability-Aware Time Scaling for Path Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00439)
*Hossein Gholampour,Logan E. Beaver*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种用于平面双积分系统的路径跟踪方法，通过离线速度缩放和在线QP跟踪相结合，确保在有界速度和加速度约束下安全跟踪包含急转弯的路径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于采样的离线规划器生成的路径可能包含急转弯和高曲率区域，即使路径几何上无碰撞，在加速度限制下的一步可达性也变得至关重要。需要确保系统能够安全跟踪此类路径而不违反物理约束。

Method: 结合离线速度缩放和在线QP跟踪：1）离线阶段：沿路径拟合样条曲线，评估一步加速度裕度，更新标量速度缩放配置文件，使所需加速度保持在可用界限内；2）在线阶段：使用相同的纯追踪式、可达性引导的二次规划跟踪器跟踪缩放后的参考路径。

Result: 该方法能够确保平面双积分系统在有界速度和加速度约束下，安全跟踪包含急转弯和高曲率区域的路径，同时保持一步加速度在物理限制范围内。

Conclusion: 通过离线速度缩放与在线可达性引导QP跟踪相结合的方法，有效解决了采样规划器生成路径的跟踪问题，确保系统在物理约束下安全执行复杂几何路径。

Abstract: This paper studies tracking of collision-free waypoint paths produced by an offline planner for a planar double-integrator system with bounded speed and acceleration. Because sampling-based planners must route around obstacles, the resulting waypoint paths can contain sharp turns and high-curvature regions, so one-step reachability under acceleration limits becomes critical even when the path geometry is collision-free. We build on a pure-pursuit-style, reachability-guided quadratic-program (QP) tracker with a one-step acceleration margin. Offline, we evaluate this margin along a spline fitted to the waypoint path and update a scalar speed-scaling profile so that the required one-step acceleration remains below the available bound. Online, the same look-ahead tracking structure is used to track the scaled reference.

</details>


### [47] [VRUD: A Drone Dataset for Complex Vehicle-VRU Interactions within Mixed Traffic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01134)
*Ziyu Wang,Hongrui Kou,Cheng Wang,Ruochen Li,Hubert P. H. Shum,Amir Atapour-Abarghouei,Yuxin Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出基于无人机的高精度数据集构建方法，创建了车辆-弱势道路使用者交互数据集(VRUD)，专注于城市复杂混合交通环境中的边缘案例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有开源数据集主要关注结构化场景（如高速公路、规范交叉口），缺乏对混乱、非结构化城市环境的数据，特别是高密度弱势道路使用者(VRU)及其高度不确定交互行为的场景。

Method: 采用高效、高精度的无人机数据集构建方法，从深圳典型"城中村"收集数据，这些区域交通监管松散、遮挡严重。数据集包含4小时4K/30Hz录像，提取了11,479个VRU轨迹和1,939个车辆轨迹，并基于新型向量碰撞时间(VTTC)阈值提取了4,002个多智能体交互场景。

Result: 创建了VRUD数据集，其中VRU占所有交通参与者的约87%，远超现有基准。数据集包含标准OpenDRIVE高清地图支持，提供了宝贵的边缘案例资源，已开源供进一步研究。

Conclusion: VRUD数据集填补了复杂非结构化城市环境数据的空白，为提升自动驾驶系统在复杂城市混合交通环境中的安全性能提供了有价值的罕见边缘案例资源。

Abstract: The Operational Design Domain (ODD) of urbanoriented Level 4 (L4) autonomous driving, especially for autonomous robotaxis, confronts formidable challenges in complex urban mixed traffic environments. These challenges stem mainly from the high density of Vulnerable Road Users (VRUs) and their highly uncertain and unpredictable interaction behaviors. However, existing open-source datasets predominantly focus on structured scenarios such as highways or regulated intersections, leaving a critical gap in data representing chaotic, unstructured urban environments. To address this, this paper proposes an efficient, high-precision method for constructing drone-based datasets and establishes the Vehicle-Vulnerable Road User Interaction Dataset (VRUD), as illustrated in Figure 1. Distinct from prior works, VRUD is collected from typical "Urban Villages" in Shenzhen, characterized by loose traffic supervision and extreme occlusion. The dataset comprises 4 hours of 4K/30Hz recording, containing 11,479 VRU trajectories and 1,939 vehicle trajectories. A key characteristic of VRUD is its composition: VRUs account for about 87% of all traffic participants, significantly exceeding the proportions in existing benchmarks. Furthermore, unlike datasets that only provide raw trajectories, we extracted 4,002 multi-agent interaction scenarios based on a novel Vector Time to Collision (VTTC) threshold, supported by standard OpenDRIVE HD maps. This study provides valuable, rare edge-case resources for enhancing the safety performance of ADS in complex, unstructured urban environments. To facilitate further research, we have made the VRUD dataset open-source at: https://zzi4.github.io/VRUD/.

</details>


### [48] [Real Time Local Wind Inference for Robust Autonomous Navigation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00343)
*Spencer Folk*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 该研究提出了一种使空中机器人能够利用机载传感器和嵌入式飞行硬件实时推理周围风场的方法，核心创新是将地形测量与稀疏现场风测量融合以预测周围流场。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决两个基本问题：1) 在密集城市环境中地形数据是否足以准确预测风场；2) 学习到的风模型在运动规划中的实用性，特别是对能源效率和避障的影响。旨在让机器人在没有先验环境知识的情况下快速评估局部风况，推动空中机器人进入更具挑战性的环境。

Method: 融合深度学习、流体力学和最优控制工具，建立使用导航LiDAR进行局部风预测的框架，并将局部风模型先验整合到后退时域最优控制器中，研究局部风知识如何影响自主导航中的能源使用和鲁棒性。

Result: 通过多样化城市风场景的仿真演示评估了风预测器的预测能力，并量化了将局部风信息整合到运动规划中对自主城市导航的改进（碰撞率和能耗）。亚尺度自由飞行实验表明算法可在嵌入式飞行计算机上实时运行，具有足够带宽稳定控制小型空中机器人。

Conclusion: 该论文贡献了一种在未知风环境中进行局部风推理和运动规划的新范式，通过使机器人能够快速评估局部风况而无需先验环境知识，加速了空中机器人进入日益挑战性环境的进程。

Abstract: This thesis presents a solution that enables aerial robots to reason about surrounding wind flow fields in real time using on board sensors and embedded flight hardware. The core novelty of this research is the fusion of range measurements with sparse in situ wind measurements to predict surrounding flow fields. We aim to address two fundamental questions: first, the sufficiency of topographical data for accurate wind prediction in dense urban environments; and second, the utility of learned wind models for motion planning with an emphasis on energy efficiency and obstacle avoidance. Drawing on tools from deep learning, fluid mechanics, and optimal control, we establish a framework for local wind prediction using navigational LiDAR, and then incorporate local wind model priors into a receding-horizon optimal controller to study how local wind knowledge affects energy use and robustness during autonomous navigation. Through simulated demonstrations in diverse urban wind scenarios we evaluate the predictive capabilities of the wind predictor, and quantify improvements to autonomous urban navigation in terms of crash rates and energy consumption when local wind information is integrated into the motion planning. Sub-scale free flight experiments in an open-air wind tunnel demonstrate that these algorithms can run in real time on an embedded flight computer with sufficient bandwidth for stable control of a small aerial robot. Philosophically, this thesis contributes a new paradigm for localized wind inference and motion planning in unknown windy environments. By enabling robots to rapidly assess local wind conditions without prior environmental knowledge, this research accelerates the introduction of aerial robots into increasingly challenging environments.

</details>


### [49] [Go Big or Go Home: Simulating Mobbing Behavior with Braitenbergian Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00350)
*Elaheh Sanoubari*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 使用Webots平台模拟Braitenberg机器人的群体捕食者躲避与围攻行为，研究围攻呼叫范围和群体规模对围攻成功率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模仿动物群体通过围攻呼叫协调攻击捕食者的行为机制，探索人工生命中的行为选择模拟和自主智能体控制架构设计。

Method: 在Webots机器人仿真平台中模拟Braitenberg机器人群体，机器人遇到光源（代表无生命捕食者）时发出围攻呼叫，若能召集盟友则围攻，否则逃跑。研究围攻呼叫范围（无限、中等、低）和群体规模（10个vs 3个机器人）对围攻成功率的影响。

Result: 围攻呼叫范围和群体规模都对围攻成功率有显著影响。具体表现为：更大的呼叫范围和更大的群体规模能提高围攻成功率。

Conclusion: 该研究为人工生命行为选择模拟和自主智能体控制架构设计提供了重要启示，表明群体协调机制在对抗性环境中的有效性。

Abstract: We used the Webots robotics simulation platform to simulate a dyadic avoiding and mobbing predator behavior in a group of Braitenbergian robots. Mobbing is an antipredator adaptation used by some animals in which the individuals cooperatively attack or harass a predator to protect themselves. One way of coordinating a mobbing attack is using mobbing calls to summon other individuals of the mobbing species. We imitated this mechanism and simulated Braitenbergian robots that use mobbing calls when they face a light source (representing an inanimate predator) and mob it if they can summon allies, otherwise, they escape from it. We explore the effects of range of mobbing call (infinite range, mid-range and low-range) and the size of the robot group (ten robots vs three) on the overall success of mobbing. Our results suggest that both variables have significant impacts. This work has implications for simulations of action selection in artificial life and designing control architectures for autonomous agents.

</details>


### [50] [Collaborative Task and Path Planning for Heterogeneous Robotic Teams using Multi-Agent PPO](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01213)
*Matthias Rubio,Julia Richter,Hendrik Kolvenbach,Marco Hutter*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出基于多智能体近端策略优化（MAPPO）的协作规划策略，用于协调异构机器人团队解决复杂的目标分配与调度问题，相比传统规划方法具有更好的可扩展性和实时重规划能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高效的地外机器人探索需要多样化的机器人能力，通过团队协作可以分配任务给多个专门子系统。传统规划算法随着问题规模增大会出现组合爆炸，导致规划周期长、推理成本高，需要学习型方法将扩展性担忧从运行时转移到训练时，实现实时规划。

Method: 采用多智能体近端策略优化（MAPPO）作为协作规划策略，协调异构机器人团队解决复杂的目标分配和调度问题。该方法将学习型方法应用于多机器人协调，避免了传统规划算法的组合爆炸问题。

Result: 通过穷举搜索获得的单目标最优解进行基准测试，并在行星探索场景中评估了在线重规划能力。结果表明该方法能够有效协调异构机器人团队，实现实时规划。

Conclusion: 基于MAPPO的协作规划策略为解决机器人团队协调问题提供了可行方案，相比传统规划方法具有更好的可扩展性，是实现实时规划的关键一步，特别适用于行星探索等复杂场景。

Abstract: Efficient robotic extraterrestrial exploration requires robots with diverse capabilities, ranging from scientific measurement tools to advanced locomotion. A robotic team enables the distribution of tasks over multiple specialized subsystems, each providing specific expertise to complete the mission. The central challenge lies in efficiently coordinating the team to maximize utilization and the extraction of scientific value. Classical planning algorithms scale poorly with problem size, leading to long planning cycles and high inference costs due to the combinatorial growth of possible robot-target allocations and possible trajectories. Learning-based methods are a viable alternative that move the scaling concern from runtime to training time, setting a critical step towards achieving real-time planning. In this work, we present a collaborative planning strategy based on Multi-Agent Proximal Policy Optimization (MAPPO) to coordinate a team of heterogeneous robots to solve a complex target allocation and scheduling problem. We benchmark our approach against single-objective optimal solutions obtained through exhaustive search and evaluate its ability to perform online replanning in the context of a planetary exploration scenario.

</details>


### [51] [A Dual-Stream Transformer Architecture for Illumination-Invariant TIR-LiDAR Person Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00363)
*Yuki Minase,Kanji Tanaka*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新颖的热红外-深度（TIR-D）跟踪架构，用于解决RGB-D跟踪在极端光照条件下性能下降的问题，通过知识迁移策略和差异学习率实现了全天候鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-D跟踪在挑战性光照条件（如完全黑暗或强烈背光）下性能严重下降，而自主移动机器人需要全天候的鲁棒人员跟踪能力。热红外（TIR）传感器在极端光照条件下表现稳定，但缺乏标注的多模态数据集限制了TIR-D跟踪的发展。

Method: 提出TIR-D跟踪架构，利用SLAM机器人的标准传感器套件（LiDAR和TIR相机）。采用序列知识迁移策略，将大规模热红外训练模型的结构先验迁移到TIR-D领域。使用"细粒度差异学习率策略"，在保持预训练特征提取能力的同时，快速适应几何深度线索。

Result: 实验结果表明，提出的TIR-D跟踪器实现了卓越性能，平均重叠率（AO）为0.700，成功率（SR）为58.7%，显著优于传统的RGB迁移和单模态基线方法。

Conclusion: 该方法为全天候机器人应用中鲁棒的人员跟随提供了实用且资源高效的解决方案，通过有效结合热红外和深度信息，克服了极端光照条件下的跟踪挑战。

Abstract: Robust person tracking is a critical capability for autonomous mobile robots operating in diverse and unpredictable environments. While RGB-D tracking has shown high precision, its performance severely degrades under challenging illumination conditions, such as total darkness or intense backlighting. To achieve all-weather robustness, this paper proposes a novel Thermal-Infrared and Depth (TIR-D) tracking architecture that leverages the standard sensor suite of SLAM-capable robots, namely LiDAR and TIR cameras. A major challenge in TIR-D tracking is the scarcity of annotated multi-modal datasets. To address this, we introduce a sequential knowledge transfer strategy that evolves structural priors from a large-scale thermal-trained model into the TIR-D domain. By employing a differential learning rate strategy -- referred to as ``Fine-grained Differential Learning Rate Strategy'' -- we effectively preserve pre-trained feature extraction capabilities while enabling rapid adaptation to geometric depth cues. Experimental results demonstrate that our proposed TIR-D tracker achieves superior performance, with an Average Overlap (AO) of 0.700 and a Success Rate (SR) of 58.7\%, significantly outperforming conventional RGB-transfer and single-modality baselines. Our approach provides a practical and resource-efficient solution for robust human-following in all-weather robotics applications.

</details>


### [52] [Sampling-based Task and Kinodynamic Motion Planning under Semantic Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00401)
*Qi Heng Ho,Zachary N. Sunberg,Morteza Lahijanian*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种在不确定环境中集成任务和运动规划的方法，处理非线性动力学机器人在部分可观测环境中的LTLf规范任务，通过混合系统建模和随时算法实现


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决在不确定环境中机器人同时进行任务规划和运动规划的问题，特别是处理环境语义标签的不确定性和非线性动力学约束

Method: 将问题建模为部分可观测随机混合系统，提出利用混合系统结构的随时算法，结合决策技术和基于采样的运动规划

Result: 证明了算法的正确性和渐近最优性，实验显示在不确定环境中优于基准方法

Conclusion: 提出的方法能有效处理不确定环境中的集成任务和运动规划问题，为非线性动力学机器人在部分可观测环境中执行复杂任务提供了解决方案

Abstract: This paper tackles the problem of integrated task and kinodynamic motion planning in uncertain environments. We consider a robot with nonlinear dynamics tasked with a Linear Temporal Logic over finite traces ($\ltlf$) specification operating in a partially observable environment. Specifically, the uncertainty is in the semantic labels of the environment. We show how the problem can be modeled as a Partially Observable Stochastic Hybrid System that captures the robot dynamics, $\ltlf$ task, and uncertainty in the environment state variables. We propose an anytime algorithm that takes advantage of the structure of the hybrid system, and combines the effectiveness of decision-making techniques and sampling-based motion planning. We prove the soundness and asymptotic optimality of the algorithm. Results show the efficacy of our algorithm in uncertain environments, and that it consistently outperforms baseline methods.

</details>


### [53] [Learning Humanoid Navigation from Human Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00416)
*Weizhuo Wang,Yanjie Ze,C. Karen Liu,Monroe Kennedy*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: EgoNav是一个仅用5小时人类行走数据训练的人形机器人导航系统，无需机器人数据或微调，能在未见环境中实现零样本部署


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决人形机器人在多样化未见环境中导航的挑战，通过仅使用人类行走数据学习导航策略，避免收集昂贵的机器人数据

Method: 使用扩散模型预测未来轨迹分布，条件包括：过去轨迹、融合颜色/深度/语义的360度视觉记忆、DINOv3视频特征；采用混合采样方案实现10步去噪的实时推理，以及滚动时域控制器从预测分布中选择路径

Result: 离线评估中在碰撞避免和多模态覆盖方面优于基线；在Unitree G1人形机器人上零样本部署，在未见室内外环境中成功导航；自然涌现出等待开门、绕开人群、避开玻璃墙等行为

Conclusion: 仅用少量人类数据即可学习有效的导航策略，无需机器人数据或微调；系统能够泛化到多样化未见环境，并涌现出复杂导航行为；将发布数据集和训练模型

Abstract: We present EgoNav, a system that enables a humanoid robot to traverse diverse, unseen environments by learning entirely from 5 hours of human walking data, with no robot data or finetuning. A diffusion model predicts distributions of plausible future trajectories conditioned on past trajectory, a 360 deg visual memory fusing color, depth, and semantics, and video features from a frozen DINOv3 backbone that capture appearance cues invisible to depth sensors. A hybrid sampling scheme achieves real-time inference in 10 denoising steps, and a receding-horizon controller selects paths from the predicted distribution. We validate EgoNav through offline evaluations, where it outperforms baselines in collision avoidance and multi-modal coverage, and through zero-shot deployment on a Unitree G1 humanoid across unseen indoor and outdoor environments. Behaviors such as waiting for doors to open, navigating around crowds, and avoiding glass walls emerge naturally from the learned prior. We will release the dataset and trained models. Our website: https://egonav.weizhuowang.com

</details>


### [54] [Certificate-Driven Closed-Loop Multi-Agent Path Finding with Inheritable Factorization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00428)
*Jiarui Li,Runyu Zhang,Gioele Zardini*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: CDCBS通过引入证书轨迹和舰队预算机制改进ACCBS，在密集场景中提供更一致的解质量，同时通过预算限制分解减少有效组规模


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有闭环MAPF算法（如ACCBS）在有限计算预算下可能产生短视的主动前缀，难以保持全局保证和利用组合结构，特别是在密集实例中

Method: 提出证书轨迹及其关联的舰队预算作为过滤闭环更新的通用机制，证书提供无冲突回退计划和剩余成本的单调上界，接受证书改进更新确保完备性；预算信息诱导预算限制分解，实现跨时间步的全局可继承分解

Result: 在基准地图上的实验表明，CDCBS比ACCBS获得更一致的解质量（尤其在密集设置中），同时提出的分解机制减少了有效组规模

Conclusion: 证书轨迹和预算机制为闭环MAPF提供了有效的全局保证和分解框架，CDCBS实例化展示了该方法在密集场景中的优越性能

Abstract: Multi-agent coordination in automated warehouses and logistics is commonly modeled as the Multi-Agent Path Finding (MAPF) problem. Closed-loop MAPF algorithms improve scalability by planning only the next movement and replanning online, but this finite-horizon viewpoint can be shortsighted and makes it difficult to preserve global guarantees and exploit compositional structure. This issue is especially visible in Anytime Closed-Loop Conflict-Based Search (ACCBS), which applies Conflict-Based Search (CBS) over dynamically extended finite horizons but, under finite computational budgets, may terminate with short active prefixes in dense instances.
  We introduce certificate trajectories and their associated fleet budget as a general mechanism for filtering closed-loop updates. A certificate provides a conflict-free fallback plan and a monotone upper bound on the remaining cost; accepting only certificate-improving updates yields completeness. The same budget information induces a budget-limited factorization that enables global, inheritable decomposition across timesteps. Instantiating the framework on ACCBS yields Certificate-Driven Conflict-Based Search (CDCBS). Experiments on benchmark maps show that CDCBS achieves more consistent solution quality than ACCBS, particularly in dense settings, while the proposed factorization reduces effective group size.

</details>


### [55] [Multi-Camera View Scaling for Data-Efficient Robot Imitation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00557)
*Yichen Xie,Yixiao Wang,Shuqi Zhao,Cheng-En Wu,Masayoshi Tomizuka,Jianwen Xie,Hao-Shu Fang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 通过多视角相机采集演示数据，无需额外人工标注即可增强模仿学习的泛化能力


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人模仿学习的泛化能力受限于专家演示的多样性，而收集多样化环境下的演示成本高且困难

Method: 使用同步多视角相机从单条专家轨迹生成伪演示，增强训练分布；分析不同动作空间与视角扩展的交互；提出相机空间表示和多视角动作聚合方法

Result: 在仿真和真实机器人操作任务中，相比单视角基线显著提升了数据效率和泛化能力

Conclusion: 扩展相机视角为模仿学习提供了实用且可扩展的解决方案，仅需最小额外硬件设置，并能与现有模仿学习算法无缝集成

Abstract: The generalization ability of imitation learning policies for robotic manipulation is fundamentally constrained by the diversity of expert demonstrations, while collecting demonstrations across varied environments is costly and difficult in practice. In this paper, we propose a practical framework that exploits inherent scene diversity without additional human effort by scaling camera views during demonstration collection. Instead of acquiring more trajectories, multiple synchronized camera perspectives are used to generate pseudo-demonstrations from each expert trajectory, which enriches the training distribution and improves viewpoint invariance in visual representations. We analyze how different action spaces interact with view scaling and show that camera-space representations further enhance diversity. In addition, we introduce a multiview action aggregation method that allows single-view policies to benefit from multiple cameras during deployment. Extensive experiments in simulation and real-world manipulation tasks demonstrate significant gains in data efficiency and generalization compared to single-view baselines. Our results suggest that scaling camera views provides a practical and scalable solution for imitation learning, which requires minimal additional hardware setup and integrates seamlessly with existing imitation learning algorithms. The website of our project is https://yichen928.github.io/robot_multiview.

</details>


### [56] [StretchBot: A Neuro-Symbolic Framework for Adaptive Guidance with Assistive Robots](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00628)
*Luca Vogelgesang,Ahmed Mehdi Soltani,Mohammadhossein Khojasteh,Xinrui Zu,Stefano De Giorgis,Madalina Croitoru,Filip Ilievski*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StretchBot：结合多模态感知与知识图谱增强大语言模型推理的混合神经符号机器人教练，用于自适应辅助拉伸指导


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有辅助机器人系统大多采用脚本化方式，无法适应用户状态、环境上下文和交互动态的变化，限制了其在家庭和医疗场景中支持身体健康的潜力

Method: 提出StretchBot混合神经符号系统，结合多模态感知与知识图谱增强的大语言模型推理，在保持结构化常规的同时支持上下文感知的调整

Result: 探索性试点研究（3名参与者）表明，自适应条件在感知适应性和上下文相关性方面优于脚本化指导，而脚本化指导在流畅性和可预测性方面仍具竞争力

Conclusion: 结构化可操作知识有助于在具身辅助交互中基于语言模型进行自适应调整，但需要更大规模的纵向研究来评估鲁棒性、泛化能力和长期用户体验

Abstract: Assistive robots have growing potential to support physical wellbeing in home and healthcare settings, for example, by guiding users through stretching or rehabilitation routines. However, existing systems remain largely scripted, which limits their ability to adapt to user state, environmental context, and interaction dynamics. In this work, we present StretchBot, a hybrid neuro-symbolic robotic coach for adaptive assistive guidance. The system combines multimodal perception with knowledge-graph-grounded large language model reasoning to support context-aware adjustments during short stretching sessions while maintaining a structured routine. To complement the system description, we report an exploratory pilot comparison between scripted and adaptive guidance with three participants. The pilot findings suggest that the adaptive condition improved perceived adaptability and contextual relevance, while scripted guidance remained competitive in smoothness and predictability. These results provide preliminary evidence that structured actionable knowledge can help ground language-model-based adaptation in embodied assistive interaction, while also highlighting the need for larger, longitudinal studies to evaluate robustness, generalizability, and long-term user experience.

</details>


### [57] [LiPS: Lightweight Panoptic Segmentation for Resource-Constrained Robotics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00634)
*Calvin Galagain,Martyna Poreba,François Goulette,Cyrill Stachniss*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: LiPS：一种轻量级全景分割方法，在保持查询式解码的同时简化特征提取与融合路径，在计算资源受限的移动机器人平台上实现高效全景分割


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前最先进的全景分割模型复杂度高，不适合部署在移动机器人等资源受限平台上，需要一种既能保持高性能又能大幅降低计算需求的解决方案

Method: 提出LiPS方法，保留基于查询的解码机制，同时引入简化的特征提取和融合路径，采用轻量级设计实现高效计算的全景分割

Result: 在标准基准测试中，LiPS达到与更重基线模型相当的精度，同时提供高达4.5倍的吞吐量（帧率），计算量减少近6.8倍

Conclusion: LiPS的高效性使其成为现代全景分割模型与现实世界机器人应用之间的重要桥梁，特别适合资源受限的移动机器人平台部署

Abstract: Panoptic segmentation is a key enabler for robotic perception, as it unifies semantic understanding with object-level reasoning. However, the increasing complexity of state-of-the-art models makes them unsuitable for deployment on resource-constrained platforms such as mobile robots. We propose a novel approach called LiPS that addresses the challenge of efficient-to-compute panoptic segmentation with a lightweight design that retains query-based decoding while introducing a streamlined feature extraction and fusion pathway. It aims at providing a strong panoptic segmentation performance while substantially lowering the computational demands. Evaluations on standard benchmarks demonstrate that LiPS attains accuracy comparable to much heavier baselines, while providing up to 4.5 higher throughput, measured in frames per second, and requiring nearly 6.8 times fewer computations. This efficiency makes LiPS a highly relevant bridge between modern panoptic models and real-world robotic applications.

</details>


### [58] [SoftHand Model-W: A 3D-Printed, Anthropomorphic, Underactuated Robot Hand with Integrated Wrist and Carpal Tunnel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00738)
*Dhillon B. Merritt,Christopher J. Ford,Haoran Li,Malia Smith,Zhixing Chen,Efi Psomopoulou,Nathan F. Lepora*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SoftHand Model-W：一款基于Pisa/IIT SoftHand的3D打印欠驱动仿人机器人手，集成拮抗肌腱机构和2自由度肌腱驱动腕部，通过腕部主动运动减少手臂补偿动作，提高操作效率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软体机器人手缺乏腕部自由度，导致在操作任务中需要大量手臂补偿运动，限制了操作效率和自然性。需要开发集成腕部自由度的紧凑型机器人手，实现更接近人类的灵巧操作能力。

Method: 开发了SoftHand Model-W：基于Pisa/IIT SoftHand的3D打印欠驱动仿人机器人手，集成拮抗肌腱机构（4个驱动自由度：5指主动屈伸，腕部主动屈伸和桡尺偏转）。采用腕管式肌腱布线实现远程电机放置于前臂，减少远端惯性。系统安装在6轴机器人手臂上，通过立方体堆叠和平面圆盘旋转任务测试腕部协调效果。

Result: 对比有无腕部驱动的任务完成时间、手臂关节行程和配置变化：腕部驱动显著减少手臂补偿和重配置运动，加快任务完成时间。腕部使原本不可能的拾放操作成为可能。腕部自由度是实现类人灵巧操作的关键。

Conclusion: SoftHand Model-W展示了近端自由度对于实现真实世界机器人应用中多功能、类人操作的重要性。紧凑设计使其适用于研究和辅助场景部署，腕部驱动显著提高操作效率和能力。

Abstract: This paper presents the SoftHand Model-W: a 3D-printed, underactuated, anthropomorphic robot hand based on the Pisa/IIT SoftHand, with an integrated antagonistic tendon mechanism and 2 degree-of-freedom tendon-driven wrist. These four degrees-of-acuation provide active flexion and extension to the five fingers, and active flexion/extension and radial/ulnar deviation of the palm through the wrist, while preserving the synergistic and self-adaptive features of such SoftHands. A carpal tunnel-inspired tendon routing allows remote motor placement in the forearm, reducing distal inertia and maintaining a compact form factor. The SoftHand-W is mounted on a 6-axis robot arm and tested with two reorientation tasks requiring coordination between the hand and arm's pose: cube stacking and in-plane disc rotation. Results comparing task time, arm joint travel, and configuration changes with and without wrist actuation show that adding the wrist reduces compensatory and reconfiguration movements of the arm for a quicker task-completion time. Moreover, the wrist enables pick-and-place operations that would be impossible otherwise. Overall, the SoftHand Model-W demonstrates how proximal degrees of freedom are key to achieving versatile, human-like manipulation in real world robotic applications, with a compact design enabling deployment in research and assistive settings.

</details>


### [59] [An Integrated Soft Robotic System for Measuring Vital Signs in Search and Rescue Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00971)
*Jorge Francisco García-Samartín,Christyan Cruz Ulloa,Andrés Sánchez-Silva,Jaime del Cerro,Antonio Barrientos*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 设计用于灾后救援的软体抓取器，集成到移动机器人系统中，可测量受害者脉搏和血压，验证了系统的准确性和适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然机器人在搜救行动中广泛应用，受害评估领域也有显著进展，但灾后场景中的心率测量和血压评估仍存在未解决的问题，需要开发专门设备。

Method: 设计软体抓取器集成到移动机器人系统，抓取器可包裹受害者手臂并像血压计一样充气，采用专门便携系统，使用不同信号处理算法提取生理参数。

Result: 系统实现脉搏偏差4bpm，收缩压和舒张压偏差约5mmHg，误差项同方差性验证证明系统能准确确定心率和血压，灾后测试验证了系统适应性、测量速度和安全性。

Conclusion: 开发的软体抓取器移动机器人系统能够准确测量灾后受害者的脉搏和血压，适合搜救行动，具备适应不同受害者位置、测量快速且对受害者安全的特点。

Abstract: Robots are frequently utilized in search-and-rescue operations. In recent years, significant advancements have been made in the field of victim assessment. However, there are still open issues regarding heart rate measurement, and no studies have been found that assess pressure in post-disaster scenarios. This work designs a soft gripper and integrates it into a mobile robotic system, thereby creating a device capable of measuring the pulse and blood pressure of victims in post-disaster environments. The gripper is designed to envelop the victim's arm and inflate like a sphygmomanometer, facilitated by a specialized portability system. The utilization of different signal processing algorithms has enabled the attainment of a pulse bias of \qty{4}{\bpm} and a bias of approximately \qty{5}{\mmHg} for systolic and diastolic pressures. The findings, in conjunction with the other statistical data and the validation of homoscedasticity in the error terms, prove the system's capacity to accurately determine heart rate and blood pressure, thereby rendering it suitable for search and rescue operations. Finally, a post-disaster has been employed as a test to validate the functionality of the entire system and to demonstrate its capacity to adapt to various victim positions, its measurement speed, and its safety for victims.

</details>


### [60] [Stein Variational Uncertainty-Adaptive Model Predictive Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01034)
*Hrishikesh Sathyanarayan,Ian Abraham*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出一种基于Stein变分分布鲁棒控制的非线性动力系统控制器，用于处理潜在参数不确定性，通过任务相关不确定性分布的粒子近似实现性能-鲁棒性权衡优化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统分布鲁棒优化（DRO）采用保守的最坏情况模糊集优化，可能牺牲名义性能。需要一种方法能够专注于对闭环性能影响最大的参数敏感性，同时避免对不确定性模型的限制性参数假设。

Method: 将最优控制与Stein变分推断耦合，使用确定性粒子基近似任务相关不确定性分布，形成单一决策理论框架。该方法保持计算并行性，通过围绕对任务目标最关键的相关不确定性塑造控制律。

Result: 在代表性控制问题上实证表明，相比名义控制器、集成方法和经典分布鲁棒基线，该方法实现了改进的性能-鲁棒性权衡。

Conclusion: 该方法在单一决策理论框架中调和了鲁棒控制与变分推断，适用于具有参数不确定性的广泛控制系统类别，通过关注关键不确定性而非最坏情况设计实现鲁棒性。

Abstract: We propose a Stein variational distributionally robust controller for nonlinear dynamical systems with latent parametric uncertainty. The method is an alternative to conservative worst-case ambiguity-set optimization with a deterministic particle-based approximation of a task-dependent uncertainty distribution, enabling the controller to concentrate on parameter sensitivities that most strongly affect closed-loop performance. Our method yields a controller that is robust to latent parameter uncertainty by coupling optimal control with Stein variational inference, and avoiding restrictive parametric assumptions on the uncertainty model while preserving computational parallelism. In contrast to classical DRO, which can sacrifice nominal performance through worst-case design, we find our approach achieves robustness by shaping the control law around relevant uncertainty that are most critical to the task objective. The proposed framework therefore reconciles robust control and variational inference in a single decision-theoretic formulation for broad classes of control systems with parameter uncertainty. We demonstrate our approach on representative control problems that empirically illustrate improved performance-robustness tradeoffs over nominal, ensemble, and classical distributionally robust baselines.

</details>


### [61] [Functional Force-Aware Retargeting from Virtual Human Demos to Soft Robot Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01224)
*Uksang Yoo,Mengjia Zhu,Evan Pezent,Jom Preechayasomboon,Jean Oh,Jeffrey Ichnowski,Amir Memar,Ben Abbatematteo,Homanga Bharadhwaj,Ashish Deshpande,Harsha Prahlad*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: SoftAct框架通过显式建模接触力，利用VR采集包含接触力的丰富人类演示，采用两阶段力感知重定向算法，使软体机器人手能够执行类人操作技能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统方法仅重定向人类关节轨迹，无法有效处理软体机器人手与人类手之间的极端形态差异和非线性柔顺特性，需要显式建模接触几何和力分布以实现有效的技能迁移。

Method: 1) 使用VR系统采集包含手部运动学、物体运动、密集接触斑块和详细接触力信息的人类演示；2) 两阶段力感知重定向算法：第一阶段将演示接触力分配给人类手指并按比例分配给机器人手指，建立力平衡映射；第二阶段结合末端执行器姿态跟踪与测地加权接触细化进行在线重定向。

Result: 在接触丰富的操作任务上评估，使用定制非拟人化气动软体机器人手。SoftAct控制器相比运动学和基于学习的基线方法，指尖轨迹跟踪RMSE降低达55%，跟踪方差降低达69%。在策略层面，在零样本真实世界部署和仿真中均获得更高成功率。

Conclusion: 显式建模接触几何和力分布对于软体机器人手的有效技能迁移至关重要，仅通过运动学模仿无法恢复这些信息。SoftAct框架能够使软体机器人手复现人类演示的功能意图，同时自然适应极端形态差异和非线性柔顺特性。

Abstract: We introduce SoftAct, a framework for teaching soft robot hands to perform human-like manipulation skills by explicitly reasoning about contact forces. Leveraging immersive virtual reality, our system captures rich human demonstrations, including hand kinematics, object motion, dense contact patches, and detailed contact force information. Unlike conventional approaches that retarget human joint trajectories, SoftAct employs a two-stage, force-aware retargeting algorithm. The first stage attributes demonstrated contact forces to individual human fingers and allocates robot fingers proportionally, establishing a force-balanced mapping between human and robot hands. The second stage performs online retargeting by combining baseline end-effector pose tracking with geodesic-weighted contact refinements, using contact geometry and force magnitude to adjust robot fingertip targets in real time. This formulation enables soft robotic hands to reproduce the functional intent of human demonstrations while naturally accommodating extreme embodiment mismatch and nonlinear compliance. We evaluate SoftAct on a suite of contact-rich manipulation tasks using a custom non-anthropomorphic pneumatic soft robot hand. SoftAct's controller reduces fingertip trajectory tracking RMSE by up to 55 percent and reduces tracking variance by up to 69 percent compared to kinematic and learning-based baselines. At the policy level, SoftAct achieves consistently higher success in zero-shot real-world deployment and in simulation. These results demonstrate that explicitly modeling contact geometry and force distribution is essential for effective skill transfer to soft robotic hands, and cannot be recovered through kinematic imitation alone. Project videos and additional details are available at https://soft-act.github.io/.

</details>

### [62] [Transformers for Program Termination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00039)
*Yoav Alon,Cristina David*

Main category: cs.PL

TL;DR: 基于Transformer的集成学习框架用于程序终止性分析，通过不平衡感知损失函数和类别感知采样技术提升性能，优于单个Transformer、大型语言模型和图方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 程序终止性分析是程序分析的核心挑战，对正确性、验证和安全性有直接影响。传统方法面临非终止示例极度稀缺的问题，需要探索Transformer架构是否能直接从源代码识别终止模式

Method: 设计了紧凑Transformer编码器的集成框架，系统性地使用不平衡感知损失函数套件和类别感知采样技术进行训练。通过组合使用不同损失函数训练的模型，构建集成系统，并引入语法感知的属性归因管道来解释终止估计

Result: 集成方法显著优于任何单个Transformer，超越了强大的现成大型语言模型和图基方法，在程序终止性识别任务上取得了更强的性能

Conclusion: Transformer架构能够从源代码识别终止模式，通过集成学习和专门设计的不平衡处理技术可以显著提升性能，同时语法感知的解释管道增强了模型的可解释性

Abstract: Determining whether a program terminates is a core challenge in program analysis with direct implications for correctness, verification, and security. We investigate whether transformer architectures can recognise termination patterns directly from source code and how their strengths can be amplified through ensembles. To overcome the extreme scarcity of non-terminating examples, we design an ensemble framework of compact transformer encoders, systematically trained with a suite of imbalance-aware loss functions and class-aware sampling techniques. By combining models trained with distinct loss functions, our ensembles achieve substantially stronger performance than any single transformer, outperforming both powerful off-the-shelf LLMs and graph-based methods. Finally, we introduce an attribution pipeline that produces syntax-aware explanations for the termination estimation.

</details>

### [63] [When Labels Are Scarce: A Systematic Mapping of Label-Efficient Code Vulnerability Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00079)
*Noor Khalal,Chakib Fettal,Lazhar Labiod,Mohamed Nadif*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 关于机器学习代码漏洞检测的综述，系统梳理了减少对人工标注依赖的方法，提出了五种范式家族，建立了设计地图和决策指南


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码漏洞检测中可靠的人工标注成本高昂、噪声大且在不同项目、语言和CWE类型间分布不均，需要减少对人工标注依赖的方法

Method: 系统综述方法：1) 识别并综合五种减少标注依赖的范式家族；2) 将机制与token、graph、hybrid和knowledge-based表示连接；3) 整合限制比较的评估和报告轴；4) 提出设计地图和约束优先的决策指南

Result: 1) 建立了五种范式家族的系统分类；2) 连接了机制与代码表示方法；3) 明确了评估和报告的关键轴；4) 提供了实用的设计地图和决策指南来权衡不同方法的优缺点和失败模式

Conclusion: 该综述为机器学习代码漏洞检测领域提供了系统框架，帮助研究者和实践者在有限标注预算下选择合适方法，通过设计地图和决策指南指导实际应用中的方法选择

Abstract: Machine-learning-based code vulnerability detection (CVD) has progressed rapidly, from deep program representations to pretrained code models and LLM-centered pipelines. Yet dependable vulnerability labeling remains expensive, noisy, and uneven across projects, languages, and CWE types, motivating approaches that reduce reliance on human labeling. This survey maps these approaches, synthesizing five paradigm families and the mechanisms they use. It connects mechanisms to token, graph, hybrid, and knowledgebased representations, and consolidates evaluation and reporting axes that limit comparison (label-budget specification, compute/cost assumptions, leakage, and granularity mismatches). A Design Map and constraintfirst Decision Guide distill trade-offs and failure modes for practical method selection.

</details>


### [64] [NFC based inventory control system for secure and efficient communication](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00181)
*Razi Iqbal,Awais Ahmad,Asfandyar Gillani*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出使用近场通信(NFC)替代传统条形码的库存控制系统方案，通过NFC标签实现电子产品商店的库存管理和收据生成


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统条形码库存控制系统存在安全性低、易损坏、在不同产品类型上不可靠等问题，而NFC具有高安全性、易用性和效率优势

Method: 为电子产品商店设计原型系统，每个产品粘贴被动NFC标签，顾客购买时通过NFC设备（智能手机或读卡器）与标签通信生成收据

Result: 未在摘要中明确说明具体实验结果，但提出了基于NFC的库存控制系统原型设计方案

Conclusion: NFC技术相比传统条形码更适合库存控制系统，能提供更高的安全性、可靠性和效率

Abstract: This paper brings up this idea of using Near Field Communication (NFC) for inventory control system instead of using traditional barcodes. NFC because of its high security, ease of use and efficiency can be very suitable for systems like inventory control. In traditional inventory control systems, each product has a barcode pasted on it, which is vulnerable to attacks as barcodes are open and have no security. Furthermore, barcodes are prone to damages and can be unreliable when pasted on different types of products e.g. hot and frozen products, circular shaped products and irregular shaped products like clothes etc. NFC on the other hand is very efficient, secure and reliable when it comes to short-range wireless communication. In this paper we will present our prototype for the inventory control system of an electronic store in which each product has a passive NFC tag pasted to it. When a customer buys a product the receipt of the product is generated using NFC between the NFC passive tag on the product and NFC enabled device (e.g. smart phone or reader) at the cash counter.

</details>


### [65] [On the Necessity of Pre-agreed Secrets for Thwarting Last-minute Coercion: Vulnerabilities and Lessons From the Loki E-voting Protocol](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00188)
*Jingxin Qiao,Myrto Arapinis,Thomas Zacharias*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文分析了Loki电子投票协议的两个安全漏洞：暴力破解攻击破坏规避策略完整性，以及强制弃权攻击可检测受害者是否遵守不投票指令。研究揭示了一个基本困境：没有预先约定的秘密凭证就无法防止最后一刻胁迫，并展示了恢复使用预先约定凭证可修复漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Loki协议旨在解决JCJ协议的两个可用性问题：1) 选民需要存储和隐藏预先约定的凭证；2) 选民在被胁迫时能够令人信服地说谎。然而，本文发现Loki协议存在安全漏洞，需要分析这些漏洞的根本原因并提出解决方案。

Method: 通过安全分析识别Loki协议的两个具体漏洞：1) 暴力破解攻击，允许攻击者以受害者名义投票并绕过规避策略；2) 强制弃权攻击，允许攻击者检测受害者是否遵守不投票指令。进一步将完整性攻击泛化，揭示没有预先约定秘密凭证就无法防止最后一刻胁迫的基本困境。

Result: 发现Loki协议存在两个关键安全漏洞：1) 暴力破解攻击使规避策略失效；2) 强制弃权攻击破坏投票隐私。研究证明，恢复使用预先约定的秘密凭证可以修复这些漏洞，但需要在计票效率和更强的信任假设之间进行权衡。

Conclusion: 电子投票协议面临一个基本困境：没有预先约定的秘密凭证就无法有效防止最后一刻胁迫。Loki协议试图通过重新投票机制解决可用性问题，但牺牲了安全性。恢复使用预先约定的凭证可以修复漏洞，但这需要在可用性和安全性之间做出权衡。

Abstract: Coercion-resistance (CR) is a crucial security property in e-voting systems. It ensures that an attacker cannot compel a voter to vote in a specific way by using threats or rewards. The Loki e-voting protocol, proposed by Giustolisi \emph{et al.} at IEEE S\&P (2024), introduces a novel design that mitigates last-minute coercion through a re-voting mechanism. It also aims to address the usability issues of the seminal JCJ e-voting protocol, specifically: i) the requirement that voters can store and hide pre-agreed credentials, and ii) the ability of voters to convincingly lie while being coerced.
  In this work, we identify two vulnerabilities in Loki. The first is a brute-force attack that compromises the integrity of the evasion strategy. Specifically, this attack allows an adversary to cast a ballot on behalf of their victim in a way that the evasion strategy cannot defend against, rendering it ineffective. The second vulnerability is a forced abstention attack, which allows an adversary to detect when their victim has complied with their instruction not to vote. We generalise the integrity attack to reveal a fundamental dilemma: without pre-agreed secret credentials, it is not possible to prevent last-minute coercion. Finally, we show how reverting to pre-agreed secret credentials fixes the aforementioned vulnerabilities and discuss the trade-off between tallying efficiency and stronger trust assumptions.

</details>


### [66] [RAGShield: Provenance-Verified Defense-in-Depth Against Knowledge Base Poisoning in Government Retrieval-Augmented Generation Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00387)
*KrishnaSaiReddy Patil*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: RAGShield：一个针对RAG系统知识库投毒攻击的五层深度防御框架，借鉴软件供应链安全理念，通过加密文档认证、信任加权检索、形式化污点格等技术，在评估中实现0.0%攻击成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 联邦机构部署的RAG系统面临知识库投毒攻击威胁，攻击者可通过注入恶意文档操纵系统输出。现有研究表明，仅需10个对抗性段落即可实现98.2%的检索成功率。RAG知识库投毒在结构上与软件供应链攻击类似，需要借鉴供应链安全理念构建防御机制。

Method: 提出RAGShield五层深度防御框架：1) 基于C2PA的加密文档认证，在摄取时阻止未签名和伪造文档；2) 信任加权检索，优先处理经过来源验证的源；3) 形式化污点格与跨源矛盾检测，即使来源有效也能捕获内部威胁；4) 来源感知生成与可审计引用；5) NIST SP 800-53合规性映射，覆盖15个控制族。

Result: 在包含500个段落的Natural Questions语料库上评估，包含63个攻击文档和200个查询，针对五个攻击者层级，实现0.0%攻击成功率（95%置信区间：[0.0%, 1.9%]），误报率为0.0%。内部人员原位替换攻击达到17.5%攻击成功率，揭示了摄取时防御的基本限制。跨源矛盾检测器能够捕获完全绕过来源验证的微妙数值操纵攻击。

Conclusion: RAGShield通过借鉴软件供应链安全理念，为RAG系统知识库投毒攻击提供了有效的多层次防御方案。虽然内部人员原位替换攻击仍构成挑战，但框架中的跨源矛盾检测等技术能够有效应对绕过来源验证的攻击。该框架为联邦机构RAG系统安全部署提供了实用解决方案。

Abstract: RAG systems deployed across federal agencies for citizen-facing services are vulnerable to knowledge base poisoning attacks, where adversaries inject malicious documents to manipulate outputs. Recent work demonstrates that as few as 10 adversarial passages can achieve 98.2% retrieval success rates. We observe that RAG knowledge base poisoning is structurally analogous to software supply chain attacks, and propose RAGShield, a five-layer defense-in-depth framework applying supply chain provenance verification to the RAG knowledge pipeline. RAGShield introduces: (1) C2PA-inspired cryptographic document attestation blocking unsigned and forged documents at ingestion; (2) trust-weighted retrieval prioritizing provenance-verified sources; (3) a formal taint lattice with cross-source contradiction detection catching insider threats even when provenance is valid; (4) provenance-aware generation with auditable citations; and (5) NIST SP 800-53 compliance mapping across 15 control families. Evaluation on a 500-passage Natural Questions corpus with 63 attack documents and 200 queries against five adversary tiers achieves 0.0% attack success rate including adaptive attacks (95% CI: [0.0%, 1.9%]) with 0.0% false positive rate. We honestly report that insider in-place replacement attacks achieve 17.5% ASR, identifying the fundamental limit of ingestion-time defense. The cross-source contradiction detector catches subtle numerical manipulation attacks that bypass provenance verification entirely.

</details>


### [67] [Efficient DPF-based Error-Detecting Information-Theoretic Private Information Retrieval Over Rings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00411)
*Pengzhen Ke,Liang Feng Zhang,Huaxiong Wang,Li-Ping Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出基于素数幂环的信息论错误检测私有信息检索方案，突破APIR的有限域限制，显著降低密钥大小和通信开销


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有APIR方案存在两个主要问题：1) 有限域结构限制只能使用素数阶DPF，导致信息论设置下密钥尺寸过大；2) 双DPF密钥设计引入不必要的通信开销，限制了大规模部署的实用性

Method: 提出基于素数幂环的信息论ED-PIR方案，利用素数幂阶信息论DPF，突破APIR的域约束，采用单itDPF密钥设计替代冗余的双密钥设置

Result: 显著降低密钥大小增长，使方案在高安全场景下可行；查询端通信开销减半，同时保持隐私性和可验证性；建立了轻量级、灵活的DPF-based恶意弹性私有信息检索框架

Conclusion: 该工作为分布式存储系统和后量子隐私协议中的隐私保护数据检索开辟了新途径，提供了更高效实用的ED-PIR解决方案

Abstract: Authenticated private information retrieval (APIR) is the state-of-the-art error-detecting private information retrieval (ED-PIR), using Distributed Point Functions (DPFs) for subpolynomial complexity and privacy. However, its finite field structure restricts it to prime-order DPFs, leading to prohibitively large key sizes under information-theoretic settings, while its dual-DPF-key design introduces unnecessary communication overhead, limiting its practicality for large-scale deployments. This paper proposes a novel ring-based information-theoretic ED-PIR (itED-PIR) scheme that overcomes these limitations by leveraging prime-power-order information-theoretic DPFs (itDPFs). Built over a prime-power ring, the proposed scheme breaks APIR's field-induced constraint to enable more efficient DPF utilization, significantly reducing key size growth and rendering the scheme feasible for high-security scenarios. Additionally, a single-itDPF-key design halves query-side communication overhead by eliminating APIR's redundant dual-key setup, without compromising privacy or verifiability. Beyond immediate efficiency gains, this work establishes a lightweight, flexible framework for constructing DPF-based malicious-resilient private information retrieval, opening new avenues for privacy-preserving data retrieval in distributed storage systems and post-quantum privacy protocols.

</details>


### [68] [Lightweight, Practical Encrypted Face Recognition with GPU Support](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00546)
*Gabrielle De Micheli,Syed Mahbub Hafiz,Geovandro Pereira,Eduardo L. Cominetti,Thales B. Paiva,Jina Choi,Marcos A. Simplicio,Bahattin Yildiz*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出BSGS-Diagonal算法和GPU优化内核，显著提升基于全同态加密的人脸识别隐私保护系统的效率和内存使用，实现亚秒级加密人脸识别。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于全同态加密的人脸识别系统存在计算成本高、内存开销大的问题，难以在资源受限的客户端部署。需要改进算法和系统设计以实现实际部署。

Method: 1. 提出BSGS-Diagonal算法，大幅减少旋转密钥数量，降低客户端和服务器内存需求；2. 开发完全GPU优化的相似度矩阵计算内核，基于FIDESlib/OpenFHE库，通过操作融合避免重复的CPU-GPU数据传输。

Result: 1. 旋转密钥数量减少91%，客户端内存减少约14GB，总体CPU峰值RAM从超过30GB降至10GB以下（支持100万条数据库）；2. 运行时性能提升：成员验证场景1.57倍，识别场景1.43倍；3. GPU实现分别获得9倍和17倍加速，支持32K条数据库的亚秒级加密人脸识别。

Conclusion: BSGS-Diagonal算法和GPU优化内核显著提升了基于全同态加密的人脸识别系统的实用性和部署可行性，为资源受限环境下的隐私保护人脸识别提供了高效解决方案。

Abstract: Face recognition models operate in a client-server setting where a client extracts a compact face embedding and a server performs similarity search over a template database. This raises privacy concerns, as facial data is highly sensitive. To provide cryptographic privacy guarantees, one can use fully homomorphic encryption to perform end-to-end encrypted similarity search. However, existing FHE-based protocols are computationally costly and, impose high memory overhead. Building on prior work, HyDia, we introduce algorithmic and system-level improvements targeting real-world deployment with resource-constrained clients. First, we propose BSGS-Diagonal, an algorithm delivering fast and memory-efficient similarity computation. BSGS-Diagonal substantially shrinks the rotation-key set, lowering both client and server memory requirements, and also improves practical server runtime. This yields a 91% reduction in the number of rotation keys, translating to approximately 14 GB less memory used on the client, and reducing overall CPU peak RAM from over 30 GB in the original HyDia to under 10 GB for databases up to size 1M. In addition, runtime is improved by up to 1.57x for the membership verification scenario and 1.43x for the identification scenario. Secondly, we introduce fully GPU-optimized similarity matrix computation kernels. The implementation is built upon FIDESlib, a CKKS-level GPU library based on OpenFHE. Rather than offloading individual CKKS primitives in isolation, the integrated kernels fuse operations to avoid repeated CPU-GPU ciphertext movement and costly FIDESlib/OpenFHE data-structure conversions. As a result, our GPU implementations of both HyDia and BSGS-Diagonal achieve up to 9x and 17x speedups, respectively, enabling sub-second encrypted face recognition for databases up to 32K entries while further reducing host memory usage.

</details>


### [69] [Quantum-Safe Code Auditing: LLM-Assisted Static Analysis and Quantum-Aware Risk Scoring for Post-Quantum Cryptography Migration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00560)
*Animesh Shaw*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出Quantum-Safe Code Auditor框架，通过静态分析检测量子易受攻击的密码原语，结合LLM上下文分析和VQE风险评分，帮助代码库迁移到后量子密码学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子计算机的出现威胁传统密码学安全（Shor算法破解RSA/ECDSA等，Grover算法削弱对称密码），尽管NIST已标准化后量子密码学，但大多数代码库缺乏自动化工具来识别量子易受攻击的密码使用并优先迁移。

Method: 开发量子感知的静态分析框架：1) 基于正则表达式检测15类量子易受攻击的密码原语；2) LLM辅助的上下文增强以分类使用情况和严重性；3) 使用Qiskit 2.x实现的变分量子本征求解器(VQE)模型进行风险评分，结合量子比特成本估计来优先处理发现。

Result: 在5个开源库（python-rsa、python-ecdsa、python-jose、node-jsonwebtoken、Bouncy Castle Java）中评估，覆盖5,775个发现。在602个标记实例的分层样本上，达到71.98%精确率、100%召回率和83.71% F1分数。所有代码、数据和复现脚本均已开源发布。

Conclusion: Quantum-Safe Code Auditor为代码库从经典密码学向后量子密码学迁移提供了有效的自动化工具，通过高召回率和合理的精确率识别量子易受攻击的密码使用，结合量子风险评分帮助优先处理迁移任务。

Abstract: The impending arrival of cryptographically relevant quantum computers (CRQCs) threatens the security foundations of modern software: Shor's algorithm breaks RSA, ECDSA, ECDH, and Diffie-Hellman, while Grover's algorithm reduces the effective security of symmetric and hash-based schemes. Despite NIST standardising post-quantum cryptography (PQC) in 2024 (FIPS 203 ML-KEM, FIPS 204 ML-DSA, FIPS 205 SLH-DSA), most codebases lack automated tooling to inventory classical cryptographic usage and prioritise migration based on quantum risk. We present Quantum-Safe Code Auditor, a quantum-aware static analysis framework that combines (i) regex-based detection of 15 classes of quantum-vulnerable primitives, (ii) LLM-assisted contextual enrichment to classify usage and severity, and (iii) risk scoring via a Variational Quantum Eigensolver (VQE) model implemented in Qiskit 2.x, incorporating qubit-cost estimates to prioritise findings. We evaluate the system across five open-source libraries -- python-rsa, python-ecdsa, python-jose, node-jsonwebtoken, and Bouncy Castle Java -- covering 5,775 findings. On a stratified sample of 602 labelled instances, we achieve 71.98% precision, 100% recall, and an F1 score of 83.71%. All code, data, and reproduction scripts are released as open-source.

</details>


### [70] [When Safe Models Merge into Danger: Exploiting Latent Vulnerabilities in LLM Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00627)
*Jiaqing Li,Zhibo Zhang,Shide Zhou,Yuxi Li,Tianlong Yu,Kailong Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 模型合并技术存在安全漏洞，TrojanMerge框架通过优化约束在源模型中嵌入潜在恶意组件，合并后产生严重不安全的模型


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 模型合并已成为结合多个微调LLM专业能力的重要技术，但其安全影响尚未得到充分探索。当前广泛采用的模型合并实践存在未发现的安全风险，需要揭示这种新技术可能引入的攻击面

Method: 提出TrojanMerge框架，将攻击建模为约束优化问题：构建满足方向一致性约束的扰动以保持源模型安全性，通过Frobenius方向对齐约束维持能力，但在合并时组合形成预计算攻击向量。源模型保持良性，合并后产生严重不安全的模型

Result: 在3个模型家族的9个LLM上进行广泛实验，TrojanMerge在合并模型中持续实现高有害响应率，而源模型的安全评分与未修改版本相当。攻击在不同合并算法和超参数配置下均有效

Conclusion: 模型合并实践存在根本性安全漏洞，TrojanMerge攻击暴露了当前方法的安全风险，迫切需要开发安全感知的合并机制

Abstract: Model merging has emerged as a powerful technique for combining specialized capabilities from multiple fine-tuned LLMs without additional training costs. However, the security implications of this widely-adopted practice remain critically underexplored. In this work, we reveal that model merging introduces a novel attack surface that can be systematically exploited to compromise safety alignment. We present TrojanMerge,, a framework that embeds latent malicious components into source models that remain individually benign but produce severely misaligned models when merged. Our key insight is formulating this attack as a constrained optimization problem: we construct perturbations that preserve source model safety through directional consistency constraints, maintain capabilities via Frobenius directional alignment constraints, yet combine during merging to form pre-computed attack vectors. Extensive experiments across 9 LLMs from 3 model families demonstrate that TrojanMerge, consistently achieves high harmful response rates in merged models while source models maintain safety scores comparable to unmodified versions. Our attack succeeds across diverse merging algorithms and remains effective under various hyperparameter configurations. These findings expose fundamental vulnerabilities in current model merging practices and highlight the urgent need for security-aware mechanisms.

</details>


### [71] [AutoEG: Exploiting Known Third-Party Vulnerabilities in Black-Box Web Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00704)
*Ruozhao Yang,Mingfei Cheng,Gelei Deng,Junjie Wang,Tianwei Zhang,Xiaofei Xie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AutoEG：针对黑盒Web应用的多智能体自动化漏洞利用生成框架，通过提取漏洞触发逻辑和反馈驱动迭代优化，显著提升漏洞利用成功率


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模Web应用广泛使用复杂的第三方组件，继承了组件漏洞带来的安全风险。现有渗透测试方法在自动生成可靠漏洞利用方面存在不足，主要面临两个问题：1）精确触发漏洞需要正确的技术细节；2）适应多样化的实际部署环境

Method: AutoEG采用两阶段多智能体框架：第一阶段从非结构化漏洞信息中提取精确的漏洞触发逻辑，封装为可重用的触发函数；第二阶段利用触发函数实现具体攻击目标，通过与目标应用的反馈驱动交互迭代优化漏洞利用

Result: 在104个真实漏洞、29个攻击目标、660个利用任务和55,440次利用尝试的评估中，AutoEG平均成功率达到82.41%，显著优于最佳基线方法的32.88%

Conclusion: AutoEG通过自动化提取漏洞触发逻辑和反馈驱动的迭代优化，有效解决了黑盒Web应用中漏洞利用生成的关键挑战，显著提升了自动化安全评估的可靠性和有效性

Abstract: Large-scale web applications are widely deployed with complex third-party components, inheriting security risks arising from component vulnerabilities. Security assessment is therefore required to determine whether such known vulnerabilities remain practically exploitable in real applications. Penetration testing is a widely adopted approach that validates exploitability by launching concrete attacks against known vulnerabilities in real-world black-box systems. However, existing approaches often fail to automatically generate reliable exploits, limiting their effectiveness in practical security assessment. This limitation mainly stems from two issues: (1) precisely triggering vulnerabilities with correct technical details, and (2) adapting exploits to diverse real-world deployment settings.
  In this paper, we propose AutoEG, a fully automated multi-agent framework for exploit generation targeting black-box web applications. AutoEG has two phases: First, AutoEG extracts precise vulnerability trigger logic from unstructured vulnerability information and encapsulates it into reusable trigger functions. Second, AutoEG uses trigger functions for concrete attack objectives and iteratively refines exploits through feedback-driven interaction with the target application. We evaluate AutoEG on 104 real-world vulnerabilities with 29 attack objectives, resulting in 660 exploitation tasks and 55,440 exploit attempts. AutoEG achieves an average success rate of 82.41%, substantially outperforming state-of-the-art baselines, whose best performance reaches only 32.88%.

</details>


### [72] [AutoMIA: Improved Baselines for Membership Inference Attack via Agentic Self-Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01014)
*Ruhao Liu,Weiqi Huang,Qi Li,Xinchao Wang*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: AutoMIA：一种将成员推理攻击重构为自动化自我探索与策略演化的智能体框架，无需手动特征工程即可匹配或超越现有方法


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有成员推理攻击方法主要依赖静态、手工设计的启发式规则，缺乏适应性，在不同大型模型间迁移时性能不佳。需要一种更灵活、自适应的自动化攻击框架。

Method: 提出AutoMIA智能体框架，将成员推理攻击重构为自动化自我探索和策略演化过程。给定高级场景规范，框架通过生成可执行的logits级策略并在闭环评估反馈中逐步优化，实现攻击空间的系统探索。通过解耦抽象策略推理与底层执行，实现模型无关的攻击搜索空间遍历。

Result: 大量实验表明，AutoMIA在无需手动特征工程的情况下，能够持续匹配或超越最先进的基线方法，展现了良好的适应性和性能。

Conclusion: AutoMIA提供了一种系统化、模型无关的成员推理攻击自动化方法，通过智能体驱动的自我探索和策略演化，解决了现有静态启发式方法适应性不足的问题，为机器学习模型隐私审计提供了更有效的工具。

Abstract: Membership Inference Attacks (MIAs) serve as a fundamental auditing tool for evaluating training data leakage in machine learning models. However, existing methodologies predominantly rely on static, handcrafted heuristics that lack adaptability, often leading to suboptimal performance when transferred across different large models. In this work, we propose AutoMIA, an agentic framework that reformulates membership inference as an automated process of self-exploration and strategy evolution. Given high-level scenario specifications, AutoMIA self-explores the attack space by generating executable logits-level strategies and progressively refining them through closed-loop evaluation feedback. By decoupling abstract strategy reasoning from low-level execution, our framework enables a systematic, model-agnostic traversal of the attack search space. Extensive experiments demonstrate that AutoMIA consistently matches or outperforms state-of-the-art baselines while eliminating the need for manual feature engineering.

</details>


### [73] [Automated Framework to Evaluate and Harden LLM System Instructions against Encoding Attacks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01039)
*Anubhab Sahu,Diptisha Samanta,Reza Soosahabi*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 论文提出自动化评估框架，测试LLM系统指令在重构为编码或结构化输出任务时的泄露风险，发现高攻击成功率，并提出基于单样本指令重塑的缓解策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM系统指令常包含敏感信息（API凭证、内部策略等），其泄露是LLM应用的关键安全风险。现有拒绝式指令假设敏感信息只能通过显式查询提取，但缺乏对重构请求形式攻击的评估。

Method: 引入自动化评估框架，测试系统指令在重构为编码或结构化输出任务时的保密性。使用四种常见模型和46个已验证系统指令，评估结构化序列化攻击效果。提出基于单样本指令重塑的缓解策略，利用思维链推理模型调整指令措辞和结构。

Result: 在结构化序列化攻击中，模型拒绝直接提取请求，但在请求的序列化格式中披露受保护内容，攻击成功率超过0.7。通过指令重塑可显著降低攻击成功率，无需模型重训练。

Conclusion: 系统指令泄露风险比预期更严重，重构请求可绕过拒绝机制。指令重塑是有效的缓解策略，强调需要更全面的安全评估和指令设计方法。

Abstract: System Instructions in Large Language Models (LLMs) are commonly used to enforce safety policies, define agent behavior, and protect sensitive operational context in agentic AI applications. These instructions may contain sensitive information such as API credentials, internal policies, and privileged workflow definitions, making system instruction leakage a critical security risk highlighted in the OWASP Top 10 for LLM Applications. Without incurring the overhead costs of reasoning models, many LLM applications rely on refusal-based instructions that block direct requests for system instructions, implicitly assuming that prohibited information can only be extracted through explicit queries. We introduce an automated evaluation framework that tests whether system instructions remain confidential when extraction requests are re-framed as encoding or structured output tasks. Across four common models and 46 verified system instructions, we observe high attack success rates (> 0.7) for structured serialization where models refuse direct extraction requests but disclose protected content in the requested serialization formats. We further demonstrate a mitigation strategy based on one-shot instruction reshaping using a Chain-of-Thought reasoning model, indicating that even subtle changes in wording and structure of system instructions can significantly reduce attack success rate without requiring model retraining.

</details>


### [74] [VibeGuard: A Security Gate Framework for AI-Generated Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01052)
*Ying Xie*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: VibeGuard：针对AI辅助编程（vibe coding）引入的新型安全漏洞的预发布安全检查工具，能够检测源文件泄露、配置漂移等传统工具忽略的盲点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AI辅助编程（vibe coding）的快速普及带来了新型安全漏洞，如Anthropic Claude Code CLI的源文件泄露事件所示。现有静态分析和秘密扫描工具无法覆盖这些新型漏洞，存在检测盲区。

Method: 开发VibeGuard预发布安全门，针对五个特定盲点：工件卫生、打包配置漂移、源文件暴露、硬编码密钥和供应链风险。在八个合成项目（七个有漏洞，一个干净对照）上进行受控实验评估。

Result: VibeGuard在实验中实现了100%召回率、89.47%精确率（F1=94.44%），并在三个策略级别上对所有八个项目做出了正确的通过/失败门控决策。

Conclusion: VibeGuard填补了AI辅助编程引入的新型漏洞与现有工具检测能力之间的差距，为依赖AI代码生成的团队提供了深度防御工作流程的指导。

Abstract: "Vibe coding," in which developers delegate code generation to AI assistants and accept the output with little manual review, has gained rapid adoption in production settings. On March 31, 2026, Anthropic's Claude Code CLI shipped a 59.8 MB source map file in its npm package, exposing roughly 512,000 lines of proprietary TypeScript. The tool had itself been largely vibe-coded, and the leak traced to a misconfigured packaging rule rather than a logic bug. Existing static-analysis and secret-scanning tools did not cover this failure mode, pointing to a gap between the vulnerabilities AI tends to introduce and the vulnerabilities current tooling is built to find. We present VibeGuard, a pre-publish security gate that targets five such blind spots: artifact hygiene, packaging-configuration drift, source-map exposure, hardcoded secrets, and supply-chain risk. In controlled experiments on eight synthetic projects (seven vulnerable, one clean control), VibeGuard achieved 100% recall, 89.47% precision (F1 = 94.44%), and correct pass/fail gate decisions on all eight projects across three policy levels. We discuss how these results inform a defense-in-depth workflow for teams that rely on AI code generation.

</details>


### [75] [Obfuscating Code Vulnerabilities against Static Analysis in JavaScript Code](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01131)
*Francesco Pagano,Lorenzo Pisu,Leonardo Regano,Davide Maiorca,Alessio Merlo,Giorgio Giacinto*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 该论文实证评估了JavaScript代码混淆对SAST工具检测能力的影响，发现即使单一混淆技术也能显著降低漏洞检测率，而组合混淆技术几乎能完全规避检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码混淆在保护知识产权的同时也被攻击者用于隐藏恶意逻辑，特别是在软件供应链中，单个受污染包可能影响数千个应用。需要评估CI/CD管道依赖的SAST工具在面对混淆代码时的鲁棒性。

Method: 定义现实的供应链威胁模型，引入漏洞检测损失(VDL)指标，进行两阶段研究：1)分析16个OWASP目录中故意设计有漏洞的Node.js应用；2)扩展到260个GitHub上的实际JavaScript/Node.js项目。应用8种语义保持的混淆技术及其组合，评估Njsscan和Bearer两个代表性SAST工具。

Result: 单一混淆技术通常能抑制大多数基线发现（包括高严重性问题），而组合混淆技术几乎能完全规避检测，VDL经常接近100%。当前JavaScript SAST对常见混淆技术基本没有鲁棒性，对混淆代码的"干净"报告可能只是虚假的安全感。

Conclusion: 当前JavaScript SAST工具在面对代码混淆时存在根本性脆弱性，需要开发混淆感知的分析方法和制定实际缓解指南。

Abstract: Code obfuscation is widely adopted in modern software development to protect intellectual property and hinder reverse engineering, but it also provides attackers with a powerful means to conceal malicious logic inside otherwise legitimate JavaScript code. In a software supply chain where a single compromised package can affect thousands of applications, this raises a critical question: how robust are the Static Application Security Testing (SAST) tools that CI/CD pipelines rely on as automated security gatekeepers? This paper answers that question by empirically quantifying the impact of JavaScript obfuscation on state-of-practice SAST. We define a realistic supply-chain threat model in which an adversary injects vulnerable code and iteratively obfuscates it until the pipeline reports a clean scan. To measure the resulting degradation, we introduce the Vulnerability Detection Loss (VDL) metric and conduct a two-phase study. First, we analyze 16 vulnerable-by-design Node.js web applications from the OWASP directory; second, we extend the analysis to 260 in-the-wild JavaScript/Node.js projects from GitHub. Across both datasets, we apply eight semantics-preserving obfuscation techniques and their combinations and evaluate two representative SAST tools, Njsscan and Bearer. Even a single obfuscation technique typically suppresses most baseline findings, including high-severity issues, while stacking techniques yield near-total evasion, with VDL often approaching 100%. Our results show that current JavaScript SAST is fundamentally not robust against commonplace obfuscations and that "clean" reports on obfuscated code may offer only a false sense of security. Finally, we discuss practical mitigation guidelines and directions for obfuscation-aware analysis.

</details>


<div id='cs.LG'></div>

# cs.LG [[Back]](#toc)

### [76] [Two-Stage Optimizer-Aware Online Data Selection for Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00001)
*Fangxin Wang,Peyman Baghershahi,Langzhou He,Henry Peng Zou,Sourav Medya,Philip S. Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种面向优化器的梯度在线数据选择与重加权框架，用于大语言模型微调，通过两阶段过滤-加权算法提升收敛速度和下游性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
