Result: 探针能准确预测检查点性能（平均AUROC>0.75），具有良好的跨检查点泛化能力（早期预测后期），将评估延迟从~1小时降至~3分钟

Conclusion: 提出了一种实用且可扩展的训练中下游评估范式，使LLM开发过程更加敏捷、信息丰富且高效

Abstract: The paradigm of scaling Large Language Models (LLMs) in both parameter size and test time has pushed the boundaries of AI capabilities, but at the cost of making the traditional generative evaluation paradigm prohibitively expensive, therefore making the latency of LLM's in-training downstream performance evaluation unbearable. However, simple metrics like training loss (perplexity) are not always correlated with downstream performance, as sometimes their trends diverge from the actual task outcomes. This dilemma calls for a method that is computationally efficient and sufficiently accurate in measuring model capabilities. To address this challenge, we introduce a new in-training evaluation paradigm that uses a lightweight probe for monitoring downstream performance. The probes take the internal representations of LLM checkpoints (during training) as input and directly predict the checkpoint's performance on downstream tasks measured by success probability (i.e., pass@1). We design several probe architectures, validating their effectiveness using the OLMo3-7B's checkpoints across a diverse set of downstream tasks. The probes can accurately predict a checkpoint's performance (with avg. AUROC$>$0.75), have decent generalizability across checkpoints (earlier predicts later), and reduce the computation latency from $\sim$1 hr (using conventional generative evaluation method) to $\sim$3 min. In sum, this work presents a practical and scalable in-training downstream evaluation paradigm, enabling a more agile, informed, and efficient LLM development process.

</details>


### [115] [Toward Personalized Darts Training: A Data-Driven Framework Based on Skeleton-Based Biomechanical Analysis and Motion Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01130)
*Zhantao Chen,Dongyi He,Jin Fang,Xi Chen,Yisuo Liu,Xiaozhen Zhong,Xuejun Hu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种数据驱动的飞镖训练辅助系统，通过无标记运动捕捉、特征建模和个性化反馈的闭环框架，为高精度目标运动提供个性化训练支持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统飞镖训练主要依赖经验和视觉观察，难以满足高精度目标导向运动的需求。现有定量方法多关注局部变量、单次释放指标或静态模板匹配，对个性化训练支持有限且忽视有用的运动变异性。

Method: 使用Kinect 2.0深度传感器和光学相机在无标记条件下采集飞镖投掷数据，从四个生物力学维度提取18个运动学特征。开发了两个模块：1)结合历史高质量样本和最小加加速度准则的个性化最优投掷轨迹模型；2)基于z分数和分层逻辑的运动偏差诊断与推荐模型。

Result: 收集了专业和非专业运动员共2396个投掷样本。系统能生成符合自然人体运动的平滑个性化参考轨迹。案例研究表明，系统能检测躯干稳定性差、肘部位移异常和速度控制不平衡等问题，并提供针对性建议。

Conclusion: 该框架将飞镖评估从偏离统一标准转变为偏离个体最优控制范围，提高了飞镖训练及其他高精度目标运动的个性化和可解释性。

Abstract: As sports training becomes more data-driven, traditional dart coaching based mainly on experience and visual observation is increasingly inadequate for high-precision, goal-oriented movements. Although prior studies have highlighted the importance of release parameters, joint motion, and coordination in dart throwing, most quantitative methods still focus on local variables, single-release metrics, or static template matching. These approaches offer limited support for personalized training and often overlook useful movement variability. This paper presents a data-driven dart training assistance system. The system creates a closed-loop framework spanning motion capture, feature modeling, and personalized feedback. Dart-throwing data were collected in markerless conditions using a Kinect 2.0 depth sensor and an optical camera. Eighteen kinematic features were extracted from four biomechanical dimensions: three-link coordination, release velocity, multi-joint angular configuration, and postural stability. Two modules were developed: a personalized optimal throwing trajectory model that combines historical high-quality samples with the minimum jerk criterion, and a motion deviation diagnosis and recommendation model based on z-scores and hierarchical logic. A total of 2,396 throwing samples from professional and non-professional athletes were collected. Results show that the system generates smooth personalized reference trajectories consistent with natural human movement. Case studies indicate that it can detect poor trunk stability, abnormal elbow displacement, and imbalanced velocity control, then provide targeted recommendations. The framework shifts dart evaluation from deviation from a uniform standard to deviation from an individual's optimal control range, improving personalization and interpretability for darts training and other high-precision target sports.

</details>


### [116] [Property-Level Flood Risk Assessment Using AI-Enabled Street-View Lowest Floor Elevation Extraction and ML Imputation Across Texas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01153)
*Xiangpeng Li,Yu-Hsuan Ho,Sam D Brody,Ali Mostafavi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 利用街景图像AI分析和机器学习插补技术，为德克萨斯州18个区域生成建筑特定高程数据，结合洪水淹没模型评估洪水风险，实现从灾害暴露到建筑内部淹没深度和预期损失的精细化评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决区域尺度洪水风险评估中缺乏建筑特定高程数据的问题，特别是对于没有全面高程证书的司法管辖区，需要地块级信息来支持减灾、规划和洪水风险管理。

Method: 开发三阶段流程：1) 使用Elev-Vision框架从Google街景图像提取最低楼层高程(LFE)和街道标高与最低楼层高度差(HDSL)；2) 基于16个地形、水文、地理和洪水暴露特征，使用随机森林和梯度提升模型插补缺失的HDSL值；3) 将生成的高程数据集与Fathom百年一遇淹没面和USACE深度-损失函数整合，估算特定物业的内部洪水深度和预期损失。

Result: 在12,241个住宅结构中，73.4%的地块有街景图像可用，49.0%(5,992个结构)成功直接提取LFE/HDSL。在13个AOI中插补性能可接受(R²值0.159-0.974)，5个AOI因性能不足被排除。研究表明街景高程测绘虽非普遍可用，但具有足够可扩展性，能显著改善区域洪水风险表征。

Conclusion: 该研究将LFE估算从试点规模概念验证推进到区域端到端工作流程，为缺乏高程证书但需要地块级信息的司法管辖区提供了可复制的框架，支持减灾、规划和洪水风险管理。

Abstract: This paper argues that AI-enabled analysis of street-view imagery, complemented by performance-gated machine-learning imputation, provides a viable pathway for generating building-specific elevation data at regional scale for flood risk assessment. We develop and apply a three-stage pipeline across 18 areas of interest (AOIs) in Texas that (1) extracts LFE and the height difference between street grade and the lowest floor (HDSL) from Google Street View imagery using the Elev-Vision framework, (2) imputes missing HDSL values with Random Forest and Gradient Boosting models trained on 16 terrain, hydrologic, geographic, and flood-exposure features, and (3) integrates the resulting elevation dataset with Fathom 1-in-100 year inundation surfaces and USACE depth-damage functions to estimate property-specific interior flood depth and expected loss. Across 12,241 residential structures, street-view imagery was available for 73.4% of parcels and direct LFE/HDSL extraction was successful for 49.0% (5,992 structures). Imputation was retained for 13 AOIs where cross-validated performance was defensible, with selected models achieving R suqre values from 0.159 to 0.974; five AOIs were explicitly excluded from prediction because performance was insufficient. The results show that street-view-based elevation mapping is not universally available for every property, but it is sufficiently scalable to materially improve regional flood-risk characterization by moving beyond hazard exposure to structure-level estimates of interior inundation and expected damage. Scientifically, the study advances LFE estimation from a pilot-scale proof of concept to a regional, end-to-end workflow. Practically, it offers a replicable framework for jurisdictions that lack comprehensive Elevation Certificates but need parcel-level information to support mitigation, planning, and flood-risk management.

</details>


### [117] [Reasoning Shift: How Context Silently Shortens LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01161)
*Gleb Rodionov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 研究发现推理模型在不同上下文条件下会显著压缩推理轨迹（最多50%），减少自我验证行为，这可能影响复杂任务的性能


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管LLMs在复杂推理任务中表现出色，但其推理行为的鲁棒性尚未得到充分研究。本研究旨在系统评估推理模型在不同上下文条件下的表现，探究其推理行为的稳定性

Method: 在三种场景下系统评估多个推理模型：1）添加冗长无关上下文的问题；2）包含独立任务的多轮对话设置；3）作为复杂任务子任务呈现的问题。通过对比分析推理轨迹长度和行为模式变化

Result: 发现推理模型在不同上下文条件下会显著压缩推理轨迹（最多减少50%），这种压缩与自我验证和不确定性管理行为（如双重检查）的减少相关。虽然简单任务性能不受影响，但可能影响复杂挑战性任务的表现

Conclusion: 推理模型的行为在不同上下文条件下存在显著变化，这种鲁棒性问题值得关注。研究呼吁更多关注推理模型的鲁棒性以及LLMs和基于LLM的智能体的上下文管理问题

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) exhibiting test-time scaling behavior, such as extended reasoning traces and self-verification, have demonstrated remarkable performance on complex, long-term reasoning tasks. However, the robustness of these reasoning behaviors remains underexplored. To investigate this, we conduct a systematic evaluation of multiple reasoning models across three scenarios: (1) problems augmented with lengthy, irrelevant context; (2) multi-turn conversational settings with independent tasks; and (3) problems presented as a subtask within a complex task. We observe an interesting phenomenon: reasoning models tend to produce much shorter reasoning traces (up to 50%) for the same problem under different context conditions compared to the traces produced when the problem is presented in isolation. A finer-grained analysis reveals that this compression is associated with a decrease in self-verification and uncertainty management behaviors, such as double-checking. While this behavioral shift does not compromise performance on straightforward problems, it might affect performance on more challenging tasks. We hope our findings draw additional attention to both the robustness of reasoning models and the problem of context management for LLMs and LLM-based agents.

</details>


### [118] [Bridging the Simulation-to-Experiment Gap with Generative Models using Adversarial Distribution Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01169)
*Kai Nelson,Tobias Kreiman,Sergey Levine,Aditi S. Krishnapriyan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出对抗分布对齐(ADA)框架，通过预训练生成模型在模拟数据上，然后与实验观测数据对齐，弥合仿真与实验之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 科学和工程中的仿真与实验之间存在差距：仿真数据完整但存在计算近似，实验数据真实但只能部分反映系统状态。需要一种方法结合两者的优势。

Method: 提出对抗分布对齐(ADA)框架：1) 在完整但近似的仿真数据上预训练生成模型；2) 通过对抗训练将模型与部分但真实的实验观测数据对齐。特别针对物理科学，对齐原子位置的生成模型与实验观测分布。

Result: 理论证明该方法能恢复目标观测分布，即使存在多个潜在相关的观测变量。在合成数据、分子数据和实验蛋白质数据上验证了框架有效性，能够将生成模型与多样化的观测数据对齐。

Conclusion: ADA框架成功弥合了仿真与实验之间的差距，通过结合仿真数据的完整性和实验数据的真实性，为复杂系统的建模提供了有效的解决方案。

Abstract: A fundamental challenge in science and engineering is the simulation-to-experiment gap. While we often possess prior knowledge of physical laws, these physical laws can be too difficult to solve exactly for complex systems. Such systems are commonly modeled using simulators, which impose computational approximations. Meanwhile, experimental measurements more faithfully represent the real world, but experimental data typically consists of observations that only partially reflect the system's full underlying state. We propose a data-driven distribution alignment framework that bridges this simulation-to-experiment gap by pre-training a generative model on fully observed (but imperfect) simulation data, then aligning it with partial (but real) observations of experimental data. While our method is domain-agnostic, we ground our approach in the physical sciences by introducing Adversarial Distribution Alignment (ADA). This method aligns a generative model of atomic positions -- initially trained on a simulated Boltzmann distribution -- with the distribution of experimental observations. We prove that our method recovers the target observable distribution, even with multiple, potentially correlated observables. We also empirically validate our framework on synthetic, molecular, and experimental protein data, demonstrating that it can align generative models with diverse observables. Our code is available at https://kaityrusnelson.com/ada/.

</details>


### [119] [Online Reasoning Calibration: Test-Time Training Enables Generalizable Conformal LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01170)
*Cai Zhou,Zekai Wang,Menghua Wu,Qianyu Julie Zhu,Flora C. Shi,Chenyu Wang,Ashia Wilson,Tommi Jaakkola,Stephen Bates*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: ORCA是一个在线推理校准框架，通过元学习和保形预测技术，在测试时动态校准语言模型的采样过程，显著提高推理效率并保证置信度估计的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大型语言模型在测试时缩放虽然能解决高难度任务，但计算成本极高。这种低效性源于后训练语言模型的校准不足以及流行采样技术缺乏校准。需要一种方法在分布偏移下提供有效的置信度估计，并提高推理效率。

Method: ORCA结合保形预测和测试时训练，引入元学习过程为每个输入更新校准模块。该框架动态校准采样过程，在不同推理阶段的思维模式或开发与部署间的提示分布偏移下提供有效的置信度估计。

Result: 在风险水平δ=0.1时，ORCA在分布内任务上为Qwen2.5-32B带来高达47.5%（监督标签）和40.7%（自一致性标签）的效率提升。在零样本分布外设置下，将MATH-500的节省率从静态校准基线的24.8%提高到67.0%，同时保持低经验错误率，这一趋势在不同模型家族和下游基准测试中均成立。

Conclusion: ORCA不仅提供保形风险的理论保证，还在实践中展示了跨不同推理任务的更高效率和泛化能力，为降低大型语言模型推理成本提供了有效的校准框架。

Abstract: While test-time scaling has enabled large language models to solve highly difficult tasks, state-of-the-art results come at exorbitant compute costs. These inefficiencies can be attributed to the miscalibration of post-trained language models, and the lack of calibration in popular sampling techniques. Here, we present Online Reasoning Calibration (ORCA), a framework for calibrating the sampling process that draws upon conformal prediction and test-time training. Specifically, we introduce a meta-learning procedure that updates the calibration module for each input. This allows us to provide valid confidence estimates under distributional shift, e.g. in thought patterns that occur across different stages of reasoning, or in prompt distributions between model development and deployment. ORCA not only provides theoretical guarantees on conformal risks, but also empirically shows higher efficiency and generalization across different reasoning tasks. At risk level $δ=0.1$, ORCA improves Qwen2.5-32B efficiency on in-distribution tasks with savings up to 47.5% with supervised labels and 40.7% with self-consistency labels. Under zero-shot out-of-domain settings, it improves MATH-500 savings from 24.8% of the static calibration baseline to 67.0% while maintaining a low empirical error rate, and the same trend holds across model families and downstream benchmarks. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/wzekai99/ORCA.

</details>


### [120] [NeuroDDAF: Neural Dynamic Diffusion-Advection Fields with Evidential Fusion for Air Quality Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01175)
*Prasanjit Dey,Soumyabrata Dev,Angela Meyer,Bianca Schoen-Phelan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: NeuroDDAF是一个物理信息化的空气质量预测框架，通过结合神经表示学习和开放系统输运模型，在多个城市数据集上实现了优于现有方法的预测性能，特别是在长期预测中显著降低了误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空气质量预测对公共健康和环境政策至关重要，但面临非线性时空动态、风驱动传输和区域分布变化等挑战。基于物理的模型可解释但计算成本高且依赖限制性假设，而纯数据驱动模型可能准确但缺乏鲁棒性和校准的不确定性。

Method: 提出Neural Dynamic Diffusion-Advection Fields (NeuroDDAF)框架，包含四个核心组件：(1) GRU-图注意力编码器捕捉时间动态和风感知空间交互；(2) 傅里叶域扩散-平流模块，具有可学习残差；(3) 风调制潜在神经ODE，在时变连接下建模连续时间演化；(4) 证据融合机制自适应结合物理引导和神经预测，同时量化不确定性。

Result: 在四个城市数据集（北京、深圳、天津、安科纳）上1-3天预测范围内，NeuroDDAF始终优于强基线方法（包括AirPhyNet），长期预测中RMSE降低达9.7%，MAE降低达9.4%。在北京数据集上，1天预测RMSE为41.63 μg/m³，3天预测为48.88 μg/m³，是所有比较方法中最佳性能。此外，NeuroDDAF改善了跨城市泛化能力，并产生良好校准的不确定性估计。

Conclusion: NeuroDDAF成功地将神经表示学习与开放系统输运模型统一起来，为空气质量预测提供了一个物理信息化的框架，在准确性、泛化能力和不确定性量化方面均表现出优越性能，为解决非线性时空动态和分布偏移问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Accurate air quality forecasting is crucial for protecting public health and guiding environmental policy, yet it remains challenging due to nonlinear spatiotemporal dynamics, wind-driven transport, and distribution shifts across regions. Physics-based models are interpretable but computationally expensive and often rely on restrictive assumptions, whereas purely data-driven models can be accurate but may lack robustness and calibrated uncertainty. To address these limitations, we propose Neural Dynamic Diffusion-Advection Fields (NeuroDDAF), a physics-informed forecasting framework that unifies neural representation learning with open-system transport modeling. NeuroDDAF integrates (i) a GRU-Graph Attention encoder to capture temporal dynamics and wind-aware spatial interactions, (ii) a Fourier-domain diffusion-advection module with learnable residuals, (iii) a wind-modulated latent Neural ODE to model continuous-time evolution under time-varying connectivity, and (iv) an evidential fusion mechanism that adaptively combines physics-guided and neural forecasts while quantifying uncertainty. Experiments on four urban datasets (Beijing, Shenzhen, Tianjin, and Ancona) across 1-3 day horizons show that NeuroDDAF consistently outperforms strong baselines, including AirPhyNet, achieving up to 9.7% reduction in RMSE and 9.4% reduction in MAE on long-term forecasts. On the Beijing dataset, NeuroDDAF attains an RMSE of 41.63 $μ$g/m$^3$ for 1-day prediction and 48.88 $μ$g/m$^3$ for 3-day prediction, representing the best performance among all compared methods. In addition, NeuroDDAF improves cross-city generalization and yields well-calibrated uncertainty estimates, as confirmed by ensemble variance analysis and case studies under varying wind conditions.

</details>


### [121] [Screening Is Enough](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01178)
*Ken M. Nakanishi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Multiscreen是一种新的语言模型架构，通过引入screening机制实现绝对查询-键相关性，避免键之间的全局竞争，在减少参数、提高训练稳定性、长上下文处理等方面优于标准Transformer。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准softmax注意力的核心限制在于它只定义相对查询-键相关性，无法显式拒绝不相关的键。注意力权重通过在所有键之间重新分配固定单位质量获得，相关性仅相对于竞争键定义，不相关键不能被明确拒绝。

Method: 引入Multiscreen架构，围绕screening机制构建。该机制评估每个键与显式阈值的关系，丢弃不相关键并聚合剩余键，从而消除键之间的全局竞争。不是在所有键之间重新分配注意力。

Result: Multiscreen在验证损失相当的情况下，比Transformer基线减少约40%参数；支持显著更大的学习率进行稳定优化；在长上下文困惑度方面保持强性能；在远超训练上下文长度时检索性能几乎没有退化；在100K上下文长度下推理延迟降低达3.2倍。

Conclusion: Multiscreen通过screening机制解决了标准softmax注意力的绝对相关性限制，在参数效率、训练稳定性、长上下文处理和推理延迟方面表现出显著优势，为语言模型架构提供了有前景的替代方案。

Abstract: A core limitation of standard softmax attention is that it does not define a notion of absolute query--key relevance: attention weights are obtained by redistributing a fixed unit mass across all keys according to their relative scores. As a result, relevance is defined only relative to competing keys, and irrelevant keys cannot be explicitly rejected. We introduce Multiscreen, a language-model architecture built around a mechanism we call screening, which enables absolute query--key relevance. Instead of redistributing attention across all keys, screening evaluates each key against an explicit threshold, discarding irrelevant keys and aggregating the remaining keys, thereby removing global competition among keys. Across experiments, Multiscreen achieves comparable validation loss with approximately 40% fewer parameters than a Transformer baseline, enables stable optimization at substantially larger learning rates, maintains strong performance in long-context perplexity, shows little to no degradation in retrieval performance even far beyond the training context length, and reduces inference latency by up to 3.2$\times$ at 100K context length.

</details>


### [122] [The Recipe Matters More Than the Kitchen:Mathematical Foundations of the AI Weather Prediction Pipeline](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01215)
*Piyush Garg,Diana R. Gergel,Andrew E. Shao,Galen J. Yacalis*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出统一数学框架分析AI天气预报技能，证明训练方法、损失函数和数据多样性比架构选择更重要，建立了学习管道误差分解和损失函数谱理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论主要关注特定架构选择而非完整学习管道，而2023-2026年的操作证据表明训练方法、损失函数设计和数据多样性至少与架构选择同等重要。需要统一数学框架来解释天气预报技能的决定因素。

Method: 理论框架基于球面近似理论、动力系统理论、信息论和统计学习理论，建立了学习管道误差分解、损失函数谱理论和外推边界。实证验证使用NVIDIA Earth2Studio对10个架构多样的AI天气模型进行推理，评估6个指标跨越30个初始化日期。

Result: 结果证实：MSE训练模型在高波数处存在普遍谱能量损失；误差共识比率上升表明大部分预测误差在不同架构间共享；极端事件期间存在线性负偏差。提出了整体模型评估分数和预测性框架。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了统一的数学框架来分析AI天气预报技能，证明估计误差（损失和数据相关）在当前尺度上主导近似误差（架构相关），为学习管道设计提供了理论基础和评估工具。

Abstract: AI weather prediction has advanced rapidly, yet no unified mathematical framework explains what determines forecast skill. Existing theory addresses specific architectural choices rather than the learning pipeline as a whole, while operational evidence from 2023-2026 demonstrates that training methodology, loss function design, and data diversity matter at least as much as architecture selection. This paper makes two interleaved contributions. Theoretically, we construct a framework rooted in approximation theory on the sphere, dynamical systems theory, information theory, and statistical learning theory that treats the complete learning pipeline (architecture, loss function, training strategy, data distribution) rather than architecture alone. We establish a Learning Pipeline Error Decomposition showing that estimation error (loss- and data-dependent) dominates approximation error (architecture-dependent) at current scales. We develop a Loss Function Spectral Theory formalizing MSE-induced spectral blurring in spherical harmonic coordinates, and derive Out-of-Distribution Extrapolation Bounds proving that data-driven models systematically underestimate record-breaking extremes with bias growing linearly in record exceedance. Empirically, we validate these predictions via inference across ten architecturally diverse AI weather models using NVIDIA Earth2Studio with ERA5 initial conditions, evaluating six metrics across 30 initialization dates spanning all seasons. Results confirm universal spectral energy loss at high wavenumbers for MSE-trained models, rising Error Consensus Ratios showing that the majority of forecast error is shared across architectures, and linear negative bias during extreme events. A Holistic Model Assessment Score provides unified multi-dimensional evaluation, and a prescriptive framework enables mathematical evaluation of proposed pipelines before training.

</details>


### [123] [LAtent Phase Inference from Short time sequences using SHallow REcurrent Decoders (LAPIS-SHRED)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.01216)
*Yuxuan Bao,Xingyue Zhang,J. Nathan Kutz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: LAPIS-SHRED：一种模块化架构，可从短时间窗口的稀疏传感器观测中重建和预测完整时空动力学


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从空间和时间上都稀疏的观测中重建完整时空动力学是复杂系统的核心挑战，但这对机制理解、模型校准和操作决策至关重要

Method: 三阶段流水线：1) SHRED模型在仿真数据上预训练，将传感器时间历史映射到结构化潜在空间；2) 时间序列模型在仿真潜在轨迹上训练，学习向前或向后传播潜在状态以覆盖未观测时间区域；3) 部署时仅需真实系统的短观测窗口，冻结的SHRED模型和时间模型联合重建或预测完整时空轨迹

Result: 在六个复杂时空物理实验中评估：湍流、多尺度推进物理、挥发性燃烧瞬变、卫星衍生环境场，展示了轻量级模块化架构在观测受限操作环境中的适用性

Conclusion: LAPIS-SHRED支持双向推理，继承模块化结构的数据同化和多尺度重建能力，适应极端观测约束（包括单帧终端输入），适用于物理或后勤限制下的操作设置

Abstract: Reconstructing full spatio-temporal dynamics from sparse observations in both space and time remains a central challenge in complex systems, as measurements can be spatially incomplete and can be also limited to narrow temporal windows. Yet approximating the complete spatio-temporal trajectory is essential for mechanistic insight and understanding, model calibration, and operational decision-making. We introduce LAPIS-SHRED (LAtent Phase Inference from Short time sequence using SHallow REcurrent Decoders), a modular architecture that reconstructs and/or forecasts complete spatiotemporal dynamics from sparse sensor observations confined to short temporal windows. LAPIS-SHRED operates through a three-stage pipeline: (i) a SHRED model is pre-trained entirely on simulation data to map sensor time-histories into a structured latent space, (ii) a temporal sequence model, trained on simulation-derived latent trajectories, learns to propagate latent states forward or backward in time to span unobserved temporal regions from short observational time windows, and (iii) at deployment, only a short observation window of hyper-sparse sensor measurements from the true system is provided, from which the frozen SHRED model and the temporal model jointly reconstruct or forecast the complete spatiotemporal trajectory. The framework supports bidirectional inference, inherits data assimilation and multiscale reconstruction capabilities from its modular structure, and accommodates extreme observational constraints including single-frame terminal inputs. We evaluate LAPIS-SHRED on six experiments spanning complex spatio-temporal physics: turbulent flows, multiscale propulsion physics, volatile combustion transients, and satellite-derived environmental fields, highlighting a lightweight, modular architecture suited for operational settings where observation is constrained by physical or logistical limitations.

</details>


<div id='cs.DB'></div>

# cs.DB [[Back]](#toc)

### [124] [Fiber-Navigable Search: A Geometric Approach to Filtered ANN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00102)
*Thuong Dang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 提出几何框架用于过滤近似最近邻搜索，通过元数据谓词过滤邻近图产生子图（纤维），其连通性和几何结构与完整图差异显著。使用局部信号设计两阶段搜索算法，结合完整图探索和过滤邻居下降，并识别三种搜索失败机制：拓扑切割、几何折叠和真实盆地。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有近似最近邻搜索方法在处理过滤查询时面临挑战，因为过滤产生的子图（纤维）的连通性和几何结构与原始图差异显著，导致搜索性能下降。需要开发能够适应这种几何变化的搜索框架。

Method: 提出两阶段搜索算法：1）完整图探索阶段；2）当局部几何有利时进行过滤邻居下降。引入局部信号来识别搜索失败的三种机制（拓扑切割、几何折叠、真实盆地）。设计轻量级锚点结构来识别包含纤维的区域并相应重启搜索。

Result: 实验表明该方法在过滤搜索上优于FAISS HNSW。三种失败机制能够清晰分离，并随着过滤选择性的变化可预测地转移。锚点结构有效支持搜索重启策略。

Conclusion: 提出的几何框架为过滤近似最近邻搜索提供了理论基础和实用算法，通过理解纤维的几何特性并设计相应的搜索策略，显著提升了过滤搜索的性能和可靠性。

Abstract: We present a geometric framework for filtered approximate nearest neighbor (ANN) search. Filtering a proximity graph by a metadata predicate produces a subgraph, a fiber, whose connectivity and geometry can differ sharply from the full graph. Using local signals, we propose a two-phase search algorithm that combines full-graph exploration with filtered-neighbor descent when the local geometry is favorable. These signals also classify search failures into three regimes: topological cuts, geometric folds, and genuine basins. A key observation is that all three share a common resolution: restarting the search in a fiber-present cluster near the query. To support this, we introduce a lightweight anchor structure that identifies such regions and restarts the search accordingly. We show empirically that the method outperforms FAISS HNSW on filtered search and the three failure regimes separate cleanly and shift predictably with filter selectivity.

</details>


### [125] [Reasoning about Transactional Isolation Levels with Isolde](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00159)
*Manuel Barros,Alcino Cunha,Jose Pereira,Eunsuk Kang*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Isolde工具能自动生成隔离级别之间差异的示例，帮助验证隔离级别定义的等价性和语义属性


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 数据库弱隔离级别虽然能提升性能，但不同隔离级别规范之间的语义差异难以推理和验证

Method: 开发Isolde工具，自动生成被一个隔离级别允许但被另一个隔离级别禁止的并发执行示例

Result: Isolde成功复现了文献中难以捉摸的结果，并发现了一个先进隔离检查器中标准隔离级别替代规范的未知bug

Conclusion: Isolde为隔离级别的形式化推理提供了实用工具，能有效验证隔离级别规范的等价性和语义属性

Abstract: Most databases can be configured to operate under isolation levels weaker than serializability. These enforce fewer restrictions on the concurrent access to data and consequently allow for more performant implementations. While formal frameworks for rigorously specifying isolation levels exist, reasoning about the semantic differences between specifications remains notoriously difficult.
  This paper proposes a tool -- Isolde -- that can automatically generate examples that are allowed by an isolation level but disallowed by another. This simple primitive unlocks a range of useful reasoning tasks, including checking equivalence between definitions, and verifying (by refutation) subtle semantic properties of isolation levels. For example, Isolde allowed us to easily and automatically reproduce a famously elusive result from the literature and to discover a previously unknown bug in the alternative specification of a standard isolation level used in a state-of-the-art isolation checker.

</details>


### [126] [The Data Hydration Gap: A Formal Model of Underinvestment in General-Purpose Data Products Under Decentralized Governance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00218)
*Gaston Besanson*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 数据网格范式下，分散的数据产品所有权导致领域团队投资不足于跨领域通用性，形成"数据网格陷阱"，造成组织福利损失和技术债务积累。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究在数据网格范式下，当组织将数据产品所有权分散到各领域团队时，为什么跨领域通用性和可重用性会投资不足，以及这种分散治理模式可能带来的组织效率损失。

Method: 建立N个领域同时选择质量(q)和通用性(g)的博弈模型，其中通用性产生正外部性但具有私人成本。分析纳什均衡、角点解、技术债务增长模式，并进行参数校准。

Result: 纳什均衡下的通用性缺口随领域数量和跨领域分析价值增加而扩大；在合理参数配置下，会出现没有可重用"银层"的角点解（数据网格陷阱）；技术债务随N呈二次方增长；校准显示组织福利损失显著。

Conclusion: 数据网格范式可能导致系统性投资不足于跨领域通用性。研究推导了集中式、联邦式和混合治理模式占优的条件，并识别了信息不对称和交易成本等实施障碍，为去中心化数据治理的实证研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: When organizations decentralize data product ownership, as in the data mesh paradigm, each domain team optimizes for its immediate analytical needs, underinvesting in the cross-domain generality that enables organization-wide reuse. We formalize this as a simultaneous-move game in which N domains choose quality (q) and generality (g). Generality creates positive externalities but is privately costly. The Nash equilibrium generality gap is increasing in the number of domains and the value of cross-domain analytics. Under plausible parameter configurations, a corner solution obtains in which no reusable silver layer emerges organically, a condition we term the data mesh trap. Technical debt from narrow products grows quadratically in N. An illustrative calibration suggests non-trivial organizational welfare losses under plausible enterprise parameters. We derive within-model conditions under which centralized, federated, and hybrid governance regimes dominate, and we identify the information asymmetries and transaction costs that complicate implementation. The model provides a formal foundation for empirical research on decentralized data governance.

</details>


### [127] [Inference-Aware & Privacy-Preserving Deletion in Databases](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00326)
*Vishal Chakraborty,Youri Kaminsky,Arnav Abhijit Dhariya,Sharad Mehrotra,Felix Naumann,Sarvesh Pandey*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: 论文从推理角度重新审视数据库删除操作，提出删除不仅需要逻辑移除或物理擦除，更需要隐私保证来限制删除后仍可推断的信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据库系统虽然提供删除功能，但未能保证用户期望的隐私保护。删除的值可能从查询结果和物理存储中消失，但仍可通过依赖关系、派生数据或删除事件本身留下的痕迹被推断出来。因此，有意义的删除需要超越逻辑移除或物理擦除的隐私保证。

Method: 采用推理中心视角分析删除操作，重点关注两个泄漏渠道：删除后状态的泄漏和删除模式本身的泄漏。使用这一视角区分逻辑删除、物理删除和语义删除，组织删除操作的设计空间。

Result: 提出了一个基于推理的删除分析框架，明确了三种删除类型：逻辑删除（仅从逻辑视图中移除）、物理删除（从物理存储中擦除）、语义删除（提供隐私保证限制可推断信息）。该框架为设计具有有意义的隐私保证的删除机制提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 有意义的删除需要隐私保证来限制删除后仍可推断的信息。论文通过推理中心视角为数据库删除操作提供了新的分析框架，并指出了在数据库系统中构建具有有意义的隐私保证的删除机制所面临的开放研究挑战。

Abstract: Deletion is a fundamental database operation, yet modern systems often fail to provide the privacy guarantee that users expect from it. A deleted value may disappear from query results and even from physical storage, yet remain inferable from dependencies, derived data, or traces exposed by the deletion event itself. Meaningful deletion, therefore, requires more than logical removal or physical erasure; it requires a privacy guarantee that limits what remains inferable after deletion. In this paper, we take an inference-centric view of deletion, focusing on two leakage channels: leakage from the post-deletion state and leakage from the deletion pattern itself. We use this lens to distinguish logical, physical, and semantic deletion, organize the design space of deletion operations, and highlight open research challenges for building deletion mechanisms with meaningful privacy guarantees in database systems.

</details>


### [128] [Making Array-Based Translation Practical for Modern, High-Performance Buffer Management](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00423)
*Xinjing Zhou,Jinming Hu,Andrew Pavlo,Michael Stonebraker*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: Calico是一个实用的DBMS控制缓冲区池，采用基于数组的翻译机制，支持现代多样化工作负载（OLTP、分析扫描、向量搜索），通过解耦逻辑翻译与OS页表，结合大页支持和细粒度页面管理，实现高性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据库系统需要支持更广泛的工作负载混合，包括OLTP、扫描密集型分析以及具有不规则高扇出图遍历访问模式的向量搜索索引。现有设计只能满足这些目标的子集，需要一种同时满足快速翻译、用户空间部署、大页兼容、易于集成且仍受DBMS控制的解决方案。

Method: Calico围绕基于数组的翻译机制构建，解耦逻辑翻译与OS页表，使DBMS能够将低开销翻译与大页支持的帧和细粒度页面管理相结合。针对具有大型稀疏分层页面标识符的DBMS，引入三种技术：带路径缓存的多级翻译、用于回收冷翻译内存的空洞打孔，以及利用并行性的组预取。

Result: 评估显示，在扫描、OLTP风格B树访问和向量搜索方面，Calico匹配或优于现有最先进的内存和超内存性能。作为PostgreSQL缓冲区管理器的替代实现，与pgvector集成后，在向量搜索和扫描密集型工作负载中，为PostgreSQL向量搜索提供高达3.9倍内存内和6.5倍超内存加速，扫描密集型查询加速高达3倍。

Conclusion: Calico证明了基于数组的翻译机制在现代硬件下是可行的，能够为多样化数据库工作负载提供高性能、DBMS控制的缓冲区池解决方案，解决了现有设计只能满足部分目标的问题。

Abstract: Modern buffer pools must now support a broader workload mix than classic OLTP alone. In addition to B-tree lookups, database systems increasingly serve scan-heavy analytics and vector-search indexes with irregular high-fan-out graph traversal access patterns. These workloads require a translation mechanism -- mapping logical page IDs to resident frames -- that is simultaneously fast across these diverse access patterns, deployable in user space,compatible with huge pages, easy to integrate, and still under DBMS control for eviction and I/O. Existing designs satisfy only subsets of these goals.
  This paper presents \textbf{\calico}, a practical DBMS-controlled buffer pool built around array-based translation, a decades-old-idea that was dissmissed but now viable with modern hardware.
  \calico decouples logical translation from OS page tables so that the DBMS can combine low-overhead translation with huge-page-backed frames and fine-grained page management. To make array translation practical and performant for DBMSes with large sparse hierarchical page identifiers, \calico introduces three techniques: multi-level translation with path caching, hole punching for reclaiming cold translation memory, and group prefetch to exploit parallelism.
  Our evaluation across scans, OLTP-style B-tree accesses, and vector search shows that \calico matches or outperforms the existing state-of-the-art in-memory and out-of-memory performance. We also implement \calico as a drop-in replacement for PostgreSQL's buffer manager and integrate it with \texttt{pgvector}. Across vector search, and scan-heavy workloads, \calico delivers up to 3.9$\times$ in-memory and 6.5$\times$ larger-than-memory speedup for PostgreSQL vector search, speeds up scan-heavy queries by up to 3$\times$.

</details>


### [129] [Accurate and Scalable Matrix Mechanisms via Divide and Conquer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.00868)
*Guanlin He,Yingtai Xiao,Jiamu Bai,Xin Gu,Zeyu Ding,Wenpeng Yin,Daniel Kifer*

Main category: cs.DB

TL;DR: QuerySmasher是一种基于分治策略的可扩展矩阵机制，通过将查询分解为子查询并重组为相互正交的子工作负载，利用现有低维矩阵机制独立优化处理，最终组合回答原始工作负载查询。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有矩阵机制（如ResidualPlanner、Weighted Fourier Factorizations）虽然能扩展到高维数据集并提供边际和循环乘积查询的最优性保证，但需要改进以更好地处理各种工作负载。QuerySmasher旨在提供一种更通用、可扩展且能超越现有方法性能的替代方案。

Method: QuerySmasher采用分治策略：1）将可基于不同数据边际回答的工作负载中的每个查询分解为子查询；2）将这些片段重组为相互正交的子工作负载；3）这些子工作负载代表小型低维问题，可由现有低维矩阵机制独立且最优地回答；4）最后将这些解决方案拼接起来回答原始工作负载中的查询。

Result: 理论证明QuerySmasher包含并优于ResidualPlanner、ResidualPlanner+和Weighted Fourier Factorizations等先前工作，在平方和误差度量下对所有工作负载都具有优势。实验验证了QuerySmasher的可扩展性和准确性。

Conclusion: QuerySmasher提供了一种通用、可扩展的矩阵机制方法，通过分治策略将高维问题分解为低维子问题，不仅包含先前方法，还能在误差性能上超越它们，为差分隐私查询回答提供了更优的解决方案。

Abstract: Matrix mechanisms are often used to provide unbiased differentially private query answers when publishing statistics or creating synthetic data. Recent work has developed matrix mechanisms, such as ResidualPlanner and Weighted Fourier Factorizations, that scale to high dimensional datasets while providing optimality guarantees for workloads such as marginals and circular product queries. They operate by adding noise to a linearly independent set of queries that can compactly represent the desired workloads.
  In this paper, we present QuerySmasher, an alternative scalable approach based on a divide-and-conquer strategy. Given a workload that can be answered from various data marginals, QuerySmasher splits each query into sub-queries and re-assembles the pieces into mutually orthogonal sub-workloads. These sub-workloads represent small, low-dimensional problems that can be independently and optimally answered by existing low-dimensional matrix mechanisms. QuerySmasher then stitches these solutions together to answer queries in the original workload.
  We show that QuerySmasher subsumes prior work, like ResidualPlanner (RP), ResidualPlanner+ (RP+), and Weighted Fourier Factorizations (WFF). We prove that it can dominate those approaches, under sum squared error, for all workloads. We also experimentally demonstrate the scalability and accuracy of QuerySmasher.

</details>
